Contexto: con el SERVIDOR mudo (conexión abre, nadie contesta) y la franja roja ya puesta, cada venta en efectivo esperaba

  POS en cada uno con la franja puesta.
- Si isFullyConnected() puede quedarse en false con el servidor sano (latido que no corre, valor inicial, otra pantalla), lo
  que dejaría TODO el efectivo en cola para siempre; o si la cola no sube al volver.
- CheckoutViewModel.prepararCobro con hayRed=isFullyConnected: cupón, descuento del cliente, cortesía, referido, $0,
  carrito con llave previa: ¿algo que antes funcionaba con internet y servidor intermitente ahora se rechaza mal?
- MAX_INTENTOS_SIN_RESPUESTA=2 y nadieContesto(): ¿clasifica como «nadie contestó» algo que sí recibió respuesta
  (InterruptedIOException por cancelación, «Canceled»)? ¿afecta el replay del worker?
- Pruebas: ¿las nuevas prueban lo que dicen? ¿alguna prueba existente queda rota (p. ej. arneses con ConnectionStateManager
  mockeado relaxed, que ahora da isFullyConnected()=false)? Busca en app/src/test.

ROMPE-LA-PROMESA | LA-PROMESA-LO-NECESITA | AJENO. «Falta en iOS» no es hallazgo.

# Efectivo con el SERVIDOR mudo: no esperar cuando ya se sabe (encargo 97042c · hz:nube0000:naa40500)

## Promesa (5 líneas)

- **Qué va a pasar:** en la TPV (PAX y Nexgo), con la franja roja «Sin conexión al servidor» o sin internet, cobrar en
  efectivo (Pago rápido, Mesas y Cobrar) guarda la venta en la terminal y termina en ~1–2 s sin llamar al servidor; la
  venta sube sola y una sola vez cuando vuelve. Antes de que salga la franja, si el servidor no contesta, se intenta 2
  veces (no 5) y la venta pasa a la cola.
- **No entra:** la tarjeta (sigue igual), los ~25 s de consultar el turno y el «Preparando…» de Cobrar ANTES de que salga
  la franja, el arranque en frío (`hz:nube0000:nab7fc93`), el texto del recibo de Pago rápido (`hz:nube0000:nac9e359`),
  ANDROID (ya lo hace) e iOS.
- **Se prueba así:** TPV `.lab` en la PAX contra el banco; `kill -STOP` al SERVIDOR; esperar la franja roja; Pago rápido
  $13 en efectivo y Cobrar con un producto en efectivo, midiendo en logcat del toque a «queued»; `kill -CONT`; en la base
  1 pago por venta. Además, con el SERVIDOR sano, una venta normal sigue saliendo con su recibo.
- **Depende de:** la fila de efectivo guardada ANTES del POST (`RecordPaymentUseCase`, `enqueueAndClaim`) y la venta local
  guardada antes de subir (`VentaLocalRepository.guardar`); el worker que sube al volver (`ConnectionEventManager` →
  `PaymentSyncScheduler.runNow`); la franja (`ConnectionStateManager.hasServer`, 2 latidos fallidos). Todo ya existe.
- **Responsable:** la conversación de la nube a3c75d (encargo 97042c).

## Mapa de entradas (quién escribe la fila de efectivo y qué cambia)

| Camino | Escritor de la fila | Hoy con el SERVIDOR mudo | Con el cambio |
|---|---|---|---|
| Pago rápido / Mesas, efectivo en vivo (PAX `PaymentViewModel` ×2 variantes, Nexgo `AngelPayPaymentViewModel`) | `RecordPaymentUseCase` → `enqueueAndClaim` antes del POST; luego `markSynced` o `release` | 5 intentos × ~11 s ≈ 58 s, luego `release` + `handleRecordFailure` (cola) | con la franja: 0 POST, `release` inmediato con un `ConnectException` (Retryable) → la misma cola; sin franja: ≤2 intentos si nadie contesta |
| Replay del worker (`PaymentSyncWorker`, `alreadyQueued = true`) | el worker con su claim | igual | **sin cambio** (sigue intentando: es quien sube al volver) |
| Tarjeta (Blumon/AngelPay), registro tras la aprobación | libreta + cola de tarjeta | igual | **sin cambio** |
| Cobrar → venta local (3 VM): `guardar` (fila reclamada) → `SubirVentaGuardadaUseCase` → `markSynced` / `markPermanentlyFailed` / `release(+1)` | `VentaLocalRepository` | 1 intento ≈ 25 s | con la franja: no se sube; `release` sin sumar intento + `runNow` |
| Cobrar → decidir el camino (`CheckoutViewModel.prepararCobro`) | guarda el carrito con su llave antes del POST | intenta crear la orden (≈25 s) y cae a venta local con la MISMA llave | con la franja: va directo a venta local (como sin internet; cupón, descuento del cliente y cortesía sin permiso se rechazan igual que sin red) |

Reintentos, cancelaciones y recuperaciones: la fila ya existe con su claim antes de decidir; `release` la deja PENDING
con la misma llave e identidad; si el proceso muere entre la decisión y el `release`, el claim vence y el worker la
retoma (igual que hoy). Nada sale por el cable en el atajo, así que nada se puede duplicar.

# CLAUDE.md - Avoqado TPV (Android POS)

## 🔴 Los builds pesados van por `avq-verify` (hacen fila)

Esta Mac corre con ~20 sesiones de IA encima y el swap al límite; **dos daemons de Kotlin a `-Xmx6g`
la tumban**. Cualquier `./gradlew` que compile (y `xcodebuild`) se lanza así, para que sólo haya uno
a la vez:

```bash
cd /Users/amieva/Documents/Programming/Avoqado
./scripts/avq-verify.sh avoqado-tpv ./gradlew assembleDebug
```

Esto **no** cambia la regla de "un compile de un solo proyecto se corre siempre, aunque la máquina
esté saturada": eso decide **si** compilas (siempre sí), esto decide **cómo** — esperando turno en
vez de encimarte. Van a pelo: lint, formato, un test suelto, `--version`.

En ESTE repo el trabajo NO se manda al Alienware (no tiene Java ni Xcode): el script sólo hace la
fila local, que es justo lo que hace falta aquí. Detalle: `Avoqado/CLAUDE.md` → "Verificación repartida".


> **Reglas de entorno** — sesiones de IA en paralelo, y cuándo verificar según la carga de la
> máquina — están en el `CLAUDE.md` del workspace (`../CLAUDE.md`), que auto-carga junto con este
> archivo. Léelas antes de correr builds/tests o de tocar git.

## 🔴 CRITICAL — Ask which payment tier (and how it gets turned on) BEFORE building or changing anything

Avoqado is a tier-gated SaaS (**FREE · PRO · PREMIUM · ENTERPRISE**). Whenever you add a new
feature, modify existing behavior, or expose a new capability, **STOP and ask the founder which
paid tier it falls under** — then wire the gating to match. A change shipped without a tier
decision is unfinished: it either leaks paid value into a lower tier or hides a free capability
behind a paywall.

- **Backend (authoritative):** `avoqado-server/src/services/access/basePlan.service.ts` +
  `avoqado-server/src/middlewares/checkFeatureAccess.middleware.ts`. Obligatory gating questions:
  `avoqado-server/.claude/rules/feature-gating.md`. PREMIUM-only codes today: `CFDI`, `INVENTORY_TRACKING`.
- **Dashboard display/CTA map:** `avoqado-web-dashboard/src/config/plan-catalog.ts`
  (`TierId`, `PLAN_TIERS`, `getTierForFeature()` → FeatureGate upsell).
- **Enforcement status:** ✅ **avoqado-web-dashboard** enforces tiers. **avoqado-android** and **avoqado-ios** have partial plan
  gates (`PlanManager` / `PlanGate`); don't touch iOS for a change here (iOS va después: `../AGENTS.md`). Treat tier codes like
  permissions: mirrored across backend + every client by exact name — a mismatch fails silently.
- **Activación (regla completa en `../CLAUDE.md`):** esta app **lee** el estado del switch; solo se construye aquí si se
  toca durante el turno desde el piso. Apagado se ve y se explica, nunca desaparece en silencio.

## How This Configuration Works

| Layer | Path | Loaded | Purpose |
|-------|------|--------|---------|
| This file | `CLAUDE.md` | Always | Router + essentials |
| Rules | `.claude/rules/*.md` | Auto, every session | Mandatory coding rules |
| Docs | `docs/` | On demand | Detailed guides and references |

**Rules auto-load** — you don't need to read them manually.

> ⚠️ **The `gymDemo` ("Demo Prod") build variant is INTENTIONAL — do NOT "fix" it.** Its
> `BLUMON_ENV=SAND` + `API_BASE_URL_DEV`→prod override, the `com.jaac.avoqado_tpv.demo` client in
> `google-services.json` (cloned from sandbox), and the `BuildConfig.DEBUG`-gated serial override in
> `DeviceInfoManager` are all deliberate (money-safe prod demo). Normal variants
> (`production`/`sandbox`/`nexgo*`) are unaffected and `productionRelease` builds as before. Full
> explanation: `.claude/rules/avoqado-demo-variant.md`.

When rules conflict: `.claude/rules/` wins > this file > `docs/`

**Maintaining this file:** Short rules (1-3 lines) go directly here. Detailed content (code examples, tables, >10 lines) goes in `docs/` or `.claude/rules/`. Keep this file under ~200 lines — it loads every session.

## Identity & Tech Stack

**Avoqado TPV** — Android POS app for PAX payment terminals. Multi-tenant restaurant/retail management.

Kotlin + Jetpack Compose | Clean Architecture (Presentation -> Domain -> Data) | Hilt DI | Room DB | Blumon PAX SDK | Socket.IO | EncryptedSharedPreferences | 1GB RAM target (PAX A80)

## Firebase Crashlytics MCP (Direct Access)

**ALWAYS check Crashlytics when:** payment errors are reported, app crashes, connectivity issues, Blumon SDK failures, or any production incident.

Firebase MCP tools are available via `mcp__plugin_firebase_firebase__crashlytics_*`. No screenshots needed.

```
# App IDs
Production: 1:219752736783:android:d09cd5eb6162e7ee52db7a
Sandbox:    1:219752736783:android:aa8d57cc3022eb9c52db7a
Project:    avoqado-d0a24

# Quick queries
crashlytics_get_report(appId, report="topIssues")           # Top crashes
crashlytics_list_events(appId, filter={issueErrorTypes:["FATAL"]})  # Recent crashes
crashlytics_get_issue(appId, issueId="<hex>")               # Issue detail
crashlytics_list_events(appId, filter={issueId:"<hex>"})    # Events for issue
```

**Proactive rule**: When investigating ANY payment bug, query Crashlytics FIRST before asking the user for screenshots. Check both FATAL and NON_FATAL events filtered by the relevant time window.

## Blumon TPV Portal Verification

When Blumon TPV SDK behavior is suspect, check Crashlytics first, then verify the processor result directly in the Blumon TPV portal with Playwright/browser automation when credentials are available.

- Production: `https://element.blumonpay.net/transacciones`
- Sandbox: `https://sandbox-atom.blumonpay.net/transacciones`
- Use credentials only from secure/session context, such as `BLUMON_PORTAL_USER` and `BLUMON_PORTAL_PASSWORD`. Never commit portal credentials, JWTs, PANs, or screenshots/logs containing sensitive card data.
- If a decline appears in the Blumon TPV portal with matching amount/card/reference/time, classify it as processor/issuer/Blumon TPV-side unless TPV logs contradict that.
- If the SDK reports a decline but no matching transaction appears in the Blumon TPV portal, treat it as high probability TPV/app integration bug and inspect SDK initialization, merchant `posId`, serial, entry mode, EMV tags, idempotency, and backend recording.

## Commands

```bash
export JAVA_HOME=$(/usr/libexec/java_home -v 23)  # Required

./gradlew installSandboxDebug       # Dev build (90% of the time)
./gradlew assembleProductionRelease  # Production APK
./gradlew compileDebugKotlin         # Quick compile check
./gradlew lint --continue            # Lint (must pass before commit)

# Unit tests (220 tests — run before commits and after refactors)
./gradlew testSandboxDebugUnitTest                    # All tests
./gradlew testSandboxDebugUnitTest --tests "com.jaac.avoqado_tpv.ClassName"  # Single class

# ADB monitoring (mandatory after every change)
adb logcat -c && adb logcat -s PaymentViewModel,MenuViewModel | grep -iE "keyword"

# Log capture for testing
./scripts/capture-logs.sh <feature> start|stop|status|read
```

## Project Structure

```
app/src/main/java/com/jaac/avoqado_tpv/
├── presentation/   # ViewModels + Compose screens
├── domain/         # Use cases + business logic
├── data/           # Repositories, Room, API clients
├── di/             # Hilt modules
└── util/           # Extensions, helpers

app/src/sandbox/    # Sandbox-specific (PaymentViewModel, BlumonInitializer)
app/src/production/ # Production-specific (same files, different SDK URLs)
```

## Critical Rules (brief — details in `.claude/rules/`)

0. 🔴 **LEGACY:** «Órdenes» (Ordenar) es LEGACY y su tarjeta y pantallas ya se quitaron de la app (D34, PR #104, 20-nov; founder 25-sep, 20-oct y 20-nov: «para ordering se usa Mesas y Cobrar»). Lo que queda en `features/ordering` es compartido y SIGUE VIVO: modelos, repos y APIs (`Product`, `Customer`, `Discount`, `Order`, `OrderRepository`…) y `presentation/menu/ReglasDeOrdenes.kt`, que usan Cobrar (`features/checkout`) y el cobro. Lo único legacy que queda es la libreta de Órdenes (`OrderSyncCoordinator`/`DraftOrder`): sólo sube cuentas viejas guardadas sin red; no se le agregan funciones, no se prueba ni se le hacen proyectos. Mesas es `features/tables`.
1. **Changelog**: EVERY modification must be logged in `CHANGELOG.md` under `[Unreleased]`. No exceptions. Rotate at ~50KB. -> `changelog-policy.md`
2. **PaymentViewModel safety**: 8 features share payment code. Test ALL flows, sync both variants. -> `critical-warnings.md`
3. **Blumon distinction**: Always "Blumon TPV" or "Blumon E-commerce", never just "Blumon". -> `critical-warnings.md`
4. **Build variants**: Sync sandbox/ and production/ files. Production uses REAL money. -> `critical-warnings.md`
5. **Room migrations**: ALWAYS create migration when adding @Entity fields. Missing = 100% crash. -> `critical-warnings.md`
6. **Auth**: Use `authRepository.getAuthContext()`. No `req.user` — that's backend. -> `critical-warnings.md`
7. **Tenant isolation**: Every DB query filters by `venueId`. No exceptions. -> `critical-warnings.md`
8. **Money**: `BigDecimal`, never Float. -> `critical-warnings.md`
9. **Performance**: Paginate everything. 1GB RAM target. -> `critical-warnings.md`
10. **API paths**: Base URL already has `/api/v1/`. Don't add `/v1/` again. -> `critical-warnings.md`
11. **Permissions**: New features need backend + TPV + dashboard permissions. Exact name match. -> `release-and-git.md`
12. **Release build**: apksigner v2 (not jarsigner), save to iCloud, send to Blumon. -> `release-and-git.md`
13. **Version bumps**: "Can user do something new?" Yes=MINOR, No=PATCH. -> `release-and-git.md`
14. **Cross-repo**: Backend deploys in minutes, TPV takes 3-5 days. Backend ALWAYS supports old versions. -> `release-and-git.md`
15. **Git**: Never commit without permission. No `Co-Authored-By`. -> `release-and-git.md`
16. **ADB monitoring**: Mandatory after every change. Log capture for testing. -> `testing-and-adb.md`
17. **Unit tests**: 220 tests, 0 failures. Run before commits and after refactors. -> [unit testing guide](docs/UNIT_TESTING_GUIDE.md)
18. **Timezone**: Never use `ZoneId.systemDefault()`. Use `VenueTimeZone.get(secureStorage)` or `ZoneId.of("America/Mexico_City")`. -> `critical-warnings.md`
19. **PAX A910S target device**: 720x1280px, 320dpi (xhdpi), 360x640dp. Always add `@Preview(widthDp=360, heightDp=640)` with banners when creating/modifying screens. No fixed sizes >80dp for buttons. -> `critical-warnings.md`

## Build Variants

Same branch (`main`), different Gradle configurations:

| Variant | Command | Use | Processor |
|---------|---------|-----|-----------|
| sandboxDebug | `./gradlew installSandboxDebug` | Daily dev — PAX (90%) | Blumon sandbox |
| productionDebug | `./gradlew installProductionDebug` | Debug prod issues — PAX | Blumon REAL money |
| productionRelease | `./gradlew assembleProductionRelease` | Final APK — PAX | Blumon REAL money |
| **nexgoDebug** | **`./gradlew installNexgoDebug`** | **Nexgo N86/N5 dev** | **AngelPay QA** |
| tutorialEmuDebug | `./gradlew installTutorialEmuDebug` | Emulator/screenshots | None |

   145	    /**
   146	     * Graba el pago en el backend usando el recorder apropiado.
   147	     *
   148	     * **Proceso:**
   149	     * 1. Analiza el tipo de PaymentContext (sealed class)
   150	     * 2. Selecciona el recorder correcto:
   151	     *    - FastPayment → FastPaymentRecorder
   152	     *    - OrderPayment → OrderPaymentRecorder
   153	     * 3. Delega la grabación al recorder
   154	     * 4. Retorna Result<PaymentReceipt>
   155	     *
   156	     * **Nota sobre error handling:**
   157	     * Los errores de red (timeout, 500, etc.) son manejados por los recorders.
   158	     * Este UseCase solo se encarga de la selección del recorder correcto.
   159	     *
   160	     * En el futuro, este UseCase puede agregar:
   161	     * - Retry logic (3 intentos con exponential backoff)
   162	     * - Offline queue (guardar en Room DB si no hay red)
   163	     * - Idempotency checks (evitar duplicados usando referenceNumber)
   164	     *
   165	     * @param context Contexto del pago (FastPayment o OrderPayment)
   166	     * @param cardDetails Detalles de la tarjeta extraídos del Blumon SDK
   167	     * @param authorizationNumber Código de autorización de Blumon
   168	     * @param referenceNumber Número de referencia de Blumon (único por transacción)
   169	     * @return Result con PaymentReceipt o Exception
   170	     *
   171	     * **Ejemplo:**
   172	     * ```kotlin
   173	     * val context = PaymentContext.FastPayment(
   174	     *     venueId = "venue_123",
   175	     *     staffId = "staff_456",
   176	     *     amount = BigDecimal("50.00"),
   177	     *     tip = BigDecimal("5.00")
   178	     * )
   179	     *
   180	     * val cardDetails = CardDetails(
   181	     *     maskedPan = "411111******1111",
   182	     *     cardBrand = CardBrand.VISA,
   183	     *     entryMode = CardEntryMode.CHIP,
   184	     *     isInternational = false
   185	     * )
   186	     *
   187	     * val result = recordPaymentUseCase(
   188	     *     context = context,
   189	     *     cardDetails = cardDetails,
   190	     *     authorizationNumber = "502511",
   191	     *     referenceNumber = "000000188231"
   192	     * )
   193	     *
   194	     * result.onSuccess { receipt ->
   195	     *     println("✅ Payment recorded: ${receipt.receiptUrl}")
   196	     * }.onFailure { error ->
   197	     *     println("❌ Error: ${error.message}")
   198	     * }

   201	    suspend operator fun invoke(
   202	        context: PaymentContext,
   203	        cardDetails: CardDetails,
   204	        authorizationNumber: String,
   205	        referenceNumber: String,
   206	        alreadyQueued: Boolean = false,
   207	    ): Result<PaymentReceipt> {
   208	        // Seleccionar recorder basado en contexto (exhaustive when)
   209	        val recorder = when (context) {
   210	            is PaymentContext.FastPayment -> {
   211	                Timber.d("📍 Using FastPaymentRecorder for fast payment")
   212	                fastPaymentRecorder
   213	            }
   214	
   215	            is PaymentContext.OrderPayment -> {
   216	                Timber.d("📍 Using OrderPaymentRecorder for order payment (orderId=${context.orderId})")
   217	                orderPaymentRecorder
   218	            }
   219	
   220	            is PaymentContext.AngelPayPayment -> {
   221	                // 🔶 ANGELPAY: Route based on whether it's a fast or order payment
   222	                if (context.orderId != null) {
   223	                    Timber.d("📍 Using OrderPaymentRecorder for AngelPay order payment (orderId=${context.orderId})")
   224	                    orderPaymentRecorder
   225	                } else {
   226	                    Timber.d("📍 Using FastPaymentRecorder for AngelPay fast payment")
   227	                    fastPaymentRecorder
   228	                }
   229	            }
   230	
   231	            is PaymentContext.RefundPayment -> {
   232	                // 🔐 REFUNDS: Use dedicated RecordRefundUseCase instead
   233	                // Refunds follow a different flow:
   234	                // 1. SDK processes TransType.REFUND
   235	                // 2. RecordRefundUseCase records to backend
   236	                // This UseCase is for SALES only
   237	                Timber.e("❌ RefundPayment should use RecordRefundUseCase, not RecordPaymentUseCase")
   238	                return Result.failure(
   239	                    IllegalStateException(
   240	                        "Refunds must be recorded via RecordRefundUseCase. " +
   241	                        "Use PaymentViewModel's processRefund() method instead."
   242	                    )
   243	                )
   244	            }
   245	        }
   246	
   247	        // El efectivo confirmado debe sobrevivir a la muerte del proceso durante el
   248	        // PRIMER POST. El INSERT incluye su claim: el worker no compite con esta llamada.
   249	        // El replay ya posee un claim y no vuelve a insertar ni a reclamar la misma fila.
   250	        var recordingContext = if (cardDetails.isCash) context else shiftRepository.restaurarContextoDelIntento(context)
   251	        val cashClaim = if (cardDetails.isCash && !alreadyQueued) {
   252	            val owned = try { shiftRepository.pagoTieneDueno(context.venueId, context.idempotencyKey, referenceNumber) }
   253	                catch (error: Exception) { return Result.failure(CashOwnerReviewRequiredException("No se pudo consultar el registro local de esta venta.", error)) }
   254	            if (owned) return Result.failure(revisionDeDueno(context, referenceNumber))
   255	            try {
   256	                shiftRepository.guardarNuevaObligacionDeCaja(context.venueId, terminalRequestId = context.terminalPaymentRequestId) { belonging ->
   257	                    recordingContext = context.withShiftBelonging(belonging)
   258	                    val payment = QueuedPayment.desdeContexto(
   259	                        context = recordingContext,
   260	                        orderNumber = (context as? PaymentContext.AngelPayPayment)?.orderNumber,
   261	                        cardDetails = cardDetails,
   262	                        authorizationNumber = authorizationNumber,
   263	                        referenceNumber = referenceNumber,
   264	                        error = null,
   265	                    )
   266	                    paymentQueueRepository.enqueueAndClaim(payment).getOrThrow()
   267	                }
   268	            } catch (cancelled: kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException) { throw cancelled }
   269	              catch (error: Exception) {
   270	                val existing = runCatching { shiftRepository.pagoTieneDueno(context.venueId, context.idempotencyKey, referenceNumber) }.getOrElse { true }
   271	                return Result.failure(if (existing) revisionDeDueno(context, referenceNumber, error)
   272	                    else CashAdmissionRejectedException.marcar(error))
   273	              }
   274	        } else null
   275	        if (cashClaim != null && (cashClaim.queueId <= 0 || cashClaim.claimToken.isNullOrBlank())) {
   276	            return Result.failure(IllegalStateException("El efectivo no tiene un registro local reservado"))
   277	        }
   278	
   279	        // Un throw/cancel conserva SYNCING: tras vencer el claim el worker retoma esta
   280	        // misma identidad. Nunca se borra o reinserta dinero para manejar una cancelación.
   281	        // 📴 Efectivo en vivo con la franja de «sin servidor» (o sin internet): la fila ya está guardada y reclamada; no se
   282	        // pregunta al servidor y se suelta a la cola al instante. Tarjeta y replay del worker (alreadyQueued) no cambian.
   283	        val result = if (cashClaim != null && conexion?.isFullyConnected() == false) {
   284	            Timber.w("📴 Efectivo con el servidor sin contestar: queda en la cola sin intentar | ref=$referenceNumber")
   285	            Result.failure(ServidorSinContestarException())
   286	        } else recordPaymentWithRetry(
   287	            recorder = recorder,
   288	            context = recordingContext.withQueueClaimToken(cashClaim?.claimToken ?: recordingContext.queueClaimToken),
   289	            cardDetails = cardDetails,
   290	            authorizationNumber = authorizationNumber,
   291	            referenceNumber = referenceNumber,
   292	            rendirseSinRespuesta = cashClaim != null,
   293	        )
   294	        if (cashClaim != null) {
   295	            val token = requireNotNull(cashClaim.claimToken)
   296	            if (result.isSuccess) {
   297	                paymentQueueRepository.markSynced(cashClaim.queueId, token)
   298	            } else {
   299	                paymentQueueRepository.release(
   300	                    cashClaim.queueId, token, cashClaim.retryCount,
   301	                    result.exceptionOrNull()?.message ?: "No se pudo confirmar el registro de efectivo",
   302	                )
   303	            }
   304	        }
   305	        result.getOrNull()?.let { rememberSimSale(recordingContext, it) }
   306	        return result
   307	    }
   308	
   309	    /**
   310	     * Una venta con SIM recién registrada (en vivo o al subirla desde la cola) queda en «Pendientes» de la terminal: si se
   311	     * reinicia sin señal antes de la primera foto, la venta sigue ahí. Los tres cobros (PAX y Nexgo) pasan por aquí.
   312	     * Nunca cambia el resultado del cobro.
   313	     */
   314	    private suspend fun rememberSimSale(context: PaymentContext, receipt: PaymentReceipt) {
   315	        val sales = actionableSales ?: return
   316	        val (portabilidad, seriales, orden) = when (context) {
   317	            is PaymentContext.FastPayment -> Triple(context.isPortabilidad, context.serialNumbers, context.orderReference)
   318	            is PaymentContext.OrderPayment -> Triple(context.isPortabilidad, context.serialNumbers, null)
   319	            is PaymentContext.AngelPayPayment -> Triple(context.isPortabilidad, context.serialNumbers, context.orderNumber)
   320	            is PaymentContext.RefundPayment -> return
   321	        }
   322	        if (seriales.isEmpty() || receipt.paymentId.isBlank()) return
   323	        try {
   324	            sales.addLocalSale(receipt.paymentId, context.amount, orden, portabilidad, seriales, context.venueId, context.staffId)
   325	        } catch (e: kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException) {
   326	            throw e
   327	        } catch (e: Exception) {
   328	            Timber.w(e, "📋 No se pudo guardar la venta con SIM en Pendientes | paymentId=${receipt.paymentId}")
   329	        }
   330	    }
   331	
   332	    /**
   333	     * Intenta grabar el pago con retry automático en caso de errores transitorios.
   334	     *
   335	     * **Estrategia de retry:**
   336	     * - Máximo 5 intentos
   337	     * - Exponential backoff: 500ms → 1s → 2s → 4s
   338	     * - La clasificación retry-vs-stop delega en [classifySyncFailure] (ver [isRetryable]),
   339	     *   por CÓDIGO HTTP — nunca por texto del mensaje.
   340	     *
   341	     * **Se reintenta (SyncOutcome.Retryable):**
   342	     * - 401/403/405 — casi siempre es la SESIÓN, no el pago (ver KDoc de [classifySyncFailure])
   343	     * - 409 — NO afirma que el pago se registró; el reintento idempotente real da 200
   344	     * - 408/429 — "reintenta", no "no"
   345	     * - 5xx, IOException (red/timeout), y cualquier error desconocido
   346	     *
   347	     * **NO se reintenta:**
   348	     * - 400/404/422 (SyncOutcome.Permanent) — error de negocio real, reintentar no cambia el resultado
   349	     *
   350	     * **Ejemplo de log:**
   351	     * ```
   352	     * 📡 Recording payment (attempt 1/5)
   353	     * ⚠️ Retriable error (attempt 1/5): Error del servidor (502)
   354	     * 🔄 Retry attempt 2/5 in 500ms...
   355	     * 📡 Recording payment (attempt 2/5)
   356	     * ✅ Payment recorded successfully after 2 attempts
   357	     * ```
   358	     */
   359	    private suspend fun recordPaymentWithRetry(
   360	        recorder: PaymentRecorder,
   361	        context: PaymentContext,
   362	        cardDetails: CardDetails,
   363	        authorizationNumber: String,
   364	        referenceNumber: String,
   365	        /** Efectivo en vivo: si nadie contesta, a los [MAX_INTENTOS_SIN_RESPUESTA] va a la cola (ver ahí). */
   366	        rendirseSinRespuesta: Boolean = false,
   367	    ): Result<PaymentReceipt> {
   368	        var lastError: Exception? = null
   369	
   370	        repeat(MAX_RETRIES) { attemptIndex ->
   371	            val attemptNumber = attemptIndex + 1
   372	
   373	            // Delay exponencial ANTES del intento (excepto el primero)
   374	            if (attemptIndex > 0) {
   375	                val delayMs = INITIAL_RETRY_DELAY_MS * (1 shl (attemptIndex - 1)) // 2^(n-1)
   376	                Timber.w("🔄 Retry attempt $attemptNumber/$MAX_RETRIES in ${delayMs}ms...")
   377	                delay(delayMs)
   378	            }
   379	
   380	            Timber.d("📡 Recording payment (attempt $attemptNumber/$MAX_RETRIES)")
   381	
   382	            // Intentar grabar el pago
   383	            val result = recorder.recordPayment(
   384	                context = context,
   385	                cardDetails = cardDetails,
   386	                authorizationNumber = authorizationNumber,
   387	                referenceNumber = referenceNumber,
   388	            )
   389	
   390	            // ✅ Si fue exitoso, retornar inmediatamente
   391	            if (result.isSuccess) {
   392	                if (attemptIndex > 0) {
   393	                    Timber.i("✅ Payment recorded successfully after $attemptNumber attempts")
   394	                }
   395	                return result
   396	            }
   397	
   398	            // ❌ Analizar el error
   399	            val exception = result.exceptionOrNull()
   400	            lastError = exception as? Exception
   401	
   402	            if (exception is com.jaac.avoqado_tpv.features.shift.data.repository.ShiftMappingPendingException) return result
   403	
   404	            // Determinar si es un error retriable
   405	            val isRetriable = isRetryable(exception)
   406	
   407	            if (!isRetriable) {
   408	                Timber.w("⚠️ Non-retriable error, stopping retries: ${exception?.message}")
   409	                return result
   410	            }
   411	
   412	            if (rendirseSinRespuesta && attemptNumber >= MAX_INTENTOS_SIN_RESPUESTA && nadieContesto(exception)) {
   413	                Timber.w("📴 Efectivo: el servidor no contestó $attemptNumber veces; pasa a la cola sin más intentos: ${exception?.message}")
   414	                return result
   415	            }
   416	
   417	            Timber.w("⚠️ Retriable error (attempt $attemptNumber/$MAX_RETRIES): ${exception?.message}")
   418	        }
   419	
   420	        // Si llegamos aquí, agotamos todos los intentos
   421	        Timber.e("❌ Payment recording failed after $MAX_RETRIES attempts")
   422	        return Result.failure(
   423	            lastError ?: Exception("Error registrando el pago después de $MAX_RETRIES intentos")
   424	        )
   425	    }
   426	
   427	    /**
   428	     * ¿Vale la pena reintentar este error?
   429	     *
   430	     * Antes esto encadenaba ~18 `message.contains(...)`, incluido `Regex("5\\d{2}")` que
   431	     * hacía match contra CUALQUIER "5XX" dentro del texto — un monto de $500.00 o una
   432	     * referencia que contuviera "500" marcaban el error como reintentable por accidente.
   433	     * Un cambio de wording del backend (traducción, mensaje reescrito) podía además
   434	     * romper la clasificación en silencio: un error transitorio se volvía permanente o
   435	     * al revés. Ver spec §4.2 F-5.
   436	     *
   437	     * Ahora delega en el clasificador único por código HTTP ([classifySyncFailure], Task 1).
   438	     *
   439	     * **Cambios de comportamiento deliberados vs. la versión anterior** (no es un bug,
   440	     * es intencional — ver KDoc de [classifySyncFailure] / [SyncOutcome]):
   441	     * - 401/403 antes paraban el retry aquí. Ahora reintentan — casi siempre son la
   442	     *   SESIÓN, no el pago (un refresh de token atorado tras Doze puede devolver el 401
   443	     *   original con el token aún válido; marcarlo permanente mataba pagos ya cobrados
   444	     *   para siempre).
   445	     * - 429 antes paraba el retry aquí. Ahora reintenta — un 429 significa "espera y

  1234	    private suspend fun prepararCobro(): Result<PaymentNavigationPayload> {
  1235	        val current = _cartState.value
  1236	        errorParaCobrar(current)?.let { return Result.failure(IllegalStateException(it)) }
  1237	        if (current.isEmpty) {
  1238	            return Result.failure(IllegalStateException("Cart is empty"))
  1239	        }
  1240	        if (current.taxCents != 0) {
  1241	            return Result.failure(IllegalStateException("El impuesto no está disponible en Cobrar V1"))
  1242	        }
  1243	        // Un «vaciar» de hace un instante termina de borrar el carrito a medias antes de que esta venta escriba el suyo.
  1244	        borradoDelCarrito?.join()
  1245	
  1246	        val amountPesos = (current.totalCents / 100.0).toString()
  1247	        // 📴 «Red» para cobrar = internet Y servidor: con la franja de «sin servidor» crear la orden esperaba ~25 s y luego caía a la
  1248	        // venta local con la misma llave. Ahora va directo, como sin internet (encargo 97042c). El carrito guardado y su llave no cambian.
  1249	        val hayRed = connectionStateManager.isFullyConnected()
  1250	        // 🔴 D5: el cupón necesita al SERVIDOR: sin red no se guarda como venta local (se subiría como un descuento libre), se dice. Y un
  1251	        // cobro rápido no tiene orden donde ligarlo: con cupón siempre hay orden, aunque sean puros importes. Igual con el descuento del
  1252	        // cliente (D1): se registra como lo que es, en su orden.
  1253	        if (current.couponCode != null && !hayRed) return Result.failure(IllegalStateException(TEXTO_CUPON_SIN_RED))
  1254	        val llevaOrden = current.hasProductItems || current.couponCode != null || current.descuentoEsDelCliente
  1255	        return when (caminoDelCobro(llevaOrden, current.totalCents, hayRed)) {
  1256	            CaminoDelCobro.RAPIDO -> {
  1257	                // Sin orden y sin carrito durable (como hoy). Un carrito a medias de un intento anterior ya no es esta venta.
  1258	                secureStorage.getVenueId()?.let { ventaSinRed.borrarCarrito(it) }
  1259	                // No order is created here, but the referral can still be captured with no `intendedOrderId`. The qualifying-order
  1260	                // webhook on the backend will pick it up via the customerId attached to the fast-payment Order shadow row.
  1261	                capturarReferido(orderId = null)
  1262	                Result.success(PaymentNavigationPayload.Fast(amountPesos))
  1263	            }
  1264	            CaminoDelCobro.EN_LINEA -> cobrarEnLinea(current, amountPesos)
  1265	            CaminoDelCobro.LOCAL -> {
  1266	                // Una venta local se reproduce por /mobile SIN token: una cortesía sin permiso la rechazaría el servidor. El PIN necesita red.
  1267	                if (llevaCortesia(current) && !permissionsRepository.hasPermission("tpv-orders:comp")) {
  1268	                    return Result.failure(IllegalStateException(
  1269	                        (if (hayRed) "" else "Sin conexión: ") + "el PIN del gerente necesita internet. Quita la cortesía o cobra cuando vuelva la red",
  1270	                    ))
  1271	                }
  1272	                // Sólo importes en $0 con red: hasta hoy salía como Fast y capturaba el referido sin orden; eso se conserva.
  1273	                if (hayRed && !current.hasProductItems) capturarReferido(orderId = null)
  1274	                ventaLocal(current, amountPesos, llaveYaUsada = null)
  1275	            }
  1276	        }
  1277	    }

  1279	    private suspend fun cobrarEnLinea(current: CartState, amountPesos: String): Result<PaymentNavigationPayload> {
  1280	        val venueId = secureStorage.getVenueId()
  1281	            ?: return Result.failure(IllegalStateException("No venueId in secure storage"))
  1282	        val staffId = staffDe(current) ?: return Result.failure(IllegalStateException("No staffId in secure storage"))
  1283	        val cliente = _selectedCustomer.value
  1284	        val pedido = pedidoEnLinea(current, staffId, cliente?.id)
  1285	        val llave = llaveDelIntento(pedido)
  1286	        // ANTES del POST: si el proceso muere esperando la respuesta, al reabrir Cobrar vuelve este carrito con ESTA llave y el reintento
  1287	        // encuentra la misma orden por `externalId`. Si no quedó escrito, no se sale a la red.
  1288	        if (!ventaSinRed.guardarCarrito(venueId, llave, current, cliente)) {
  1289	            return Result.failure(IllegalStateException(TEXTO_NO_SE_GUARDO_LA_VENTA))
  1290	        }
  1291	        val creada = crearOrdenEnLinea(venueId, pedido, llave)
  1292	        val fallo = creada.exceptionOrNull()
  1293	        if (fallo != null) {
  1294	            if (!pasaALaVentaLocal(fallo)) return Result.failure(fallo) // 4xx, 500…: como hoy (el carrito y su llave se quedan)
  1295	            // D5: el carrito y su llave se quedan: si la creación sí llegó, el reintento encuentra la misma orden.
  1296	            if (current.couponCode != null) return Result.failure(IllegalStateException(TEXTO_CUPON_SIN_RED))
  1297	            Timber.w(fallo, "🛒 El servidor no contestó al crear la orden: sigue como venta local con la misma llave")
  1298	            return ventaLocal(current, amountPesos, llaveYaUsada = llave)
  1299	        }
  1300	        val order = creada.getOrThrow()
  1301	        // La venta en línea terminó de prepararse: la orden ya existe y el cobro sigue en la pantalla de pago.
  1302	        ventaSinRed.borrarCarrito(venueId)
  1303	        // Persist the referral right before we hand off to PaymentScreen. Failures are swallowed: the cart already has the discount
  1304	        // applied, so blocking the charge over a transient backend failure would punish the customer for a backend hiccup.
  1305	        capturarReferido(orderId = order.id)
  1306	        // D5 (Codex r1 #7): con cupón manda el total del SERVIDOR, que acepta un centavo de redondeo: ése se cobra, y sólo es cortesía si
  1307	        // allá quedó en $0 (si no, la cuenta se quedaba debiendo $0.01 con la pantalla diciendo «completada»).
  1308	        val totalDelServidor = order.total.takeIf { current.couponCode != null }
  1309	        val gratis = totalDelServidor?.let { it.signum() == 0 } ?: (current.totalCents == 0)
  1310	        val importe = totalDelServidor?.toPlainString() ?: amountPesos
  1311	        return Result.success(
  1312	            if (gratis) {
  1313	                PaymentNavigationPayload.CompletedFreeCart(
  1314	                    amountPesosString = importe,
  1315	                    orderId = order.id,
  1316	                    orderNumber = order.orderNumber,
  1317	                )
  1318	            } else {
  1319	                PaymentNavigationPayload.Order(
  1320	                    amountPesosString = importe,
  1321	                    orderId = order.id,
  1322	                    orderNumber = order.orderNumber,
  1323	                    wasPayLaterOrder = false,
  1324	                )
  1325	            },
  1326	        )
  1327	    }

  1329	    /**
  1330	     * 💵 La venta local: el pedido para mobile (centavos, `isOfflineReplay`) y los renglones del ticket, con [llaveYaUsada] si ya
  1331	     * hubo un intento en línea (o la del carrito si no). En $0 se guarda YA como fila y no abre la pantalla de cobro; si no, el carrito a
  1332	     * medias se guarda antes de navegar con la venta (sólo efectivo) y la pantalla de cobro inserta la fila (C3).
  1333	     */
  1334	    private suspend fun ventaLocal(current: CartState, amountPesos: String, llaveYaUsada: String?): Result<PaymentNavigationPayload> {
  1335	        // D1 (diseño v5): el descuento del cliente nunca entra a una venta local. Ésta es la única puerta (sin red, y la caída de
  1336	        // `with-items` por red), como la cortesía sin red: se dice y la cajera lo quita o espera la red.
  1337	        if (current.descuentoEsDelCliente) {
  1338	            return Result.failure(IllegalStateException(textoDescuentoSinRed()))
  1339	        }
  1340	        val venueId = secureStorage.getVenueId()
  1341	            ?: return Result.failure(IllegalStateException("No venueId in secure storage"))
  1342	        val staffId = staffDe(current) ?: return Result.failure(IllegalStateException("No staffId in secure storage"))
  1343	        val cliente = _selectedCustomer.value
  1344	        val llave = llaveYaUsada ?: llaveDelIntento(pedidoEnLinea(current, staffId, cliente?.id))
  1345	        if (!ventaSinRed.guardarCarrito(venueId, llave, current, cliente)) {
  1346	            return Result.failure(IllegalStateException(TEXTO_NO_SE_GUARDO_LA_VENTA))
  1347	        }
  1348	        val shiftId = try {
  1349	            ventaSinRed.turnoDeLaVenta(venueId)
  1350	        } catch (e: Exception) {
  1351	            if (e is CancellationException) throw e
  1352	            Timber.e(e, "🛒 No se pudo leer la caja guardada para preparar la venta")
  1353	            return Result.failure(IllegalStateException("No se pudo leer la caja guardada. La venta se conserva; vuelve a intentar.", e))
  1354	        }
  1355	        val productos = _products.value.associateBy { it.id }
  1356	        val ahora = System.currentTimeMillis()
  1357	        val venta = VentaLocal(
  1358	            llave = llave,
  1359	            venueId = venueId,
  1360	            staffId = staffId,
  1361	            shiftId = shiftId,
  1362	            createdAtLocal = ahora,
  1363	            totalCents = current.totalCents,
  1364	            pedidoJson = armarPedidoParaMobile(current, llave, staffId, shiftId, ahora, productos, customerId = cliente?.id),
  1365	            renglones = renglonesDeTicket(current, llave, productos),
  1366	        )
  1367	        if (current.totalCents == 0) {
  1368	            return ventaSinRed.guardarVentaEnCero(venta).fold(
  1369	                onSuccess = {
  1370	                    // El carrito ya terminó: una recreación de la pantalla conserva SU ticket, nunca permite cobrarlo otra vez.
  1371	                    _ventaEnCeroGuardada.value = venta
  1372	                    reiniciarCarrito()
  1373	                    Result.success(PaymentNavigationPayload.VentaEnCeroGuardada(amountPesos, venta))
  1374	                },
  1375	                onFailure = { e ->
  1376	                    Timber.e(e, "🛒 No se pudo guardar la venta en \$0")
  1377	                    Result.failure(if (e is VentaLocalYaGuardadaException) e else IllegalStateException(TEXTO_NO_SE_GUARDO_LA_VENTA, e))
  1378	                },
  1379	            )
  1380	        }
  1381	        return Result.success(PaymentNavigationPayload.VentaLocal(amountPesos, Gson().toJson(venta)))
  1382	    }
  1383	
  1384	    private suspend fun capturarReferido(orderId: String?) {
  1385	        runCatching { captureReferralOnPayment(orderId = orderId) }
  1386	            .onFailure { e -> Timber.e(e, "🎁 captureReferralOnPayment threw (orderId=$orderId)") }
  1387	    }
  1388	
  1389	    /**
  1390	     * Creates an order with the current cart items linked to the selected
  1391	     * customer, leaving it `paymentStatus = PENDING`. The order shows up in
  1392	     * the dashboard's "Cuentas por Cobrar" view and in the operator's
  1393	     * pay-later list. No PaymentScreen navigation — operator goes back to a
  1394	     * fresh cart after creation.
  1395	     *
  1396	     * Requires `_selectedCustomer != null` (the whole point is binding the
  1397	     * debt to a customer). Cart must have items (no $0 free-cart in this path).
  1398	     * Mientras corre, el carrito no cambia ([ventaEnPreparacion]).
  1399	     *
  1400	     * **Known drift**: when the dashboard later settles this order via the
  1401	     * existing `settleOrder` endpoint, inventory is NOT deducted (the
  1402	     * inventory hook only fires on the regular fully-paid path). Operations
  1403	     * accepts this until `settleOrder` is fixed in a separate ticket. The
  1404	     * order itself is faithful — items, discounts, cortesía all persisted as
  1405	     * with regular Cobrar orders.
  1406	     */
  1407	    suspend fun createPayLaterOrder(): Result<Order> = candado.mientras {
  1408	        val current = _cartState.value
  1409	        val error = if (!connectionStateManager.hasInternet()) {
  1410	            if (current.couponCode != null) TEXTO_CUPON_SIN_RED else "Necesita internet"
  1411	        } else {
  1412	            errorParaCobrar(current)
  1413	        }
  1414	        when {
  1415	            error != null -> Result.failure(IllegalStateException(error))
  1416	            current.isEmpty -> Result.failure(IllegalStateException("El carrito está vacío"))
  1417	            _selectedCustomer.value == null -> Result.failure(IllegalStateException("Selecciona un cliente para Pagar después"))
  1418	            current.totalCents == 0 -> Result.failure(
  1419	                IllegalStateException("El total es \$0 — usa cortesía/cobrar normal en lugar de Pagar después"),
  1420	            )
  1421	            // The backend createOrderWithItems endpoint already accepts customerId
  1422	            // and leaves the order in PENDING + PENDING when total > 0. That's
  1423	            // exactly the pay-later shape. No separate "leave open" flag needed.
  1424	            // D5 (Codex r2 #4): si el servidor no contestó con un cupón, se dice lo del cupón, como en Cobrar.
  1425	            else -> createOrderWithCurrentItems().recoverCatching { fallo ->
  1426	                if (fallo.message == TEXTO_NO_CONFIRMADA_COBRAR) throw Exception("No pudimos confirmar la venta. Inténtalo otra vez")
  1427	                throw if (current.couponCode != null && pasaALaVentaLocal(fallo)) IllegalStateException(TEXTO_CUPON_SIN_RED, fallo) else fallo
  1428	            }
  1429	        }
  1430	    }
  1431	
  1432	    /**
  1433	     * Creates the full order in one backend call. This is intentionally only
  1434	     * used by the new Cobrar flow; legacy order screens keep their existing
  1435	     * create-order + add-items path.
  1436	     */
  1437	    private suspend fun createOrderWithCurrentItems(): Result<Order> {
  1438	        val venueId = secureStorage.getVenueId() ?: return Result.failure(
  1439	            IllegalStateException("No venueId in secure storage"),
  1440	        )
  1441	        val current = _cartState.value
  1442	        val staffId = staffDe(current) ?: return Result.failure(IllegalStateException("No staffId in secure storage"))
  1443	        val pedido = pedidoEnLinea(current, staffId, _selectedCustomer.value?.id)
  1444	        return crearOrdenEnLinea(venueId, pedido, llaveDelIntento(pedido))
  1445	    }
  1446	
  1447	    private fun staffDe(current: CartState): String? =
  1448	        current.selectedStaffId.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: secureStorage.getStaffId()
  1449	
  1450	    /**
  1451	     * El cuerpo de `/tpv/.../orders/with-items` del carrito, SIN `externalId` ni la autorización del descuento (es también la huella de
  1452	     * la venta: un reintento con o sin el PIN del gerente conserva su llave). Las dos se ponen en [crearOrdenEnLinea].
  1453	     */
  1454	    private fun pedidoEnLinea(current: CartState, staffId: String, customerId: String?): TpvCreateOrderWithItemsRequest {
  1455	        val requestItems = current.items.map { item ->
  1456	            val productId = (item.type as? CartItemType.ProductItem)?.productId
  1457	            TpvCreateOrderWithItemsItem(
  1458	                productId = productId,
  1459	                name = if (productId == null) item.name else null,
  1460	                quantity = item.quantity,
  1461	                unitPrice = if (productId == null) item.effectiveUnitPriceCents.centsToPesos() else null,
  1462	                modifierIds = item.selectedModifiers.map { it.modifierId },
  1463	                notes = item.itemNote,
  1464	                isCortesia = item.isCortesia,
  1465	                cortesiaReason = item.cortesiaReason,
  1466	                itemDiscountId = item.itemDiscountId,
  1467	            )
  1468	        }
  1469	
  1470	        val grossSubtotalCents = current.items.sumOf { it.grossLineCents() }
  1471	
  1472	        // When `orderDiscount` is null but `manualDiscountCents` > 0, the
  1473	        // freeform manual discount travels as `discount`; backend treats it
  1474	        // as an unbacked reduction with no Discount entity. Con cupón o con un descuento del catálogo (el del cliente incluido) viaja
  1475	        // lo que muestra el carrito: el SERVIDOR lo calcula al centavo y lo compara; si cambió, lo dice (antes iba 0 y lo rechazaba).
  1476	        val cupon = current.couponCode
  1477	        val discountForWire = if (current.orderDiscount != null) current.discountCents else current.manualDiscountCents
  1478	
  1479	        return TpvCreateOrderWithItemsRequest(
  1480	            items = requestItems,
  1481	            staffId = staffId,
  1482	            orderType = OrderType.TAKEOUT,
  1483	            source = "TPV",
  1484	            customerId = customerId,
  1485	            discount = discountForWire.centsToPesos(),
  1486	            // El id de un cupón no es de un descuento del catálogo: un id de cupón aquí era el 400 que no dejaba cobrar (D5).
  1487	            orderDiscountId = current.orderDiscount?.id?.takeIf { cupon == null && it.isNotBlank() },
  1488	            couponCode = cupon,
  1489	            taxAmount = BigDecimal.ZERO,
  1490	            tip = BigDecimal.ZERO,
  1491	            subtotal = grossSubtotalCents.centsToPesos(),
  1492	            total = current.totalCents.centsToPesos(),
  1493	            note = current.orderNote,
  1494	        )
  1495	    }
  1496	
  1497	    /**
  1498	     * La llave de la venta (= `externalId` de la orden = llave de la venta local). Idempotencia por venta (retry-safety): si el pedido
  1499	     * es IDÉNTICO al del intento anterior (la respuesta se perdió y el cajero volvió a picar, o el carrito volvió tras morir el proceso),
  1500	     * se reusa la misma llave y el backend devuelve la orden original en vez de crear otra — que en free-cart ($0) significaba segunda
  1501	     * deducción de stock. Si el carrito CAMBIÓ, la huella difiere y nace llave nueva: jamás se puede recibir de vuelta una orden con los
  1502	     * items viejos. Al CREARSE la orden (o guardarse la venta) la llave se olvida — reusarla entre dos ventas devolvería la anterior.
  1503	     */
  1504	    private fun llaveDelIntento(pedido: TpvCreateOrderWithItemsRequest): String {
  1505	        val huella = pedido.toString()
  1506	        return orderAttemptExternalId.takeIf { orderAttemptFingerprint == huella }
  1507	            ?: UUID.randomUUID().toString().also {
  1508	                orderAttemptExternalId = it
  1509	                orderAttemptFingerprint = huella
  1510	            }

     3	import com.google.firebase.crashlytics.FirebaseCrashlytics
     4	import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
     5	import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
     6	import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow

     8	import timber.log.Timber

    12	/**
    13	 * Connection State Manager
    14	 *
    15	 * Singleton that holds the current connection state.
    16	 * This bridges ConnectionViewModel (which handles reconnection logic)
    17	 * with DeviceHealthViewModel (which shows unified alerts).
    18	 *
    19	 * **Why Singleton?**
    20	 * - ConnectionViewModel updates connection state
    21	 * - DeviceHealthViewModel observes and includes in unified alert list
    22	 * - Single source of truth for connection status across the app
    23	 *
    24	 * **Alert Priority:**
    25	 * - P0: No internet (most critical)
    26	 * - P2: Server down
    27	 *
    28	 * **Slow Connection Detection:**
    29	 * Uses hysteresis to prevent the "Internet lento" banner from flickering.
    30	 * Once a slow measurement (>= 5000ms) is detected, the alert stays active
    31	 * for at least [SLOW_COOLDOWN_MS] even if subsequent measurements are fast
    32	 * (heartbeat payloads are small, so warm connections appear fast even on slow networks).
    33	 */
    34	@Singleton
    35	class ConnectionStateManager @Inject constructor() {
    36	
    37	    companion object {
    38	        const val SLOW_THRESHOLD_MS = 5_000L
    39	        const val SLOW_COOLDOWN_MS = 60_000L
    40	    }
    41	
    42	    private val _connectionState = MutableStateFlow(ConnectionState())
    43	    val connectionState: StateFlow<ConnectionState> = _connectionState.asStateFlow()
    44	
    45	    /** Timestamp (epoch ms) when a slow measurement was last observed */
    46	    @Volatile
    47	    private var lastSlowDetectedAt: Long = 0L
    48	
    49	    /** Skip the first measurement (cold TCP + TLS + DNS is always slow) */
    50	    @Volatile
    51	    private var measurementCount: Int = 0
    52	
    53	    private val _latido = MutableStateFlow(Latido())
    54	
    55	    /**
    56	     * Cada escritura del LATIDO (su respuesta, o «sin red» tras su gracia), con lo que dejó: una sola emisión por escritura,
    57	     * así nunca se empareja un latido con el estado de otro (D13, Codex r3 #1), y se ve también un latido idéntico al
    58	     * anterior, que no cambia [connectionState] (Codex r2). Con `escrituras = 0`, el `true/true` de arriba es el supuesto
    59	     * del arranque, no evidencia de que el servidor conteste (Codex r1 #1). `setWifiSinSalida` no es del latido.
    60	     */
    61	    val latido: StateFlow<Latido> = _latido.asStateFlow()
    62	
    63	    private fun publicarLatido() =
    64	        _latido.update { Latido(escrituras = it.escrituras + 1, contesto = _connectionState.value.isFullyConnected) }
    65	
    66	    /**
    67	     * Update internet connectivity status
    68	     */
    69	    fun setInternetConnected(connected: Boolean) {
    70	        val current = _connectionState.value
    71	        if (current.hasInternet != connected) {
    72	            Timber.i("🌐 [ConnectionState] Internet: $connected")
    73	            _connectionState.value = current.copy(hasInternet = connected)
    74	        }
    75	        publicarLatido()
    76	    }
    77	
    78	    /**
    79	     * Update server connectivity status
    80	     */
    81	    fun setServerConnected(connected: Boolean) {
    82	        val current = _connectionState.value
    83	        if (current.hasServer != connected) {
    84	            Timber.i("🖥️ [ConnectionState] Server: $connected")
    85	            _connectionState.value = current.copy(hasServer = connected)
    86	        }
    87	        publicarLatido()
    88	    }
    89	
    90	    /**
    91	     * Update both states at once (with optional latency measurement).
    92	     * Applies hysteresis for slow connection detection.
    93	     */
    94	    fun updateState(hasInternet: Boolean, hasServer: Boolean, latencyMs: Long? = null) {
    95	        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
    96	
    97	        // Skip first measurement (cold TCP + TLS handshake is always slow)
    98	        if (latencyMs != null) measurementCount++
    99	        val isWarm = measurementCount > 1
   100	
   101	        // Track slow detection with hysteresis (only after first measurement)
   102	        val isMeasurementSlow = isWarm && latencyMs != null && latencyMs >= SLOW_THRESHOLD_MS
   103	        if (isMeasurementSlow) {
   104	            lastSlowDetectedAt = now
   105	        }
   106	        val inCooldown = (now - lastSlowDetectedAt) < SLOW_COOLDOWN_MS
   107	        val effectiveSlow = isMeasurementSlow || (inCooldown && lastSlowDetectedAt > 0L)
   108	
   109	        val newState = ConnectionState(
   110	            hasInternet = hasInternet,
   111	            hasServer = hasServer,
   112	            latencyMs = latencyMs,
   113	            isSlowConnection = effectiveSlow,
   114	            // Lo publica WifiSinSalidaMonitor; un heartbeat no lo borra.
   115	            wifiSinSalida = _connectionState.value.wifiSinSalida,
   116	        )
   117	        if (_connectionState.value != newState) {
   118	            val latencyStr = if (latencyMs != null) ", latency=${latencyMs}ms" else ""
   119	            val slowStr = if (effectiveSlow) " [SLOW]" else ""
   120	            Timber.i("🔄 [ConnectionState] Updated: internet=$hasInternet, server=$hasServer$latencyStr$slowStr")
   121	            _connectionState.value = newState
   122	
   123	            // 🛡️ CRASHLYTICS KEYS (2026-04-09) — Network state at crash time.
   124	            // Updated every time connection state changes so ANY crash (fatal or non-fatal)
   125	            // automatically includes the exact network conditions. No more guessing from
   126	            // WhatsApp screenshots — Crashlytics "Claves" tab shows these directly.
   127	            try {
   128	                FirebaseCrashlytics.getInstance().apply {
   129	                    setCustomKey("network_internet", hasInternet)
   130	                    setCustomKey("network_server", hasServer)
   131	                    setCustomKey("network_slow", effectiveSlow)
   132	                    if (latencyMs != null) setCustomKey("network_latency_ms", latencyMs)
   133	                }
   134	            } catch (_: Exception) { /* Firebase not initialized yet during early startup */ }
   135	        }
   136	        publicarLatido()
   137	    }
   138	
   139	    /** Lo publican [WifiSinSalidaMonitor] (DETECTADO / NO) y [ControlDeWifi] vía el VM (REINICIANDO). */
   140	    fun setWifiSinSalida(estado: EstadoWifiSinSalida) {
   141	        val current = _connectionState.value
   142	        if (current.wifiSinSalida != estado) {
   143	            Timber.i("📶 [ConnectionState] WiFi sin salida: $estado")
   144	            _connectionState.value = current.copy(wifiSinSalida = estado)
   145	        }
   146	    }
   147	
   148	    /**
   149	     * Check if fully connected (has internet AND server)
   150	     */
   151	    fun isFullyConnected(): Boolean {
   152	        val state = _connectionState.value
   153	        return state.hasInternet && state.hasServer
   154	    }
   155	
   156	    /**
   157	     * Check if device has internet connectivity (regardless of server reachability).
   158	     * Used by flag-gated preflight that only requires processor connectivity, not our backend.
   159	     */
   160	    fun hasInternet(): Boolean = _connectionState.value.hasInternet
   161	
   162	    /**
   163	     * Reset to connected state (e.g., after successful reconnection)
   164	     */
   165	    fun resetToConnected() {
   166	        lastSlowDetectedAt = 0L
   167	        measurementCount = 0
   168	        _connectionState.value = ConnectionState(hasInternet = true, hasServer = true, latencyMs = null)
   169	        Timber.i("✅ [ConnectionState] Reset to fully connected")
   170	    }
   171	}
   172	
   173	/**
   174	 * Connection state data class
   175	 *
   176	 * [isSlowConnection] is computed by [ConnectionStateManager] with hysteresis,
   177	 * not purely from a single [latencyMs] measurement.
   178	 */
   179	/** Una escritura del latido: cuántas van y si con ella quedó internet Y servidor (lo que el aviso rojo llama conectado). */
   180	data class Latido(val escrituras: Long = 0L, val contesto: Boolean = false)
   181	
   182	data class ConnectionState(
   183	    val hasInternet: Boolean = true,
   184	    val hasServer: Boolean = true,
   185	    val latencyMs: Long? = null,
   186	    val isSlowConnection: Boolean = false,
   187	    val wifiSinSalida: EstadoWifiSinSalida = EstadoWifiSinSalida.NO,
   188	) {
   189	    val isFullyConnected: Boolean get() = hasInternet && hasServer
   190	    val hasAnyIssue: Boolean get() = !hasInternet || !hasServer
   191	}

     3	import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableSharedFlow
     4	import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharedFlow
     5	import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asSharedFlow
     6	import javax.inject.Inject
     7	import javax.inject.Singleton
     8	
     9	/**
    10	 * Connection Event Manager
    11	 *
    12	 * Singleton service for broadcasting connection restoration events.
    13	 * Allows ViewModels to listen to connection changes without directly coupling them.
    14	 *
    15	 * **Architecture Pattern (Toast POS / Square POS):**
    16	 * ```
    17	 * ConnectionViewModel → detects reconnection → emits event via ConnectionEventManager
    18	 * ShiftViewModel → listens to event → reloads shift data
    19	 * HomeViewModel → listens to event → reloads config (future)
    20	 * OrderViewModel → listens to event → reloads orders (future)
    21	 * ```
    22	 *
    23	 * **Why not inject ViewModels?**
    24	 * - Hilt prohibits injecting @HiltViewModel into other ViewModels
    25	 * - ViewModels should not depend on each other
    26	 * - Use singleton services for cross-ViewModel communication
    27	 *
    28	 * **Usage:**
    29	 * ```kotlin
    30	 * // In ConnectionViewModel (emitter):
    31	 * connectionEventManager.emitConnectionRestored(
    32	 *     attemptsBeforeReconnection = reconnectionAttempts
    33	 * )
    34	 *
    35	 * // In ShiftViewModel (listener):
    36	 * connectionEventManager.connectionRestoredEvents.collect { event ->
    37	 *     loadCurrentShift() // Reload data
    38	 * }
    39	 * ```
    40	 */
    41	@Singleton
    42	class ConnectionEventManager @Inject constructor(
    43	    @dagger.hilt.android.qualifiers.ApplicationContext private val appContext: android.content.Context,
    44	) {
    45	
    46	    private val _connectionRestoredEvents = MutableSharedFlow<ConnectionRestoredEvent>(
    47	        replay = 0,
    48	        extraBufferCapacity = 10
    49	    )
    50	
    51	    /**
    52	     * Connection Restored Events
    53	     *
    54	     * Emitted when backend connection is restored after being disconnected.
    55	     * Multiple ViewModels can subscribe to this event.
    56	     */
    57	    val connectionRestoredEvents: SharedFlow<ConnectionRestoredEvent> = _connectionRestoredEvents.asSharedFlow()
    58	
    59	    /**
    60	     * Emit connection restored event
    61	     *
    62	     * Called by ConnectionViewModel when heartbeat succeeds after failures.
    63	     */
    64	    suspend fun emitConnectionRestored(
    65	        attemptsBeforeReconnection: Int
    66	    ) {
    67	        runCatching { PaymentSyncScheduler.runNow(appContext) }
    68	            .onFailure { timber.log.Timber.w(it, "Caja y registros conservan el respaldo periódico de sincronización.") }
    69	        _connectionRestoredEvents.emit(
    70	            ConnectionRestoredEvent(
    71	                timestamp = java.time.Instant.now().toString(),
    72	                attemptsBeforeReconnection = attemptsBeforeReconnection
    73	            )
    74	        )
    75	    }
    76	}
    77	
    78	/**
    79	 * Connection Restored Event
    80	 *
    81	 * Emitted when backend connection is restored after being disconnected.
    82	 * Triggers "reconciliation sync" pattern (Toast POS / Square POS).
    83	 *
    84	 * **What to sync:**
    85	 * - Shift status (critical - drives "Sin turno activo" UI)
    86	 * - Terminal config (merchant accounts, settings)
    87	 * - Open orders (if any)
    88	 * - Pending transactions (if any)
    89	 *
    90	 * @param timestamp ISO-8601 timestamp of reconnection
    91	 * @param attemptsBeforeReconnection Number of failed attempts before success
    92	 */
    93	data class ConnectionRestoredEvent(
    94	    val timestamp: String,
    95	    val attemptsBeforeReconnection: Int
    96	)

     3	import android.content.Context
     4	import androidx.work.Constraints
     5	import androidx.work.ExistingPeriodicWorkPolicy
     6	import androidx.work.NetworkType
     7	import androidx.work.PeriodicWorkRequestBuilder
     8	import androidx.work.WorkManager
     9	import com.jaac.avoqado_tpv.core.data.workers.PaymentSyncWorker
    10	import timber.log.Timber
    11	import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit
    12	
    13	/**
    14	 * Payment Sync Scheduler
    15	 *
    16	 * Manages the lifecycle of the PaymentSyncWorker.
    17	 * Follows Square Terminal and Toast POS patterns for offline payment queue management.
    18	 *
    19	 * **Responsibilities:**
    20	 * - Start payment sync on login
    21	 * - Stop payment sync on logout
    22	 * - Configure sync interval and constraints
    23	 * - Ensure only ONE payment sync worker runs at a time
    24	 *
    25	 * **Usage:**
    26	 * ```kotlin
    27	 * // Start payment sync (call on login success)
    28	 * PaymentSyncScheduler.start(context)
    29	 *
    30	 * // Stop payment sync (call on logout)
    31	 * PaymentSyncScheduler.stop(context)
    32	 *
    33	 * // Check if running
    34	 * val isRunning = PaymentSyncScheduler.isRunning(context)
    35	 * ```
    36	 *
    37	 * **Design Decisions:**
    38	 * - **ExistingPeriodicWorkPolicy.KEEP**: Don't restart if already running
    39	 * - **CONNECTED network constraint**: Only sync when network is available
    40	 * - **15 minute interval**: Standard for POS offline queue (Square=15min, Toast=15min)
    41	 * - **KEEP policy**: Avoid restarting sync on every login (preserves backoff state)
    42	 *
    43	 * **Why Static Object?**
    44	 * - Payment sync scheduling doesn't hold state
    45	 * - Simple API matching Android best practices (like NotificationManager)
    46	 * - Avoids DI complexity for utility class
    47	 *
    48	 * **World-Class References:**
    49	 * - Square Terminal: OfflineSyncScheduler with 15-min interval
    50	 * - Toast POS: PaymentQueueScheduler with 15-min interval
    51	 * - Stripe Terminal: TransactionSyncScheduler with exponential backoff
    52	 */
    53	object PaymentSyncScheduler {
    54	
    55	    private const val PAYMENT_SYNC_WORK_NAME = "payment_sync_worker"
    56	    private const val PAYMENT_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES = 15L // Standard: Square=15min, Toast=15min
    57	
    58	    /** Nombre único del worker inmediato. Sin esto, cada runNow() creaba uno nuevo. */
    59	    const val IMMEDIATE_WORK_NAME = "payment_sync_now"
    60	
    61	    /**
    62	     * Start payment sync monitoring
    63	     *
    64	     * **When to call:**
    65	     * - After successful login
    66	     * - After app restart (if user already logged in)
    67	     * - After first payment is queued
    68	     *
    69	     * **Behavior:**
    70	     * - KEEPS existing worker if already running (KEEP policy)
    71	     * - Starts after 15 minutes, then repeats every 15 minutes
    72	     * - Runs in background even when app is closed
    73	     *
    74	     * **Constraints:**
    75	     * - Requires network connection (CONNECTED)
    76	     * - No battery/charging constraints (payments must sync ASAP)
    77	     *
    78	     * **Important:** Using KEEP policy prevents restarting worker on every login.
    79	     * This preserves WorkManager's exponential backoff state if worker is retrying.
    80	     *
    81	     * @param context Application or Activity context
    82	     */
    83	    fun start(context: Context) {
    84	        Timber.d("🚀 Starting payment sync scheduler (interval: ${PAYMENT_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES}min)")
    85	
    86	        // Define constraints: Only run when network is available
    87	        val constraints = Constraints.Builder()
    88	            .setRequiredNetworkType(NetworkType.CONNECTED) // Don't run when offline
    89	            .build()
    90	
    91	        // Build periodic work request
    92	        val paymentSyncWorkRequest = PeriodicWorkRequestBuilder<PaymentSyncWorker>(
    93	            repeatInterval = PAYMENT_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES,
    94	            repeatIntervalTimeUnit = TimeUnit.MINUTES
    95	        )
    96	            .setConstraints(constraints)
    97	            .addTag(PAYMENT_SYNC_WORK_NAME)
    98	            .build()
    99	
   100	        // Enqueue work (KEEP policy preserves existing worker)
   101	        WorkManager.getInstance(context).enqueueUniquePeriodicWork(
   102	            PAYMENT_SYNC_WORK_NAME,
   103	            ExistingPeriodicWorkPolicy.KEEP, // Keep existing work if already running
   104	            paymentSyncWorkRequest
   105	        )
   106	
   107	        runNow(context) // Arranque/reingreso: también puede haber sólo aperturas o cierres, sin pagos pendientes.
   108	        Timber.d("✅ Payment sync scheduler started")
   109	    }
   110	
   111	    /** APPEND persiste la siguiente pasada aunque la inmediata actual aún esté RUNNING (KEEP la perdería). */
   112	    fun continuarDespuesDeEstaPasada(context: Context) {
   113	        val request = androidx.work.OneTimeWorkRequestBuilder<PaymentSyncWorker>()
   114	            .setConstraints(Constraints.Builder().setRequiredNetworkType(NetworkType.CONNECTED).build())
   115	            .addTag(PAYMENT_SYNC_WORK_NAME).build()
   116	        WorkManager.getInstance(context).enqueueUniqueWork(IMMEDIATE_WORK_NAME, androidx.work.ExistingWorkPolicy.APPEND_OR_REPLACE, request)
   117	    }
   118	
   119	    /**
   120	     * Stop payment sync monitoring
   121	     *
   122	     * **When to call:**
   123	     * - On logout
   124	     * - When terminal is deactivated
   125	     *
   126	     * **Behavior:**
   127	     * - Cancels all pending and running payment sync workers
   128	     * - Gracefully stops background execution
   129	     *
   130	     * **Important:** This does NOT delete queued payments from Room database.
   131	     * Payments remain in queue and will sync when worker is restarted.
   132	     *
   133	     * @param context Application or Activity context
   134	     */
   135	    fun stop(context: Context) {
   136	        Timber.d("🛑 Stopping payment sync scheduler")
   137	
   138	        WorkManager.getInstance(context).cancelUniqueWork(PAYMENT_SYNC_WORK_NAME)
   139	
   140	        Timber.d("✅ Payment sync scheduler stopped")
   141	    }
   142	
   143	    /**
   144	     * Check if payment sync is currently running
   145	     *
   146	     * **Use cases:**
   147	     * - Debugging: Verify sync started after login
   148	     * - UI indicators: Show "Syncing..." badge on PaymentSuccessScreen
   149	     * - Health checks: Ensure sync running in production
   150	     *
   151	     * @param context Application or Activity context
   152	     * @return true if payment sync worker is enqueued or running
   153	     */
   154	    suspend fun isRunning(context: Context): Boolean {
   155	        val workInfos = WorkManager.getInstance(context)
   156	            .getWorkInfosForUniqueWork(PAYMENT_SYNC_WORK_NAME)
   157	            .get()
   158	
   159	        val isRunning = workInfos.isNotEmpty() && workInfos.any { workInfo ->
   160	            !workInfo.state.isFinished
   161	        }
   162	
   163	        Timber.d("Payment sync running: $isRunning")
   164	        return isRunning
   165	    }
   166	
   167	    /**
   168	     * Force immediate sync execution (for testing and manual retry)
   169	     *
   170	     * **Use cases:**
   171	     * - Debugging: Test sync logic immediately
   172	     * - User action: "Sync Now" button in UI
   173	     * - After queueing payment: Trigger immediate sync attempt
   174	     *
   175	     * WorkManager doesn't guarantee immediate execution,
   176	     * but this requests it to run ASAP (usually within 10 seconds).
   177	     *
   178	     * **Important:** This creates a ONE-TIME work request (doesn't affect periodic worker)
   179	     *
   180	     * @param context Application or Activity context
   181	     */
   182	    fun runNow(context: Context) {
   183	        Timber.d("🔥 Requesting immediate payment sync execution")
   184	
   185	        val constraints = Constraints.Builder()
   186	            .setRequiredNetworkType(NetworkType.CONNECTED)
   187	            .build()
   188	
   189	        val immediateWorkRequest = androidx.work.OneTimeWorkRequestBuilder<PaymentSyncWorker>()
   190	            .setConstraints(constraints)
   191	            .build()
   192	
   193	        // KEEP, no REPLACE: si ya hay un worker drenando la cola queremos que TERMINE.
   194	        // REPLACE lo mataria a media tanda y las filas reclamadas tendrian que esperar
   195	        // al umbral de stale-claim para volver a salir.
   196	        WorkManager.getInstance(context).enqueueUniqueWork(
   197	            IMMEDIATE_WORK_NAME,
   198	            androidx.work.ExistingWorkPolicy.KEEP,
   199	            immediateWorkRequest,
   200	        )
   201	
   202	        Timber.d("✅ Immediate payment sync requested")
   203	    }
   204	
   205	    /**
   206	     * Get count of pending payments (for UI badge)
   207	     *
   208	     * **Use cases:**
   209	     * - Show "3 payments pending" badge in app
   210	     * - Display alert if failed payments > 0
   211	     *
   212	     * **Note:** This requires injecting PaymentQueueRepository.
   213	     * For now, this is a placeholder for future implementation.
   214	     *
   215	     * Alternative: Use LiveData/Flow from repository in ViewModel layer.
   216	     */
   217	    // TODO: Implement getPendingCount() using repository (requires DI)
   218	    // suspend fun getPendingCount(context: Context): Int { ... }
   219	}

+        assertThat(RecordPaymentUseCase.nadieContesto(null)).isFalse()

   104	    /** Checkpoint 2 (E5): el 2xx del replay se aplica como VEREDICTO en la libreta ANTES de dar por consumida la fila. */
   105	    private val paymentAttemptLedger: com.jaac.avoqado_tpv.features.payment.data.ledger.PaymentAttemptLedger,
   106	    private val subirVentaGuardadaUseCase: SubirVentaGuardadaUseCase,
