   111	    private val proofOfSaleQueueRepository: com.jaac.avoqado_tpv.features.verification.data.ProofOfSaleQueueRepository? = null,
   112	) : CoroutineWorker(appContext, workerParams) {
   113	
   114	    companion object {
   115	        /**
   116	         * Unique worker name for WorkManager.
   117	         * Use this to enqueue/cancel work.
   118	         */
   119	        const val WORKER_NAME = "payment_sync_worker"
   120	
   121	        // 🔴 F-10: NO local MAX_RETRY_ATTEMPTS constant here on purpose. The only caller
   122	        // (the SyncOutcome.Permanent branch of syncPayment()) used to release() a row
   123	        // straight to FAILED by claiming retryCount == MAX_RETRY_ATTEMPTS — replaced by
   124	        // markPermanentlyFailedClaim(), which sets FAILED + permanent=true directly and
   125	        // never touches retry_count. PendingPaymentEntity.MAX_RETRY_ATTEMPTS (10) remains
   126	        // the single source of truth for transient-failure retry accounting (release()'s
   127	        // CASE in PendingPaymentDao).
   128	
   129	        /**
   130	         * Maximum payments to process per worker run.
   131	         * Prevents WorkManager 10-minute timeout when queue is large.
   132	         * Remaining payments will be processed in the next periodic run (15 min).
   133	         */
   134	        private const val MAX_PAYMENTS_PER_RUN = 10
   135	
   136	        /** Tope de reembolsos por corrida, por la misma razón que [MAX_PAYMENTS_PER_RUN]. */
   137	        private const val MAX_REFUNDS_PER_RUN = 10
   138	
   139	        /**
   140	         * Espejo por nombre EXACTO del `code` del servidor (`NotFoundError(..., 'ORDER_NOT_FOUND')`
   141	         * en `payment.tpv.service.ts`, serializado por `src/app.ts`). Un nombre mal escrito NO
   142	         * truena: el respaldo a venta rápida simplemente deja de ocurrir y el cobro queda en
   143	         * revisión manual — el lado seguro, pero hay que enterarse. Por eso vive en una constante
   144	         * y no suelto en la comparación.
   145	         */
   146	        private const val CODIGO_ORDEN_INEXISTENTE = "ORDER_NOT_FOUND"
   147	    }
   148	
   149	    /**
   150	     * Execute payment sync task
   151	     *
   152	     * **Return Values:**
   153	     * - Result.success() → All pending payments processed (even if some failed)
   154	     * - Result.retry() → Worker crashed, WorkManager should retry
   155	     * - Result.failure() → Permanent failure (e.g., terminal not activated)
   156	     *
   157	     * **Retry Policy:**
   158	     * This worker ALWAYS returns Result.success() to continue periodic runs.
   159	     * Each payment gets exactly ONE attempt per call to doWork() (F-9) — the retry
   160	     * cadence across attempts is owned by WorkManager (15-min periodic run + the
   161	     * NetworkType.CONNECTED constraint), never by a loop/delay() in this worker.
   162	     *
   163	     * **Important:** Unlike HeartbeatWorker, this worker processes ALL pending payments
   164	     * in a single run. It doesn't stop at the first failure — `markSyncedChecked` /
   165	     * `releaseClaim` / `markPermanentlyFailedClaim` and the repository writes underneath
   166	     * them (`markSynced`, `release`, `markPermanentlyFailed`) all catch internally and
   167	     * return `0` on error, so one payment's write failing (disk full, DB closed
   168	     * mid-teardown) can't throw out of the `for (payment in batch)` loop
   169	     * and abandon the rest of an already-claimed, already-charged batch (F-9, Fix round 1
   170	     * — `release()` used to be the one write in `PaymentQueueRepositoryImpl` without a
   171	     * try/catch, unlike its siblings).
   172	     */
   173	    override suspend fun doWork(): Result {
   174	        // D7: filas que esta pasada reclamó y todavía no resuelve. Si el worker sale antes
   175	        // (WorkManager lo detiene porque la red «validada» parpadeó al reconectar, o una
   176	        // excepción lo tumba), se sueltan aquí; antes esperaban STALE_CLAIM_MS (15 min).
   177	        var sinTerminar: List<com.jaac.avoqado_tpv.features.payment.domain.model.QueuedPayment> = emptyList()
   178	        return try {
   179	            Timber.d("💾 [Payment Sync] Worker started")
   180	
   181	            var sessionProgress = shiftRepository?.sincronizarAperturasLocales() ?: 0
   182	            val tableProgress = tableSyncCoordinator.replayCurrentVenueBatch()
   183	            shiftRepository?.consultarCriptosPendientes()
   184	            var paymentProgress = 0
   185	
   186	            // Reclamar la tanda: otro worker corriendo en paralelo NO puede tomar estas filas.
   187	            // NonCancellable: si WorkManager cancela justo cuando Room ya confirmó el reclamo, un
   188	            // withContext cancelable tira el resultado y nadie sabría qué filas soltar (Codex r1,
   189	            // hallazgo 1). La cancelación se nota en la siguiente suspensión, con la lista ya guardada.
   190	            val batch = kotlinx.coroutines.withContext(kotlinx.coroutines.NonCancellable) {
   191	                paymentQueueRepository.claimBatch(MAX_PAYMENTS_PER_RUN)
   192	            }
   193	            sinTerminar = batch
   194	
   195	            if (batch.isEmpty()) {
   196	                // 🔴 Ya NO se hace `return` aquí: los reembolsos se drenan abajo aunque no haya
   197	                // cobros pendientes. Con el `return` temprano, una cola con sólo reembolsos no se
   198	                // reproducía nunca.
   199	                Timber.d("✅ [Payment Sync] No hay pagos pendientes por sincronizar")
   200	            } else {
   201	                Timber.i("🔄 [Payment Sync] ${batch.size} pagos reclamados para sincronizar")
   202	
   203	                // Process each payment independently (one failure doesn't block others)
   204	                var successCount = 0
   205	                var failedCount = 0
   206	
   207	                for ((i, payment) in batch.withIndex()) {
   208	                    val success = syncPayment(payment)
   209	                    // Sin suspensión entre el return de syncPayment y esta línea: una
   210	                    // cancelación nunca deja fuera de la lista una fila sin resolver.
   211	                    sinTerminar = batch.subList(i + 1, batch.size)
   212	                    if (success) {
   213	                        successCount++
   214	                        paymentProgress++
   215	                    } else {
   216	                        failedCount++
   217	                    }
   218	                }
   219	
   220	                // Log summary
   221	                Timber.i(
   222	                    "✅ [Payment Sync] Worker completed | " +
   223	                            "success=$successCount | failed=$failedCount | batch=${batch.size}"
   224	                )
   225	            }
   226	
   227	            // 💸 Los reembolsos van DESPUÉS de los cobros: un reembolso apunta al pago original
   228	            // por su id de servidor, así que primero tiene que existir el pago allá.
   229	            drainRefunds()
   230	            sessionProgress += shiftRepository?.sincronizarCierresLocales() ?: 0
   231	            // 📸 Las fotos de comprobante van al final de todo (también después de los cierres de caja): cuelgan de un pago que
   232	            // ya tiene que existir en el servidor, y una subida lenta a Firebase no debe retrasar el dinero.
   233	            val moreProofs = drainProofOfSale()
   234	            if ((tableProgress.resolved > 0 && tableProgress.moreAfterLimit) ||
   235	                (paymentProgress > 0 && batch.size == MAX_PAYMENTS_PER_RUN && paymentQueueRepository.getPendingCount() > 0) ||
   236	                (sessionProgress > 0 && shiftRepository?.haySesionesPorReproducir() == true) ||
   237	                moreProofs) {
   238	                com.jaac.avoqado_tpv.core.util.PaymentSyncScheduler.continuarDespuesDeEstaPasada(applicationContext)
   239	            }
   240	
   241	            // 🔴 Avisar al banner (bug real 2026-08-07): el contador de
   242	            // "pagos pendientes" es un StateFlow de push manual
   243	            // (PaymentQueueStateManager) — el boton de reintentar lo refresca al
   244	            // RESETEAR, pero nadie lo refrescaba cuando la tanda TERMINABA. Los 2
   245	            // pagos atorados sincronizaron (success=2) y el banner siguio diciendo
   246	            // "2 pagos pendientes" hasta reiniciar la app: el cajero reintenta un
   247	            // trabajo ya hecho. Refrescar aqui cierra el ciclo con la verdad de Room.
   248	            // En try/catch propio: un fallo al CONTAR jamas puede tirar el worker —
   249	            // los pagos de esta tanda YA se procesaron.
   250	            // 💸 Se refresca SIEMPRE, también cuando sólo hubo reembolsos (auditoría de Codex F10): un
   251	            // reembolso atorado era invisible hasta intentar cerrar el turno.
   252	            try {
   253	                val pending = paymentQueueRepository.getPendingCount()
   254	                val failed = paymentQueueRepository.getFailedCount()
   255	                val pendingRefunds = refundQueueRepository.getPendingCount()
   256	                val failedRefunds = refundQueueRepository.getFailedCount()
   257	                paymentQueueStateManager.refreshCounts(pending, failed, pendingRefunds, failedRefunds)
   258	            } catch (e: CancellationException) {
   259	                throw e
   260	            } catch (e: Exception) {
   261	                Timber.w(e, "⚠️ [Payment Sync] No se pudieron refrescar los conteos del banner")
   262	            }
   263	
   264	            // ALWAYS return success to continue periodic runs
   265	            // Even if some payments failed, we want the worker to run again in 15 minutes
   266	            Result.success()
   267	        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
   268	            // Worker detenido (WorkManager: la red dejó de estar validada, límite de tiempo):
   269	            // no es un intento fallido, así que no cuenta.
   270	            soltarSinTerminar(sinTerminar, contarIntentoDeLaPrimera = false)
   271	            throw e
   272	        } catch (e: Exception) {
   273	            // Unexpected error (e.g., database crash). La fila en curso sí cuenta el intento:
   274	            // si revienta siempre igual, llega a FAILED y la ve una persona en vez de girar.
   275	            Timber.e(e, "💥 [Payment Sync] Worker crashed, will retry")
   276	            soltarSinTerminar(sinTerminar, contarIntentoDeLaPrimera = true)
   277	            Result.retry()
   278	        }
   279	    }
   280	
   281	    /**
   282	     * D7: regresa a PENDING las filas que esta pasada reclamó y no resolvió, para que la
   283	     * siguiente (WorkManager la vuelve a correr al volver la red) las tome de inmediato.
   284	     *
   285	     * Es seguro soltarlas: el worker que las tenía ya no escribe nada más (está saliendo), cada
   286	     * `release` es compare-and-swap por su token (si ya se marcó o la reclamó otro, 0 filas), y
   287	     * si el envío en vuelo sí llegó al SERVIDOR, el reenvío lleva la MISMA llave de idempotencia
   288	     * (`@@unique([venueId, idempotencyKey])` en Payment): es lo mismo que pasaba al vencer
   289	     * STALE_CLAIM_MS, sólo que sin esperar 15 min. `NonCancellable` porque en una corrutina ya
   290	     * cancelada el `withContext(Dispatchers.IO)` del repositorio no correría.
   291	     *
   292	     * Si el PROCESO muere, nadie llega aquí: esa fila la sigue recuperando el barrido de 15 min.
   293	     */
   294	    private suspend fun soltarSinTerminar(
   295	        filas: List<com.jaac.avoqado_tpv.features.payment.domain.model.QueuedPayment>,
app/src/main/java/com/jaac/avoqado_tpv/core/data/local/dao/PendingPaymentDao.kt:478:    suspend fun claimBatch(limit: Int, token: String, now: Long, staleBefore: Long): List<PendingPaymentEntity> {
app/src/main/java/com/jaac/avoqado_tpv/core/data/local/dao/PendingPaymentDao.kt:536:    suspend fun release(id: Long, token: String, retryCount: Int, error: String): Int

    41	@Singleton
    42	class ConnectionEventManager @Inject constructor(
    43	    @dagger.hilt.android.qualifiers.ApplicationContext private val appContext: android.content.Context,
    44	) {
    45	
    46	    private val _connectionRestoredEvents = MutableSharedFlow<ConnectionRestoredEvent>(
    47	        replay = 0,
    48	        extraBufferCapacity = 10
    49	    )
    50	
    51	    /**
    52	     * Connection Restored Events
    53	     *
    54	     * Emitted when backend connection is restored after being disconnected.
    55	     * Multiple ViewModels can subscribe to this event.
    56	     */
    57	    val connectionRestoredEvents: SharedFlow<ConnectionRestoredEvent> = _connectionRestoredEvents.asSharedFlow()
    58	
    59	    /**
    60	     * Emit connection restored event
    61	     *
    62	     * Called by ConnectionViewModel when heartbeat succeeds after failures.
    63	     */
    64	    suspend fun emitConnectionRestored(
    65	        attemptsBeforeReconnection: Int
    66	    ) {
    67	        runCatching { PaymentSyncScheduler.runNow(appContext) }
    68	            .onFailure { timber.log.Timber.w(it, "Caja y registros conservan el respaldo periódico de sincronización.") }
    69	        _connectionRestoredEvents.emit(
    70	            ConnectionRestoredEvent(
    71	                timestamp = java.time.Instant.now().toString(),
    72	                attemptsBeforeReconnection = attemptsBeforeReconnection
    73	            )
    74	        )
    75	    }
    76	}
    77	
    78	/**
    79	 * Connection Restored Event
    80	 *
    81	 * Emitted when backend connection is restored after being disconnected.
    82	 * Triggers "reconciliation sync" pattern (Toast POS / Square POS).
    83	 *
    84	 * **What to sync:**
    85	 * - Shift status (critical - drives "Sin turno activo" UI)
    86	 * - Terminal config (merchant accounts, settings)
    87	 * - Open orders (if any)
    88	 * - Pending transactions (if any)
    89	 *
    90	 * @param timestamp ISO-8601 timestamp of reconnection
    91	 * @param attemptsBeforeReconnection Number of failed attempts before success
    92	 */
    93	data class ConnectionRestoredEvent(
    94	    val timestamp: String,
    95	    val attemptsBeforeReconnection: Int
    96	)
    45	 * - Simple API matching Android best practices (like NotificationManager)
    46	 * - Avoids DI complexity for utility class
    47	 *
    48	 * **World-Class References:**
    49	 * - Square Terminal: OfflineSyncScheduler with 15-min interval
    50	 * - Toast POS: PaymentQueueScheduler with 15-min interval
    51	 * - Stripe Terminal: TransactionSyncScheduler with exponential backoff
    52	 */
    53	object PaymentSyncScheduler {
    54	
    55	    private const val PAYMENT_SYNC_WORK_NAME = "payment_sync_worker"
    56	    private const val PAYMENT_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES = 15L // Standard: Square=15min, Toast=15min
    57	
    58	    /** Nombre único del worker inmediato. Sin esto, cada runNow() creaba uno nuevo. */
    59	    const val IMMEDIATE_WORK_NAME = "payment_sync_now"
    60	
    61	    /**
    62	     * Start payment sync monitoring
    63	     *
    64	     * **When to call:**
    65	     * - After successful login
    66	     * - After app restart (if user already logged in)
    67	     * - After first payment is queued
    68	     *
    69	     * **Behavior:**
    70	     * - KEEPS existing worker if already running (KEEP policy)
    71	     * - Starts after 15 minutes, then repeats every 15 minutes
    72	     * - Runs in background even when app is closed
    73	     *
    74	     * **Constraints:**
    75	     * - Requires network connection (CONNECTED)
    76	     * - No battery/charging constraints (payments must sync ASAP)
    77	     *
    78	     * **Important:** Using KEEP policy prevents restarting worker on every login.
    79	     * This preserves WorkManager's exponential backoff state if worker is retrying.
    80	     *
    81	     * @param context Application or Activity context
    82	     */
    83	    fun start(context: Context) {
    84	        Timber.d("🚀 Starting payment sync scheduler (interval: ${PAYMENT_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES}min)")
    85	
    86	        // Define constraints: Only run when network is available
    87	        val constraints = Constraints.Builder()
    88	            .setRequiredNetworkType(NetworkType.CONNECTED) // Don't run when offline
    89	            .build()
    90	
    91	        // Build periodic work request
    92	        val paymentSyncWorkRequest = PeriodicWorkRequestBuilder<PaymentSyncWorker>(
    93	            repeatInterval = PAYMENT_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES,
    94	            repeatIntervalTimeUnit = TimeUnit.MINUTES
    95	        )
    96	            .setConstraints(constraints)
    97	            .addTag(PAYMENT_SYNC_WORK_NAME)
    98	            .build()
    99	
   100	        // Enqueue work (KEEP policy preserves existing worker)
   101	        WorkManager.getInstance(context).enqueueUniquePeriodicWork(
   102	            PAYMENT_SYNC_WORK_NAME,
   103	            ExistingPeriodicWorkPolicy.KEEP, // Keep existing work if already running
   104	            paymentSyncWorkRequest
   105	        )
   106	
   107	        runNow(context) // Arranque/reingreso: también puede haber sólo aperturas o cierres, sin pagos pendientes.
   108	        Timber.d("✅ Payment sync scheduler started")
   109	    }
   110	
   111	    /** APPEND persiste la siguiente pasada aunque la inmediata actual aún esté RUNNING (KEEP la perdería). */
   112	    fun continuarDespuesDeEstaPasada(context: Context) {
   113	        val request = androidx.work.OneTimeWorkRequestBuilder<PaymentSyncWorker>()
   114	            .setConstraints(Constraints.Builder().setRequiredNetworkType(NetworkType.CONNECTED).build())
   115	            .addTag(PAYMENT_SYNC_WORK_NAME).build()
   116	        WorkManager.getInstance(context).enqueueUniqueWork(IMMEDIATE_WORK_NAME, androidx.work.ExistingWorkPolicy.APPEND_OR_REPLACE, request)
   117	    }
   118	
   119	    /**
   120	     * Stop payment sync monitoring
   121	     *
   122	     * **When to call:**
   123	     * - On logout
   124	     * - When terminal is deactivated
   125	     *
   126	     * **Behavior:**
   127	     * - Cancels all pending and running payment sync workers
   128	     * - Gracefully stops background execution
   129	     *
   130	     * **Important:** This does NOT delete queued payments from Room database.
   131	     * Payments remain in queue and will sync when worker is restarted.
   132	     *
   133	     * @param context Application or Activity context
   134	     */
   135	    fun stop(context: Context) {
   136	        Timber.d("🛑 Stopping payment sync scheduler")
   137	
   138	        WorkManager.getInstance(context).cancelUniqueWork(PAYMENT_SYNC_WORK_NAME)
   139	
   140	        Timber.d("✅ Payment sync scheduler stopped")
   141	    }
   142	
   143	    /**
   144	     * Check if payment sync is currently running
   145	     *
   146	     * **Use cases:**
   147	     * - Debugging: Verify sync started after login
   148	     * - UI indicators: Show "Syncing..." badge on PaymentSuccessScreen
   149	     * - Health checks: Ensure sync running in production
   150	     *
   151	     * @param context Application or Activity context
   152	     * @return true if payment sync worker is enqueued or running
   153	     */
   154	    suspend fun isRunning(context: Context): Boolean {
   155	        val workInfos = WorkManager.getInstance(context)
   156	            .getWorkInfosForUniqueWork(PAYMENT_SYNC_WORK_NAME)
   157	            .get()
   158	
   159	        val isRunning = workInfos.isNotEmpty() && workInfos.any { workInfo ->
   160	            !workInfo.state.isFinished
   161	        }
   162	
   163	        Timber.d("Payment sync running: $isRunning")
   164	        return isRunning
   165	    }
   166	
   167	    /**
   168	     * Force immediate sync execution (for testing and manual retry)
   169	     *
   170	     * **Use cases:**
   171	     * - Debugging: Test sync logic immediately
   172	     * - User action: "Sync Now" button in UI
   173	     * - After queueing payment: Trigger immediate sync attempt
   174	     *
   175	     * WorkManager doesn't guarantee immediate execution,
   176	     * but this requests it to run ASAP (usually within 10 seconds).
   177	     *
   178	     * **Important:** This creates a ONE-TIME work request (doesn't affect periodic worker)
   179	     *
   180	     * @param context Application or Activity context
   181	     */
   182	    fun runNow(context: Context) {
   183	        Timber.d("🔥 Requesting immediate payment sync execution")
   184	
   185	        val constraints = Constraints.Builder()
   186	            .setRequiredNetworkType(NetworkType.CONNECTED)
   187	            .build()
   188	
   189	        val immediateWorkRequest = androidx.work.OneTimeWorkRequestBuilder<PaymentSyncWorker>()
   190	            .setConstraints(constraints)
   191	            .build()
   192	
   193	        // KEEP, no REPLACE: si ya hay un worker drenando la cola queremos que TERMINE.
   194	        // REPLACE lo mataria a media tanda y las filas reclamadas tendrian que esperar
   195	        // al umbral de stale-claim para volver a salir.
   196	        WorkManager.getInstance(context).enqueueUniqueWork(
   197	            IMMEDIATE_WORK_NAME,
   198	            androidx.work.ExistingWorkPolicy.KEEP,
   199	            immediateWorkRequest,
   200	        )
   201	
   202	        Timber.d("✅ Immediate payment sync requested")
   203	    }
   204	
   205	    /**
   206	     * Get count of pending payments (for UI badge)
   207	     *
   208	     * **Use cases:**
   209	     * - Show "3 payments pending" badge in app
   210	     * - Display alert if failed payments > 0
   211	     *
   212	     * **Note:** This requires injecting PaymentQueueRepository.
   213	     * For now, this is a placeholder for future implementation.
   214	     *
   215	     * Alternative: Use LiveData/Flow from repository in ViewModel layer.
   216	     */
   217	    // TODO: Implement getPendingCount() using repository (requires DI)
   218	    // suspend fun getPendingCount(context: Context): Int { ... }
   219	}

    34	@Singleton
    35	class ConnectionStateManager @Inject constructor() {
    36	
    37	    companion object {
    38	        const val SLOW_THRESHOLD_MS = 5_000L
    39	        const val SLOW_COOLDOWN_MS = 60_000L
    40	    }
    41	
    42	    private val _connectionState = MutableStateFlow(ConnectionState())
    43	    val connectionState: StateFlow<ConnectionState> = _connectionState.asStateFlow()
    44	
    45	    /** Timestamp (epoch ms) when a slow measurement was last observed */
    46	    @Volatile
    47	    private var lastSlowDetectedAt: Long = 0L
    48	
    49	    /** Skip the first measurement (cold TCP + TLS + DNS is always slow) */
    50	    @Volatile
    51	    private var measurementCount: Int = 0
    52	
    53	    private val _latido = MutableStateFlow(Latido())
    54	
    55	    /**
    56	     * Cada escritura del LATIDO (su respuesta, o «sin red» tras su gracia), con lo que dejó: una sola emisión por escritura,
    57	     * así nunca se empareja un latido con el estado de otro (D13, Codex r3 #1), y se ve también un latido idéntico al
    58	     * anterior, que no cambia [connectionState] (Codex r2). Con `escrituras = 0`, el `true/true` de arriba es el supuesto
    59	     * del arranque, no evidencia de que el servidor conteste (Codex r1 #1). `setWifiSinSalida` no es del latido.
    60	     */
    61	    val latido: StateFlow<Latido> = _latido.asStateFlow()
    62	
    63	    private fun publicarLatido() =
    64	        _latido.update { Latido(escrituras = it.escrituras + 1, contesto = _connectionState.value.isFullyConnected) }
    65	
    66	    /**
    67	     * Update internet connectivity status
    68	     */
    69	    fun setInternetConnected(connected: Boolean) {
    70	        val current = _connectionState.value
    71	        if (current.hasInternet != connected) {
    72	            Timber.i("🌐 [ConnectionState] Internet: $connected")
    73	            _connectionState.value = current.copy(hasInternet = connected)
    74	        }
    75	        publicarLatido()
    76	    }
    77	
    78	    /**
    79	     * Update server connectivity status
    80	     */
    81	    fun setServerConnected(connected: Boolean) {
    82	        val current = _connectionState.value
    83	        if (current.hasServer != connected) {
    84	            Timber.i("🖥️ [ConnectionState] Server: $connected")
    85	            _connectionState.value = current.copy(hasServer = connected)
    86	        }
    87	        publicarLatido()
    88	    }
    89	
    90	    /**
    91	     * Update both states at once (with optional latency measurement).
    92	     * Applies hysteresis for slow connection detection.
    93	     */
    94	    fun updateState(hasInternet: Boolean, hasServer: Boolean, latencyMs: Long? = null) {
    95	        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
    96	
    97	        // Skip first measurement (cold TCP + TLS handshake is always slow)
    98	        if (latencyMs != null) measurementCount++
    99	        val isWarm = measurementCount > 1
   100	
   101	        // Track slow detection with hysteresis (only after first measurement)
   102	        val isMeasurementSlow = isWarm && latencyMs != null && latencyMs >= SLOW_THRESHOLD_MS
   103	        if (isMeasurementSlow) {
   104	            lastSlowDetectedAt = now
   105	        }
   106	        val inCooldown = (now - lastSlowDetectedAt) < SLOW_COOLDOWN_MS
   107	        val effectiveSlow = isMeasurementSlow || (inCooldown && lastSlowDetectedAt > 0L)
   108	
   109	        val newState = ConnectionState(
   110	            hasInternet = hasInternet,
   111	            hasServer = hasServer,
   112	            latencyMs = latencyMs,
   113	            isSlowConnection = effectiveSlow,
   114	            // Lo publica WifiSinSalidaMonitor; un heartbeat no lo borra.
   115	            wifiSinSalida = _connectionState.value.wifiSinSalida,
   116	        )
   117	        if (_connectionState.value != newState) {
   118	            val latencyStr = if (latencyMs != null) ", latency=${latencyMs}ms" else ""
   119	            val slowStr = if (effectiveSlow) " [SLOW]" else ""
   120	            Timber.i("🔄 [ConnectionState] Updated: internet=$hasInternet, server=$hasServer$latencyStr$slowStr")
   121	            _connectionState.value = newState
   122	
   123	            // 🛡️ CRASHLYTICS KEYS (2026-04-09) — Network state at crash time.
   124	            // Updated every time connection state changes so ANY crash (fatal or non-fatal)
   125	            // automatically includes the exact network conditions. No more guessing from
   126	            // WhatsApp screenshots — Crashlytics "Claves" tab shows these directly.
   127	            try {
   128	                FirebaseCrashlytics.getInstance().apply {
   129	                    setCustomKey("network_internet", hasInternet)
   130	                    setCustomKey("network_server", hasServer)
   131	                    setCustomKey("network_slow", effectiveSlow)
   132	                    if (latencyMs != null) setCustomKey("network_latency_ms", latencyMs)
   133	                }
   134	            } catch (_: Exception) { /* Firebase not initialized yet during early startup */ }
   135	        }
   136	        publicarLatido()
   137	    }
   138	
   139	    /** Lo publican [WifiSinSalidaMonitor] (DETECTADO / NO) y [ControlDeWifi] vía el VM (REINICIANDO). */
   140	    fun setWifiSinSalida(estado: EstadoWifiSinSalida) {
   141	        val current = _connectionState.value
   142	        if (current.wifiSinSalida != estado) {
   143	            Timber.i("📶 [ConnectionState] WiFi sin salida: $estado")
   144	            _connectionState.value = current.copy(wifiSinSalida = estado)
   145	        }
   146	    }
   147	
   148	    /**
   149	     * Check if fully connected (has internet AND server)
   150	     */
   151	    fun isFullyConnected(): Boolean {
   152	        val state = _connectionState.value
   153	        return state.hasInternet && state.hasServer
   154	    }
   155	
   156	    /**
   157	     * Check if device has internet connectivity (regardless of server reachability).
   158	     * Used by flag-gated preflight that only requires processor connectivity, not our backend.
   159	     */
   160	    fun hasInternet(): Boolean = _connectionState.value.hasInternet
   161	
   162	    /**
   163	     * Reset to connected state (e.g., after successful reconnection)
   164	     */
   165	    fun resetToConnected() {
   166	        lastSlowDetectedAt = 0L
   167	        measurementCount = 0
   168	        _connectionState.value = ConnectionState(hasInternet = true, hasServer = true, latencyMs = null)
   169	        Timber.i("✅ [ConnectionState] Reset to fully connected")
   170	    }
   171	}
   172

   831	                // na16c21f: también con «No se pudo imprimir» abierto (su éxito guardado sin recibo).
   832	                val exitoSinRecibo = exitoVisible(_state.value)?.takeIf { it.receipt == null }
   833	                if (retry != null && exitoSinRecibo != null) {
   834	                    Timber.i("🔄 [PaymentViewModel] Re-attempting backend recording for pending receipt | ref=${retry.referenceNumber}")
   835	                    try {
   836	                        // 🔴 NonCancellable: si el proceso muere/la pantalla se abandona a
   837	                        // media espera (recordPaymentUseCase reintenta hasta 5 veces con
   838	                        // backoff, ~67.5s peor caso), la llamada NO debe abortarse a medio
   839	                        // camino — sin esto el cobro puede saltarse la cola offline por
   840	                        // completo: dinero cobrado, sin registro, sin fila en la cola.
   841	                        val result = withContext(NonCancellable) {
   842	                            recordPaymentUseCase(
   843	                                context = retry.context,
   844	                                cardDetails = retry.cardDetails,
   845	                                authorizationNumber = retry.authorizationNumber,
   846	                                referenceNumber = retry.referenceNumber,
   847	                            )
   848	                        }
   849	                        result.onSuccess { receipt ->
   850	                            Timber.i("✅ [PaymentViewModel] Receipt recovered on reconnect | paymentId=${receipt.paymentId}")
   851	                            applyRecordedReceiptToSuccessState(
   852	                                receipt = receipt,
   853	                                cardDetails = retry.cardDetails,
   854	                                referenceNumber = retry.referenceNumber,
   855	                                orderIdForFlow = retry.orderIdForFlow,
   856	                                llave = retry.context.idempotencyKey,
   857	                            )
   858	                            // Codex r2 #1 (encargo 1df453): sólo si sigue siendo ESTE reintento; el cobro siguiente pudo dejar el suyo.
   859	                            if (pendingReceiptRetry === retry) pendingReceiptRetry = null
   860	                        }.onFailure { error ->
   861	                            Timber.w("⚠️ [PaymentViewModel] Receipt retry failed on reconnect (will retry next reconnect): ${error.message}")
   862	                            // Leave pendingReceiptRetry intact — will retry on next reconnect event
   863	                            // The periodic queue worker is the backstop
   864	                        }
   865	                    } catch (e: CancellationException) {
   866	                        // CancellationException es RuntimeException en Kotlin: relanzar ANTES
   867	                        // del catch(Exception) general, o se traga y rompe la cancelacion
   868	                        // estructurada.
   869	                        throw e
   870	                    } catch (e: Exception) {
   871	                        Timber.e(e, "❌ [PaymentViewModel] Unexpected error during receipt retry on reconnect")
   872	                    }
   873	                }
   874	            }
   875	        }

   320	     * Retryable — la fila vuelve a PENDING (claim liberado) y el siguiente disparo
   321	     * (periódico o NetworkType.CONNECTED) reintenta; nunca un loop ni un delay() locales.
   322	     *
   323	     * `CancellationException` se repropaga sin tocar la fila aquí: la suelta `doWork()` al
   324	     * salir ([soltarSinTerminar], D7), sin contarla como intento. Sólo si el proceso muere la
   325	     * recupera el barrido de stale-claim (`STALE_CLAIM_MS`).
   326	     *
   327	     * @param payment Queued payment to sync
   328	     * @return true si quedó sincronizado (o el backend ya lo tenía), false si no
   329	     */
   330	    private suspend fun syncPayment(payment: com.jaac.avoqado_tpv.features.payment.domain.model.QueuedPayment): Boolean {
   331	        if (payment.shiftMembership == "MANAGED") {
   332	            try {
   333	                (shiftReplayGate ?: throw com.jaac.avoqado_tpv.features.shift.data.repository.ShiftMappingPendingException())
   334	                    .reference(payment.toPaymentContext(), payment.referenceNumber)
   335	            } catch (cancelled: CancellationException) { throw cancelled }
   336	              catch (waiting: com.jaac.avoqado_tpv.features.shift.data.repository.ShiftMappingPendingException) {
   337	                releaseClaim(payment, payment.retryCount, waiting.message ?: "La venta espera su caja.")
   338	                return false
   339	              }
   340	        }
   341	        if (payment.orderRequestJson == null) {
   342	            return registrarYClasificar(payment, payment.toPaymentContext(), permitirCaidaAVentaRapida = true)
   343	        }
   344	
   345	        // Su pedido y su pago van juntos; un 404 nunca autoriza convertirlos en una venta rápida sin artículos.
   346	        val outcome = try {
   347	            subirVentaGuardadaUseCase(payment)
   348	        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
   349	            throw e
   350	        } catch (e: Exception) {
   351	            classifySyncFailure(e)
   352	        }
   353	        return when (outcome) {
   354	            is SyncOutcome.Synced -> {
   355	                markSyncedChecked(payment)
   356	            }
   357	            is SyncOutcome.Permanent -> {
   358	                markPermanentlyFailedClaim(payment, outcome.reason)
   359	                false
   360	            }
   361	            is SyncOutcome.Retryable -> {
   362	                releaseClaim(payment, payment.retryCount + 1, "No se pudo subir la venta guardada; se reintenta")
   363	                false
   364	            }
   365	        }
   366	    }
   367	
   368	    /**
   369	     * Registra [context] y clasifica el desenlace. Recursiva **una sola vez**, y sólo por el
   370	     * camino de [esOrdenQueYaNoExiste]: el segundo intento entra con
   371	     * `permitirCaidaAVentaRapida = false`, así que no puede haber bucle.

   373	    private suspend fun registrarYClasificar(
   374	        payment: com.jaac.avoqado_tpv.features.payment.domain.model.QueuedPayment,
   375	        context: PaymentContext,

   378	        Timber.d("🔄 [Payment Sync] Syncing payment | ref=${payment.referenceNumber} | queueId=${payment.queueId}")
   379	
   380	        val result = try {
   381	            recordPaymentUseCase(
   382	                context = context,
   383	                cardDetails = payment.toCardDetails(),
   384	                authorizationNumber = payment.authorizationNumber ?: "",
   385	                referenceNumber = payment.referenceNumber,
   386	                alreadyQueued = true,

   388	        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
   389	            throw e // Worker cancelado — no tocar la fila aquí: la suelta doWork() al salir (D7)
   390	        } catch (e: Exception) {
   391	            // Excepción inesperada (no un Result.failure) — se normaliza para pasar por el
   392	            // mismo clasificador que un fallo devuelto normalmente. Ver KDoc arriba.
   393	            // 🔴 kotlin.Result CALIFICADO A PROPÓSITO: dentro de una clase que extiende
   394	            // CoroutineWorker, el `Result` sin calificar resuelve al `Result` ANIDADO de
   395	            // ListenableWorker (androidx.work), no al de Kotlin — error de tipos silencioso
   396	            // en compilación (esperaba `Data`, no `Throwable`).
   397	            Timber.e(e, "💥 [Payment Sync] Excepción inesperada al sincronizar | ref=${payment.referenceNumber}")
   398	            kotlin.Result.failure(e)
   399	        }
   400	
   401	        if (result.isSuccess) {
   402	            Timber.i(
   403	                "✅ [Payment Sync] Payment synced successfully | " +
   404	                        "ref=${payment.referenceNumber} | queueId=${payment.queueId}"
   405	            )
   406	            // Checkpoint 2 (E5, Codex v3 cambio 4): el 2xx puede ser una EVIDENCIA (segunda captura, colisión) y la libreta
   407	            // tiene que saberlo ANTES de que esta fila desaparezca — S6 no puede recuperar un intento sin vínculo S1, así
   408	            // que este 2xx es su único canal. Si el veredicto NO quedó durable y la libreta tiene fila, la cola se conserva
   409	            // reintentable con la MISMA llave (el REST es idempotente; nunca se vuelve al SDK). Una cola anterior a la
   410	            // libreta (sin fila) sigue siendo no-op.
   411	            if (!veredictoDurableEnLaLibreta(payment, context, result.getOrThrow())) {
   412	                releaseClaim(payment, payment.retryCount + 1, "El veredicto del servidor no quedó guardado en la libreta; se reintenta")
   413	                return false
   414	            }
   415	            return markSyncedChecked(payment)
   416	        }
   417	
   418	        // 🔴 F-6: clasificar por código HTTP, NUNCA por texto del mensaje.
   419	        // Antes: errorMessage.contains("409") — un reference number como
   420	        // "000000409231" contiene "409" y marcaba como SUCCESS una venta que
   421	        // nunca llegó al backend. Ver SyncOutcome.kt / BackendHttpException.kt.
   422	        return when (val outcome = classifySyncFailure(result.exceptionOrNull())) {
   423	            is SyncOutcome.Synced -> {
   424	                Timber.i(

   426	                    payment.referenceNumber,
   427	                )

   429	            }
   430	
   431	            is SyncOutcome.Permanent -> {

   435	                // 404 la mata para siempre (`permanent = true`, que sólo un tap manual revierte) —
   436	                // incluso a un cobro de TARJETA ya capturado, o sea dinero que salió del cliente y
   437	                // no existe en Avoqado. Se reintenta UNA vez como venta rápida, con la MISMA llave
   438	                // de idempotencia y la MISMA referencia: el saldo queda registrado y lo único que
   439	                // se pierde es el vínculo con una orden que ya no está.

   441	                    Timber.w(

   455	                    payment.referenceNumber,

   473	                    payment.referenceNumber,
   474	                    transientError,
   475	                )
   476	                releaseClaim(payment, retryCount = payment.retryCount + 1, error = transientError)
   477	                false
   478	            }
   479	        }
   480	    }
   481	
   482	    /**
   483	     * ¿El 404 que acabamos de recibir es «la orden ya no existe» sobre un cobro de ORDEN?
   484	     *
   485	     * Las CUATRO condiciones importan:
   486	     * - el contexto que se mandó era [PaymentContext.OrderPayment] (un pago rápido no tiene
   487	     *   orden que perder, y volver a mandarlo sería el mismo intento otra vez);
   488	     * - la fila NO es de AngelPay — su replay de orden es el comportamiento de siempre y no se
   489	     *   toca (su contexto tampoco es `OrderPayment`, así que esto es cinturón y tirantes);
   490	     * - el código es **404** exacto. Un 400 (payload mal formado) o un 422 (regla de negocio,
   491	     *   p.ej. orden ya cerrada) NO se arreglan quitando la orden: ahí sí es permanente;
   492	     * - 🔴 y el servidor dijo **`ORDER_NOT_FOUND`**. Aceptar CUALQUIER 404 era el P1 nuevo de la
   493	     *   2ª auditoría de Codex (2026-09-07): el mismo endpoint contesta 404 con «Venue or Order
   494	     *   not found» cuando el venue es otro o la ruta cambió, y convertir ESO en venta rápida
   495	     *   deja una orden VIVA sin cobro — que es exactamente la puerta al doble cobro humano
   496	     *   (otra terminal la ve sin pagar y la vuelve a cobrar). Sin código, la fila queda
   497	     *   permanente y la revisa una persona: ruidoso se arregla, silencioso no.
   498	     *
   499	     * Los DOS códigos que el servidor distingue en este 404, y qué hace el worker con cada uno:
   500	     * - **`ORDER_NOT_FOUND`** — la orden no existe en ninguna parte (borrada, migrada) ⇒ se
   501	     *   reproduce UNA sola vez como **venta rápida**, con la MISMA `idempotencyKey` y la MISMA
   502	     *   referencia: el dinero queda registrado y lo único que se pierde es el vínculo con una
   503	     *   orden que ya no está.
   504	     * - **`ORDER_NOT_IN_VENUE`** — la orden SÍ existe, pero en otra sucursal ⇒ **permanente**,
   505	     *   revisión humana. Convertirla en venta suelta dejaría esa orden VIVA sin cobro y otra
   506	     *   terminal la volvería a cobrar: el doble cobro humano que esta guardia existe para evitar.
   507	     * - Cualquier **otro** código, o **ninguno** (servidor viejo, cuerpo vacío, HTML de un proxy)
   508	     *   ⇒ permanente, por lo mismo: sin que el servidor lo diga, nadie puede afirmar la causa.
   509	     */
   510	    private fun esOrdenQueYaNoExiste(
   511	        payment: com.jaac.avoqado_tpv.features.payment.domain.model.QueuedPayment,
   512	        context: PaymentContext,
   513	        error: Throwable?,
   514	    ): Boolean = context is PaymentContext.OrderPayment &&
   515	        payment.processor != ProcessorType.ANGELPAY &&
   516	        error is BackendHttpException &&
   517	        error.statusCode == 404 &&
   518	        error.errorCode == CODIGO_ORDEN_INEXISTENTE
   519	
   520	    /**
   521	     * Marca la fila como SUCCESS — compare-and-swap con el `claimToken` que trae [payment]
   522	     * (F-8, Fix round 1). Si otro worker ya reclamó esta fila (se puso stale y alguien más
   523	     * la tomó), la escritura afecta 0 filas: el backend YA tiene el pago — eso no cambia —
   524	     * solo perdimos la carrera por el bookkeeping local. NUNCA reintentar el write en ese
   525	     * caso; quien tenga el claim vigente resolverá su propio intento por su cuenta.
   526	     */
   527	    private suspend fun markSyncedChecked(payment: com.jaac.avoqado_tpv.features.payment.domain.model.QueuedPayment): Boolean {
   528	        val affected = paymentQueueRepository.markSynced(payment.queueId, payment.claimToken.orEmpty())
   529	        if (affected == 0) {
   530	            Timber.w(
   531	                "⚠️ [Payment Sync] markSynced() no afectó filas | ref=%s | queueId=%d | " +
   532	                    "no se confirmó el cierre local; el backend ya confirmó el pago",
   385	     * @param id Row ID of payment
   386	     * @param token El claim_token con el que este worker obtuvo la fila (de claimBatch)
   387	     * @param error Razón legible (de SyncOutcome.Permanent.reason), p.ej. "HTTP 404: Order not found"
   388	     * @return Filas afectadas (0 = otro worker ya reclamó esta fila)
   389	     */
   390	    @Query("""
   391	        UPDATE pending_payments
   392	        SET sync_status = 'FAILED',
   393	            permanent = 1,
   394	            last_error = :error,
   395	            claim_token = NULL,
   396	            claimed_at = NULL
   397	        WHERE id = :id AND claim_token = :token
   398	    """)
   399	    suspend fun markPermanentlyFailed(id: Long, token: String, error: String): Int
   400	
   401	    /**
   402	     * Reset all TRANSIENT failed payments back to PENDING for retry.
   403	     *
   404	     * **Use Case:** When connection is restored, give transient failures another chance.
   405	     * Resets retry_count to 0 so they get full retry attempts.
   406	     *
   407	     * **Excludes permanent failures (F-10):** a row with `permanent = 1` failed on a
   408	     * business rule (400/404/422) that reconnecting cannot fix. Resurrecting it here
   409	     * would burn MAX_RETRY_ATTEMPTS (10) more attempts on every connectivity flap,
   410	     * forever, instead of settling into a stable, inert FAILED state — which today is
   411	     * surfaced only by the [getFailedCount] badge; [getAllFailed] has no caller yet, so
   412	     * there is no dedicated review screen (that's a real gap, not solved by this flag).
   413	     * Rows FAILED before v29 have `permanent = 0` (unknown → resurrectable by default,
   414	     * see PendingPaymentEntity.permanent) and are unaffected by this change.
   415	     *
   416	     * **This is the AUTOMATIC path** (fires on every reconnect — see
   417	     * [com.jaac.avoqado_tpv.core.presentation.viewmodels.HomeViewModel]). For the
   418	     * deliberate human tap that should also un-stick permanent rows, use
   419	     * [resetAllFailedIncludingPermanent] instead — see its KDoc, added fix round 1 after
   420	     * a 401/403-misclassification bug showed this exclusion needed an escape hatch.
   421	     *
   422	     * @return Number of payments reset
   423	     */
   424	    @Query("""
   425	        UPDATE pending_payments
   426	        SET sync_status = 'PENDING', retry_count = 0, acknowledged = 0, acknowledged_by = NULL, acknowledged_at = NULL
   427	        WHERE sync_status = 'FAILED' AND permanent = 0
   428	    """)
   429	    suspend fun resetAllFailed(): Int
   430	
   431	    /**
   432	     * Reset ALL failed payments back to PENDING for retry — INCLUDING `permanent = 1`
   433	     * rows (F-10, fix round 1). This is the deliberate HUMAN escape hatch: [resetAllFailed]
   434	     * (what the automatic reconnect flow calls) excludes permanent rows on purpose, but an
   435	     * operator who taps "reintentar" — after re-logging in, or an admin fixing the venue —
   436	     * needs a way to actually un-stick a row that got wrongly stuck, or that a since-fixed
   437	     * condition no longer blocks. Also resets `permanent` back to 0: if the underlying
   438	     * problem is still there, the very next sync attempt re-marks it FAILED+permanent via
   439	     * [markPermanentlyFailed] — this is a one-shot second chance, not blanket amnesty.
   440	     *
   441	     * @return Number of payments reset (transient + permanent)
   442	     */
   443	    @Query("""
   444	        UPDATE pending_payments
   445	        SET sync_status = 'PENDING', retry_count = 0, permanent = 0, acknowledged = 0, acknowledged_by = NULL, acknowledged_at = NULL
   446	        WHERE sync_status = 'FAILED'
   447	    """)
   448	    suspend fun resetAllFailedIncludingPermanent(): Int
   449	
   450	    /**
   451	     * Busca una fila por reference number, acotada al venue del pago que está
   452	     * intentando encolarse.
   453	     *
   454	     * Se usa al chocar el índice único en [insert]: hay que distinguir "el mismo pago
   455	     * ya está encolado y a salvo" (PENDING/SYNCING → OK) de "una fila vieja bloquea el
   456	     * índice y este pago NO entró" (SUCCESS/FAILED → error). Ver spec §4.2 F-7.
   457	     *
   458	     * **Por qué lleva `venueId` (Fix round 1, finding 1):** el índice único de
   459	     * `reference_number` es GLOBAL, pero esta tabla NO se vacía al cambiar de venue o
   460	     * hacer logout (a propósito — guarda datos de dinero). Un terminal reasignado de un
   461	     * venue a otro (patrón operativo real: promotores de relevo, re-parenting) puede
   462	     * reusar un reference_number corto/secuencial que OTRO venue aún tiene vivo
   463	     * localmente. Sin este filtro, esa fila ajena se leería como "el mismo pago,
   464	     * ya a salvo" y el pago nuevo se perdería en silencio — el mismo bug que esta
   465	     * función existe para cerrar, solo que cruzando venues en vez de cruzando el tiempo.
   466	     */
   467	    @Query("SELECT * FROM pending_payments WHERE reference_number = :reference AND venue_id = :venueId LIMIT 1")
   468	    suspend fun findByReference(reference: String, venueId: String): PendingPaymentEntity?
   469	
   470	    /**
   471	     * Reclama hasta [limit] filas de forma atómica y devuelve exactamente las que
   472	     * quedaron marcadas con [token].
   473	     *
   474	     * Toma filas PENDING, y también SYNCING abandonadas (claimed_at < staleBefore),
   475	     * para que un worker muerto no deje pagos atorados para siempre.
   476	     */
   477	    @Transaction
   478	    suspend fun claimBatch(limit: Int, token: String, now: Long, staleBefore: Long): List<PendingPaymentEntity> {
   479	        normalizarClaimsFuturos(now, staleBefore)
   480	        markClaimed(limit, token, now, staleBefore)
   481	        return getClaimed(token)
   482	    }
   483	
   484	    // Una corrección grande del reloj conserva el dueño y le da un lease completo antes del relevo.
   485	    @Query("""
   486	        UPDATE pending_payments SET claimed_at = :now
   487	        WHERE sync_status = 'SYNCING' AND claimed_at > :now + (:now - :staleBefore)
   488	    """)
   489	    suspend fun normalizarClaimsFuturos(now: Long, staleBefore: Long)
   490	
   491	    /** Incluye ventas locales: el worker las sube con su pedido antes de registrar el pago. */
   492	    @Query("""
   493	        UPDATE pending_payments
   494	        SET sync_status = 'SYNCING', claim_token = :token, claimed_at = :now,
   495	            acknowledged = 0, acknowledged_by = NULL, acknowledged_at = NULL
   496	        WHERE id IN (
   497	            SELECT id FROM pending_payments
   498	            WHERE (sync_status = 'PENDING'
   499	               OR (sync_status = 'SYNCING' AND claimed_at IS NOT NULL AND claimed_at < :staleBefore))
   500	            ORDER BY created_at ASC, id ASC
   501	            LIMIT :limit
   502	        )
   503	    """)
   504	    suspend fun markClaimed(limit: Int, token: String, now: Long, staleBefore: Long)
   505	
   506	    @Query("SELECT * FROM pending_payments WHERE claim_token = :token ORDER BY created_at ASC, id ASC")
   507	    suspend fun getClaimed(token: String): List<PendingPaymentEntity>
   508	
   509	    /**
   510	     * Suelta una fila reclamada y la devuelve a PENDING con el retry incrementado
   511	     * (o FAILED si llegó al tope) — compare-and-swap on [token] (F-8, Fix round 1).
   512	     *
   513	     * **Por qué CAS:** sin el candado de `claim_token`, un worker que se queda pasmado más
   514	     * allá del umbral de stale-claim puede terminar tarde y soltar una fila que un segundo
   515	     * worker ya reclamó y está registrando activamente — le borraría el claim en pleno
   516	     * vuelo. Un tercer claim podría entonces tomar esa fila junto con el segundo worker:
   517	     * dos workers registrando el mismo pago ya cobrado. Afecta 0 filas cuando [token] ya
   518	     * no coincide (otro worker la reclamó primero); el llamador NUNCA debe reintentar ese
   519	     * write — la fila ya no es suya.
   520	     *
   521	     * @param token El claim_token con el que este worker obtuvo la fila (de claimBatch)
   522	     * @return Filas afectadas (0 = otro worker ya reclamó esta fila)
   523	     */
   524	    @Query("""
   525	        UPDATE pending_payments
   526	        SET retry_count = :retryCount,
   527	            last_error = :error,
   528	            claim_token = NULL,
   529	            claimed_at = NULL,
   530	            sync_status = CASE
   531	                WHEN :retryCount >= ${PendingPaymentEntity.MAX_RETRY_ATTEMPTS} THEN 'FAILED'
   532	                ELSE 'PENDING'
   533	            END
   534	        WHERE id = :id AND claim_token = :token
   535	    """)
   536	    suspend fun release(id: Long, token: String, retryCount: Int, error: String): Int
   537	
   115	                (existing?.syncStatus == PendingPaymentEntity.SYNC_STATUS_PENDING ||
   116	                    existing?.syncStatus == PendingPaymentEntity.SYNC_STATUS_SYNCING)
   117	
   118	            if (stillQueued) {
   119	                Timber.w("⚠️ [Payment Queue] Pago ya encolado | ref=${payment.referenceNumber}")
   120	                Result.success(Unit)
   121	            } else {
   122	                // 🔴 Fila SUCCESS/FAILED vieja, de OTRO venue, o de OTRO pago (idempotencyKey
   123	                // distinta) bloquea el índice: este pago NO entró. Reportarlo como fallo es
   124	                // lo que hace que el cajero vea el aviso rojo real ("avisa al supervisor")
   125	                // en vez del falso "EN COLA".
   126	                Timber.e(
   127	                    "❌ [Payment Queue] Encolado BLOQUEADO por fila previa (%s) | ref=%s venue=%s",
   128	                    existing?.syncStatus ?: "de otro venue/pago",
   129	                    payment.referenceNumber,
   130	                    payment.venueId,
   131	                )
   132	                Result.failure(
   133	                    IllegalStateException(
   134	                        "El pago no pudo encolarse: ya existe una fila ${existing?.syncStatus ?: "de otro venue/pago"} " +
   135	                            "con reference ${payment.referenceNumber}",
   136	                    ),
   137	                )
   138	            }
   139	        } catch (e: Exception) {
   140	            Timber.e(e, "❌ [Payment Queue] Failed to enqueue payment")
   141	            Result.failure(e)
   142	        }
   143	    }
   144	
   145	    override suspend fun enqueueAndClaim(payment: QueuedPayment): Result<QueuedPayment> =
   146	        withContext(NonCancellable + Dispatchers.IO) {
   147	            try {
   148	                require(!payment.idempotencyKey.isNullOrBlank()) { "El efectivo necesita su llave de reintento" }
   149	                val token = java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString()
   150	                val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
   151	                val entity = payment.toEntity().copy(
   152	                    syncStatus = PendingPaymentEntity.SYNC_STATUS_SYNCING,
   153	                    claimToken = token,
   154	                    claimedAt = now,
   155	                )
   156	                val id = pendingPaymentDao.insert(entity)
   157	                check(id > 0) { "El pago ya está guardado; no se enviará otra vez desde esta pantalla" }
   158	                Result.success(entity.copy(id = id).toQueuedPayment())
   159	            } catch (e: Exception) {
   160	                Result.failure(e)
   161	            }
   162	        }
   163	
   164	    override suspend fun getAllPending(): List<QueuedPayment> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
   165	        try {
   166	            val entities = pendingPaymentDao.getAllPending()
   167	            Timber.d("📋 [Payment Queue] Fetched ${entities.size} pending payments")
   168	            entities.map { it.toQueuedPayment() }
   169	        } catch (e: Exception) {
   170	            Timber.e(e, "❌ [Payment Queue] Failed to fetch pending payments")
   171	            emptyList()
   172	        }
   173	    }
   174	
   175	    override suspend fun markSynced(queueId: Long, token: String): Int = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
   176	        try {
   177	            val affected = pendingPaymentDao.markSynced(queueId, token)
   178	            if (affected > 0) {
   179	                Timber.i("✅ [Payment Queue] Payment marked as synced | queueId=$queueId")
   180	            } else {
   181	                // 🔒 CAS miss (F-8, Fix round 1): another worker reclaimed this row after our
   182	                // claim went stale. The backend call that got us here already succeeded — this
   183	                // is lost local bookkeeping, not a failed sync. Caller must not retry the write.
   184	                Timber.w("⚠️ [Payment Queue] markSynced() no afectó filas (claim reclamado por otro worker) | queueId=$queueId")
   185	            }
   186	            affected
   187	        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
   188	            // 🔴 F-9, Fix round 2 (pre-existing, not introduced by round 1): CancellationException
   189	            // IS a RuntimeException/Exception in Kotlin — a bare `catch (e: Exception)` below
   190	            // would swallow worker cancellation and return 0 instead of letting it propagate to
   191	            // doWork()'s `catch (e: CancellationException) { throw e }`. A cancelled worker must
   192	            // leave this row claimed for the stale sweep, never keep writing/looping. Rethrow
   193	            // BEFORE the generic catch.
   194	            throw e
   195	        } catch (e: Exception) {
   196	            Timber.e(e, "❌ [Payment Queue] Failed to mark payment as synced")
   197	            0
   198	        }
   199	    }
   290	            }
   291	        }
   292	
   293	    override suspend fun claimBatch(limit: Int): List<QueuedPayment> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
   294	        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
   295	        pendingPaymentDao
   296	            .claimBatch(
   297	                limit = limit,
   298	                token = java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
   299	                now = now,
   300	                staleBefore = now - STALE_CLAIM_MS,
   301	            )
   302	            .map { it.toQueuedPayment() }
   303	    }
   304	
   305	    override suspend fun release(queueId: Long, token: String, retryCount: Int, error: String): Int =
   306	        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
   307	            try {
   308	                pendingPaymentDao.release(queueId, token, retryCount, error)
   309	            } catch (e: CancellationException) {
   310	                // 🔴 F-9, Fix round 2: introduced by Fix round 1's own try/catch below —
   311	                // CancellationException IS a RuntimeException/Exception in Kotlin, so
   312	                // `catch (e: Exception)` alone would swallow worker cancellation and return 0
   313	                // instead of propagating. Before round 1, release() had NO catch at all, so
   314	                // cancellation reached doWork()'s `catch (e: CancellationException) { throw e }`
   315	                // untouched — a cancelled worker must NEVER keep writing into the next payment of
   316	                // the batch. Rethrow BEFORE the generic catch. (D7: the claims it leaves are
   317	                // released by PaymentSyncWorker.soltarSinTerminar() under NonCancellable, so this
   318	                // path is only reached by a cancellation that arrives mid-write.)
   319	                throw e
   320	            } catch (e: Exception) {
   321	                // 🔒 F-9, Fix round 1 (Important 1): sin este try/catch, un throw aquí (disco
   322	                // lleno, DB cerrada durante teardown) escapaba de releaseClaim → syncPayment →
   323	                // el for (payment in batch) de doWork() → el catch genérico → Result.retry(),
   324	                // abandonando el resto de la tanda YA reclamada (hasta 9 pagos ya cobrados)
   325	                // en SYNCING hasta que venciera STALE_CLAIM_MS (15 min). markSynced ya tenía
   326	                // este mismo try/catch (aunque con el mismo bug de CancellationException,
   327	                // arreglado arriba en la misma ronda) — release() era el único write sin ÉL.
   328	                Timber.e(e, "❌ [Payment Queue] release() falló | queueId=$queueId")
   329	                0

   154	        /**
   155	         * Minimum gap between successive `recordBackendUnreachable` non-fatals so
   156	         * one extended outage doesn't spam Crashlytics. We get one event per real
   157	         * incident; the gap_ms key on each event tells the story of how long the
   158	         * banner stayed up.
   159	         */
   160	        const val UNREACHABLE_NONFATAL_COOLDOWN_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000L
   161	    }

   163	    // Anti-stale guard: monotonic counter to prevent race conditions between
   164	    // concurrent callers (monitoring loop, network observer, forceCheck).
   165	    // Accessed only from Main dispatcher — no AtomicInteger needed.
   166	    private var checkVersion = 0
   167	    private fun nextVersion(): Int = ++checkVersion
   168	    private fun isLatest(version: Int): Boolean = version == checkVersion

   180	        // Send immediate heartbeat when app starts/restarts
   181	        // This ensures dashboard knows terminal is back online quickly after RESTART command
   182	        // The startMonitoring() function calls checkConnection() BEFORE any delay
   183	        Timber.i("🚀 [Connection] ViewModel initialized - sending immediate heartbeat")
   184	        startMonitoring()
   185	        observeNetworkChanges()
   186	    }

   189	    // PUBLIC METHODS

   192	    /**
   193	     * Start connection monitoring
   194	     *
   195	     * Runs in background, checking connectivity every 30 seconds.
   196	     * Uses exponential backoff when disconnected.
   197	     */
   198	    fun startMonitoring() {
   199	        Timber.d("🌐 [Connection] Starting monitoring")
   200	
   201	        monitoringJob?.cancel()
   202	        monitoringJob = viewModelScope.launch {
   203	            while (true) {
   204	                val version = nextVersion()
   205	                performFullHeartbeat(version)
   206	                evaluateCellularFailoverSafely(source = "monitoring_loop")
   207	
   208	                // Adaptive interval based on connection state
   209	                val interval = when (_state.value) {
   210	                    is ConnectionState.Connected -> 30_000L // 30s when connected
   211	                    is ConnectionState.DisconnectedNoInternet,
   212	                    is ConnectionState.DisconnectedServerDown -> calculateBackoffDelay() // Exponential backoff
   213	                    is ConnectionState.Reconnecting -> 5_000L // 5s when reconnecting
   214	                    else -> 10_000L // 10s default
   215	                }
   216	
   217	                delay(interval)
   218	            }
   219	        }
   220	    }
   221	
   222	    /**
   223	     * Stop connection monitoring
   224	     *
   225	     * Called when app is destroyed or user logs out.
   226	     */
   227	    fun stopMonitoring() {
   228	        Timber.d("🌐 [Connection] Stopping monitoring")
   229	        monitoringJob?.cancel()
   230	        monitoringJob = null
   231	    }
   232	
   233	    /**
   234	     * Force immediate connection check
   235	     *
   236	     * Used when user manually triggers refresh or returns to app.
   237	     */
   238	    fun forceCheck() {
   239	        viewModelScope.launch {
   240	            isDismissed = false  // Clear dismissed state when manually retrying
   241	            _state.value = ConnectionState.Reconnecting  // Immediate visual feedback
   242	            val version = nextVersion()
   243	            probeConnectivity(version)
   244	            evaluateCellularFailoverSafely(source = "force_check")
   245	        }
   246	    }
