   249	     * Dismiss the connection banner temporarily
   250	     *
   251	     * User can dismiss the offline banner if they want to work without distractions.
   252	     * Banner will reappear if connection state changes again.
   253	     */
   254	    fun dismissBanner() {
   278	     * This gives WiFi/DNS time to fully initialize after screen wake.
   279	     */
   280	    private fun observeNetworkChanges() {
   281	        networkObserverJob?.cancel()
   282	        networkObserverJob = viewModelScope.launch {
   283	            connectivityObserver.observe().collect { status ->
   284	                when (status) {
   285	                    NetworkStatus.Available -> {
   286	                        Timber.i("🌐 [Connection] Network restored - checking after stabilization")
   287	                        cancelOfflineTransition()
   288	                        // Grace period: Wait for network to fully stabilize after wake (DNS/DHCP)
   289	                        delay(ONLINE_STABILIZATION_MS)
   290	                        // Clear dismissed state - network change is a new situation
   291	                        isDismissed = false
   292	                        val version = nextVersion()
   293	                        probeConnectivity(version)
   294	                        evaluateCellularFailoverSafely(source = "network_available")
   295	
   296	                        // NOTE: SDK re-init on network restore is handled by HomeViewModel.listenForConnectionRestored()
   297	                        // which passes the correct merchantPosId. Do NOT call ensureInitialized() here
   298	                        // without posId — it causes a race condition that leaves the SDK with stale posId.
   299	                    }
   300	                    NetworkStatus.Unavailable -> {
   301	                        Timber.w("⚠️ [Connection] Network lost — scheduling offline transition")
   302	                        scheduleOfflineTransition("observer")
   303	                        evaluateCellularFailoverSafely(source = "network_unavailable")
   304	                    }
   305	                }
   306	            }
   307	        }
   308	    }
   309	
   310	    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   311	    // OFFLINE HYSTERESIS (Asymmetric: slow offline, fast online)
   312	    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   313	
   314	    /**
   315	     * Schedule a delayed transition to DisconnectedNoInternet.
   316	     *
   317	     * Absorbs transient network flickers (screen lock/unlock, WiFi roaming).
   318	     * After [OFFLINE_GRACE_MS], re-checks the network to avoid stale transitions.
   319	     * If network recovered during the grace period, the transition is skipped.
   320	     *
   321	     * @param source Logging label for the caller (observer, probe, heartbeat)
   322	     */
   323	    private fun scheduleOfflineTransition(source: String) {
   324	        if (offlineTransitionJob?.isActive == true) return // Already scheduled
   325	
   326	        offlineTransitionJob = viewModelScope.launch {
   327	            Timber.d("⏳ [Connection] Offline grace period started ($source)")
   328	            delay(OFFLINE_GRACE_MS)
   329	
   330	            // Re-validate: network may have recovered during grace period
   331	            val networkInfo = networkMonitor.getCurrentNetworkInfo()
   332	            if (!networkInfo.isConnected) {
   333	                Timber.w("⚠️ [Connection] Confirmed offline after grace period ($source)")
   334	                if (!isDismissed) _state.value = ConnectionState.DisconnectedNoInternet
   335	                connectionStateManager.setInternetConnected(false)
   336	                reconnectionAttempts++
   337	            } else {
   338	                Timber.i("🌐 [Connection] Network recovered during grace period ($source)")
   339	            }
   340	        }
   341	    }
   342	
   343	    private fun cancelOfflineTransition() {
   344	        offlineTransitionJob?.cancel()
   345	        offlineTransitionJob = null
   346	    }
   347	
   348	    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   349	    // CONNECTIVITY CHECKS (Split: UI probe vs full heartbeat)
   350	    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   351	
   352	    /**
   353	     * Fast UI-only connectivity probe
   354	     *
   355	     * Used by forceCheck (Reintentar) and network observer.
   356	     * Sends a heartbeat with an 8s timeout for UI responsiveness.
   357	     * Does NOT process pending commands — that's the monitoring loop's job.
   358	     *
   359	     * @param version Anti-stale guard — side effects skipped if superseded
   360	     */
   361	    private suspend fun probeConnectivity(version: Int) {
   362	        if (!deviceInfoManager.isDeviceActivated()) {
   363	            if (isLatest(version)) _state.value = ConnectionState.Connected
   364	            return
   365	        }
   366	
   367	        val networkInfo = networkMonitor.getCurrentNetworkInfo()
   368	        if (!networkInfo.isConnected) {
   369	            if (isLatest(version)) {
   370	                scheduleOfflineTransition("probe")
   371	            }
   372	            return
   373	        }
   374	
   375	        try {
   376	            Timber.d("🌐 [Connection] Probe: checking backend connectivity...")
   377	
   378	            if (isLatest(version)) {
   379	                val wasDisconnected = _state.value is ConnectionState.DisconnectedNoInternet ||
   380	                        _state.value is ConnectionState.DisconnectedServerDown
   381	                if (wasDisconnected) _state.value = ConnectionState.Reconnecting
   382	            }
   383	
   384	            val heartbeat = buildLightweightHeartbeat()
   385	            val startMs = System.currentTimeMillis()
   386	
   387	            // Short timeout for UI responsiveness — don't wait the full 30s OkHttp timeout
   388	            val result = withTimeout(8_000) {
   389	                heartbeatRepository.sendHeartbeat(heartbeat)
   390	            }
   391	            val latencyMs = System.currentTimeMillis() - startMs
   392	
   393	            // After suspension point — re-check version before writing ANY side effect
   394	            if (!isLatest(version)) return
   395	
   396	            when (result) {
   397	                is Result.Success -> {
   398	                    Timber.i("✅ [Connection] Probe OK (latency=${latencyMs}ms)")
   399	                    cancelOfflineTransition()
   400	                    markServerProbeSucceeded()
   401	                    connectionStateManager.updateState(hasInternet = true, hasServer = true, latencyMs = latencyMs)
   402	                    handleReconnectionSuccess(version)
   403	
   404	                    // Process pending commands — critical for offline→online transition
   405	                    // Backend marks commands as SENT when included in ANY heartbeat response,
   406	                    // so we must process them here or they'll be lost (never re-delivered)
   407	                    val pendingCommands = result.data.pendingCommands
   408	                    if (!pendingCommands.isNullOrEmpty()) {
   409	                        Timber.i("📥 [Connection] Probe received ${pendingCommands.size} pending command(s)")
   410	                        processPendingCommands(pendingCommands)
   411	                    }
   412	                }
   413	                is Result.Error -> {
   414	                    Timber.w("⚠️ [Connection] Probe: backend unreachable: ${result.exception?.message}")
   415	                    markServerProbeFailed(source = "probe", cause = result.exception)
   416	                }
   417	            }
   418	        } catch (e: TimeoutCancellationException) {
   419	            if (!isLatest(version)) return
   420	            Timber.w("⚠️ [Connection] Probe timed out (8s)")
   421	            markServerProbeFailed(source = "probe_timeout", cause = e)
   422	        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
   423	            throw e  // Coroutine cancelled — rethrow, don't set error state
   424	        } catch (e: Exception) {
   425	            if (!isLatest(version)) return
   426	            Timber.e(e, "❌ [Connection] Probe failed")
   427	            markServerProbeFailed(source = "probe_exception", cause = e)
   428	        }
   429	    }
   430	
   431	    /**
   432	     * Full heartbeat with command processing
   433	     *
   434	     * Used ONLY by the monitoring loop. Sends heartbeat (full OkHttp timeout),
   435	     * updates UI state, AND processes pending commands from the response.
   436	     *
   437	     * @param version Anti-stale guard — UI side effects skipped if superseded.
   438	     *                Commands ALWAYS execute regardless of staleness.
   439	     */
   440	    private suspend fun performFullHeartbeat(version: Int) {
   441	        if (!deviceInfoManager.isDeviceActivated()) {
   442	            if (isLatest(version)) _state.value = ConnectionState.Connected
   443	            return
   444	        }
   445	
   446	        val networkInfo = networkMonitor.getCurrentNetworkInfo()
   447	        if (!networkInfo.isConnected) {
   448	            if (isLatest(version)) {
   449	                scheduleOfflineTransition("heartbeat")
   450	            }
   451	            return
   452	        }
   453	
   454	        try {
   455	            Timber.d("🌐 [Connection] Heartbeat: checking backend connectivity...")
   456	
   457	            if (isLatest(version)) {
   458	                if (_state.value is ConnectionState.DisconnectedNoInternet || _state.value is ConnectionState.DisconnectedServerDown) {
   459	                    _state.value = ConnectionState.Reconnecting
   460	                }
   461	            }
   462	
   463	            val heartbeat = buildLightweightHeartbeat()
   464	            val heartbeatStartMs = System.currentTimeMillis()
   465	            val result = heartbeatRepository.sendHeartbeat(heartbeat)
   466	            val latencyMs = System.currentTimeMillis() - heartbeatStartMs
   467	
   468	            when (result) {
   469	                is Result.Success -> {
   470	                    Timber.i("✅ [Connection] Heartbeat OK (latency=${latencyMs}ms)")
   471	
   472	                    // UI state — gated by version
   473	                    if (isLatest(version)) {
   474	                        cancelOfflineTransition()
   475	                        markServerProbeSucceeded()
   476	                        connectionStateManager.updateState(hasInternet = true, hasServer = true, latencyMs = latencyMs)
   477	                        handleReconnectionSuccess(version)
   478	                    }
   479	
   480	                    // Commands — ALWAYS processed, never skipped by stale guard
   481	                    val pendingCommands = result.data.pendingCommands
   482	                    if (!pendingCommands.isNullOrEmpty()) {
   483	                        Timber.i("📥 [Connection] Received ${pendingCommands.size} pending command(s)")
   484	                        processPendingCommands(pendingCommands)
   485	                    }
   486	                }
   487	                is Result.Error -> {
   488	                    Timber.w("⚠️ [Connection] Heartbeat: backend unreachable: ${result.exception?.message}")
   489	                    if (isLatest(version)) {
   490	                        markServerProbeFailed(source = "heartbeat", cause = result.exception)
   491	                    }
   492	                }
   493	            }
   494	        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
   495	            throw e
   496	        } catch (e: Exception) {
   497	            Timber.e(e, "❌ [Connection] Heartbeat failed")
   498	            if (isLatest(version)) {
   499	                markServerProbeFailed(source = "heartbeat_exception", cause = e)
   500	            }
   501	        }
   502	    }
   503	
   504	    /**
   505	     * Mark a server probe as failed.
   506	     *
   507	     * 🛡️ Centralizes the "we couldn't reach the backend" handling for both
   508	     * [probeConnectivity] and [performFullHeartbeat]. Implements the hysteresis
   518	    private fun markServerProbeFailed(source: String, cause: Throwable? = null) {
   519	        ultimaCausaFallo = generateSequence(cause) { it.cause }.lastOrNull()?.javaClass?.simpleName ?: source
   520	        consecutiveServerProbeFailures++
   521	        val gap = System.currentTimeMillis() - lastSuccessfulProbeAt
   522	
   523	        Timber.w(
   524	            "⚠️ [Connection] Server probe failed ($source) — streak=$consecutiveServerProbeFailures, gap=${gap}ms"
   525	        )
   526	        CrashlyticsContext.logNetworkBreadcrumb(
   527	            "probe failed: source=$source streak=$consecutiveServerProbeFailures gap=${gap}ms"
   528	        )
   529	
   530	        // Below threshold → keep showing Connected/banner, give the network a
   531	        // chance to recover before painting the red banner. This filters out
   532	        // single-failure noise from HTTP/2 stale streams that pingInterval will
   533	        // evict within 15s.
   534	        if (consecutiveServerProbeFailures < SERVER_DOWN_FAILURE_THRESHOLD) {
   535	            // 🛡️ If the UI is currently on a transient state (Reconnecting from
   536	            // forceCheck / post-disconnect probe, or Checking from app init), it
   537	            // would otherwise stay stuck on the spinner until the next monitoring
   538	            // loop iteration (5-30s). Revert to the last confirmed terminal state
   539	            // so the user sees a definitive UI.
   540	            if (_state.value is ConnectionState.Reconnecting ||
   541	                _state.value is ConnectionState.Checking
   542	            ) {
   543	                _state.value = lastConfirmedConnectionState
   544	            }
   545	            // Schedule a fast follow-up probe so we hit SERVER_DOWN_FAILURE_THRESHOLD
   546	            // within ~2s instead of waiting the regular 5-30s monitoring backoff.
   547	            // Critical for forceCheck UX: without this, "Reintentar" gives no
   548	            // definitive feedback for up to 30s after a single failed probe.
   549	            scheduleFastReprobe(reason = source)
   550	            return
   551	        }
   552	
   553	        // At/over threshold → declare server-down (banner shows)
   554	        fastReprobeJob?.cancel()
   555	        fastReprobeJob = null
   556	        cancelOfflineTransition() // Internet confirmed — cancel pending NoInternet
   557	        if (!isDismissed) _state.value = ConnectionState.DisconnectedServerDown
   558	        lastConfirmedConnectionState = ConnectionState.DisconnectedServerDown
   559	        connectionStateManager.updateState(hasInternet = true, hasServer = false)
   560	        reconnectionAttempts++
   561	
   562	        // Single-shot non-fatal per incident (cooldown UNREACHABLE_NONFATAL_COOLDOWN_MS)
   563	        // First time: lastUnreachableNonFatalAt == 0 → fires immediately
   564	        // Repeat firings only after cooldown to avoid spam during a long outage
   565	        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
   566	        if (now - lastUnreachableNonFatalAt > UNREACHABLE_NONFATAL_COOLDOWN_MS) {
   567	            CrashlyticsContext.recordBackendUnreachable(
   568	                gapSinceLastSuccessMs = gap,
   569	                cause = cause,
   570	                source = source,
   571	            )
   572	            lastUnreachableNonFatalAt = now
   573	        }
   574	    }
   575	
   576	    /**
   577	     * Mark a server probe as successful — resets hysteresis state.
   578	     */
   579	    private fun markServerProbeSucceeded() {
   580	        // Cancel any pending fast re-probe — we're already healthy, no need to retry
   581	        fastReprobeJob?.cancel()
   582	        fastReprobeJob = null
   583	
   584	        if (consecutiveServerProbeFailures > 0) {
   585	            CrashlyticsContext.logNetworkBreadcrumb(
   586	                "probe recovered after $consecutiveServerProbeFailures failures"
   587	            )
   588	        }
   589	        consecutiveServerProbeFailures = 0
   590	        lastSuccessfulProbeAt = System.currentTimeMillis()
   591	        lastConfirmedConnectionState = ConnectionState.Connected
   592	        // Allow a fresh non-fatal next time we go down
   593	        lastUnreachableNonFatalAt = 0L
   594	    }
   595	
   596	    /**
   597	     * Schedule a follow-up probe ~2s after a below-threshold failure.
   598	     *
   599	     * Without this, a user tapping "Reintentar" while seeing the offline banner
   600	     * would have to wait 5-30s for the next monitoring loop iteration to confirm
   601	     * whether the network is actually still down. With it, the second
   602	     * confirmation arrives within ~2s and the banner stabilizes (either back to
   603	     * Connected if recovered, or to DisconnectedServerDown if truly down).
   604	     */
   605	    private fun scheduleFastReprobe(reason: String) {
   606	        // Idempotent — multiple failures in quick succession share one re-probe
   607	        if (fastReprobeJob?.isActive == true) return
   608	        fastReprobeJob = viewModelScope.launch {
   609	            delay(2_000)
   610	            // Bail out if state was resolved by another path during the wait
   611	            if (consecutiveServerProbeFailures == 0) return@launch
   612	            if (_state.value is ConnectionState.DisconnectedServerDown) return@launch
   613	
   614	            Timber.d("⏱️ [Connection] Fast re-probe (reason=$reason) — confirming below-threshold failure")
   615	            CrashlyticsContext.logNetworkBreadcrumb("fast re-probe firing (reason=$reason)")
   616	            val version = nextVersion()
   617	            probeConnectivity(version)
   618	        }
   619	    }
   620	
   621	    /**
   622	     * Handle successful reconnection — show Reconnected banner briefly
   623	     *
   624	     * Shared by probeConnectivity and performFullHeartbeat.
   625	     * Cancels any previous banner timer to avoid duplicates.
   626	     *
   627	     * @param version Anti-stale guard
   628	     */
   629	    private suspend fun handleReconnectionSuccess(version: Int) {
   630	        if (!isLatest(version)) return
   631	
   632	        if (reconnectionAttempts > 0) {
   633	            viewModelScope.launch { commandExecutor.recoverPending() }
   634	            // Refresh paid access after recovery without delaying the connection banner or checkout.
   635	            viewModelScope.launch {
   636	                tpvSettingsRepository.refreshFromTerminalConfig(deviceInfoManager.getSerialNumber())
   637	            }
   638	            Timber.i("🔄 [Connection] Connection restored after $reconnectionAttempts attempts")
   639	
   640	            connectionEventManager.emitConnectionRestored(
   641	                attemptsBeforeReconnection = reconnectionAttempts
   642	            )
   643	
   644	            // Re-check after suspension point (emitConnectionRestored may suspend)
   645	            if (!isLatest(version)) return
   646	
   647	            _state.value = ConnectionState.Reconnected
   648	
   649	            // Cancel previous banner timer to avoid duplicate transitions
   650	            reconnectedBannerJob?.cancel()
   651	            reconnectedBannerJob = viewModelScope.launch {
   652	                delay(2000)
   653	                if (_state.value is ConnectionState.Reconnected) {
   654	                    _state.value = ConnectionState.Connected
   655	                }
   656	            }
   657	
   658	            reconnectionAttempts = 0
   659	        } else {
   660	            _state.value = ConnectionState.Connected
   661	        }
   662	    }

   963	    internal suspend fun handleRecordFailure(
   964	        paymentLabel: String,
   965	        context: PaymentContext.AngelPayPayment,
   966	        error: Throwable,
   967	    ): AngelPayPaymentState {
   968	        // 📡 POS→TPV: the card WAS charged (money moved) — the backend record merely failed and is
   969	        // being enqueued for offline sync. Report SUCCESS to the POS now (founder rule 2026-07:
   970	        // money moved ⇒ success). No paymentId yet; the synced REST record reconciles the row later.
   971	        // No-op unless socket-sourced.
   972	        // La aprobación bancaria ya ocurrió en tarjeta. El efectivo, que no tiene ese
   973	        // comprobante, sólo se confirma al POS cuando su fila quedó guardada.
   974	        val isCash = context.cardDetails?.isCash == true
   975	        if (isCash && (com.jaac.avoqado_tpv.features.payment.domain.usecase.CashAdmissionRejectedException.identifica(error) ||
   976	                        error is com.jaac.avoqado_tpv.features.payment.domain.usecase.CashOwnerReviewRequiredException)) {
   977	            return AngelPayPaymentState.Error(if (error is com.jaac.avoqado_tpv.features.payment.domain.usecase.CashOwnerReviewRequiredException)
   978	                error.message ?: "Consulta la venta guardada." else "No se guardó el cobro: ${error.message}", canRetry = false)
   979	        }
   980	        if (!isCash) emitSocketResultIfSocketSourced(status = "success")
   981	
   982	        val queued = QueuedPayment(
   983	            // UNIQUE column in Room — AngelPay can return a blank reference on edge
   984	            // paths, so fall back to the attempt UUID to keep the row insertable.

   987	            },
   988	            venueId = context.venueId,
   989	            staffId = context.staffId,

   991	            tip = context.tip,
   992	            rating = context.rating,

   994	            blumonSerialNumber = "", // N/A — AngelPay row
   995	            deviceSerialNumber = context.deviceSerialNumber,
   996	            maskedPan = context.cardDetails?.maskedPan,
   997	            cardBrand = context.cardDetails?.cardBrand?.name,
   998	            entryMode = context.cardDetails?.entryMode?.name ?: CardEntryMode.OTHER.name,
   999	            isInternational = context.cardDetails?.isInternational ?: false,
  1000	            cardNature = context.cardDetails?.naturalezaParaLaCola(), // D5: el replay manda el mismo método
  1001	            authorizationNumber = context.authorizationCode,
  1002	            idempotencyKey = context.idempotencyKey,
  1003	            processor = ProcessorType.ANGELPAY,
  1004	            // 📡 Carry the arbitration link through the queue — the replayed record is what
  1005	            // closes the terminal's TerminalPaymentRequest row (the watchdog can't reconcile
  1006	            // a fast payment: its row has no orderId).
  1007	            terminalPaymentRequestId = context.terminalPaymentRequestId,
  1008	            orderId = context.orderId,

  1019	        return if (enqueueResult.isSuccess) {
  1020	            if (isCash) emitSocketResultIfSocketSourced(status = "success")
  1021	            // 📒 [Libreta] ENTREGADA_A_COLA — pending_payments owns the money now (its own
  1022	            // idempotency + retry); the ledger row rests. For the cash path (no ledger row
  1023	            // is opened for cash) this is a CAS no-match no-op inside the ledger.

  1037	                    "error" to (error.message ?: "unknown"),
  1038	                ),
  1039	            )
  1040	            // Best-effort immediate kick — the 15-min periodic worker is the guarantee.
  1041	            runCatching { PaymentSyncScheduler.runNow(appContext) }
  1042	            // 🟢 F-1 fix (spec §4.2): money moved AND it's safely queued — this is a
  1043	            // SUCCESS with a caveat, not an Error. Was AngelPayPaymentState.Error before;
  1044	            // the cashier saw a red screen on a charge that had already succeeded and
  1045	            // recharged the customer out of fear. See AngelPayPaymentState.Queued kdoc.

  1054	                orderNumber = queued.orderNumber,

  1056	            )
  1057	        } else {
  1058	            val enqueueError = enqueueResult.exceptionOrNull()

  1061	            // supervisor sees, so an index-blocked enqueue (F-7) is distinguishable from a
  1062	            // plain backend outage. Additive — backendRecordFailureState's signature is
  1063	            // untouched; the original backend error is preserved as `cause` for Crashlytics.
  1064	            val combinedError = enqueueError?.message?.let { enqueueDetail ->
  1065	                Exception(

  2891	
  2892	    fun startCashPayment() {
  2893	        val seleccionAlCobrar = _state.value as? AngelPayPaymentState.SelectingMerchant
  2894	        if (soloEfectivo && seleccionAlCobrar == null) return
  2895	        val ventaAlCobrar = ventaLocal
  2896	        val attemptId = ventaAlCobrar?.llave ?: ensurePaymentAttemptId()
  2897	        val referencia = referenciaDeEfectivo(attemptId)

  2899	        confirmedNegativeOutcome = false
  2900	        confirmedNegativeEvidence = null
  2901	        // 🔴 Se toma SÍNCRONO, antes de lanzar: un candado DENTRO del `launch` no protegería de
  2902	        // un segundo toque que entre mientras el primero está suspendido. Ver [cobroEnEfectivoEnVuelo].
  2903	        if (!cobroEnEfectivoEnVuelo.compareAndSet(false, true)) {
  2904	            Timber.w("🔶 [AngelPay] Toque ignorado: ya hay un cobro en efectivo en vuelo")
  2905	            return
  2906	        }
  2907	        viewModelScope.launch {
  2908	            try {
  2909	                // 🛑 C.5: el efectivo de un cobro del POS se ancla ANTES de registrar. Si el cancel ya ganó,
  2910	                // aquí se para: registrar sería cobrar algo que el servidor dio por cancelado.
  2911	                if (!puedeArrancarEfectivoOCripto()) return@launch
  2912	                if (ventaAlCobrar != null) {

  2916	                    }
  2917	                    return@launch
  2918	                }
  2919	                _state.value = AngelPayPaymentState.ProcessingCash()
  2920	
  2921	                com.jaac.avoqado_tpv.core.observability.CrashlyticsContext.setPaymentContext(
  2922	                    processor = "ANGELPAY",
  2923	                    method = "CASH",
  2924	                    merchantId = _currentMerchant.value?.merchantAccountId,
  2925	                    amount = pendingAmount.add(pendingTip).toPlainString(),
  2926	                    orderId = pendingOrderId,
  2927	                    attemptId = currentPaymentAttemptId,
  2928	                )
  2929	
  2930	                // 💰 Money moved (cash collected). Recover venue/staff from the most reliable source so
  2931	                // this sale is ALWAYS recorded or enqueued — never dropped (cached → auth → secureStorage).
  2932	                val venueId = cachedVenueId ?: authRepository.getVenueId() ?: secureStorage.getVenueId()
  2933	                val staffId = cachedStaffId ?: authRepository.getStaffId() ?: secureStorage.getStaffId()
  2934	                if (venueId == null || staffId == null) {
  2935	                    _state.value = reportChargedButUnrecordable(
  2936	                        paymentLabel = "El pago en efectivo",
  2937	                        error = IllegalStateException("venue/staff no recuperable tras cobro en efectivo"),
  2938	                    )
  2939	                    return@launch
  2940	                }
  2941	
  2942	                val paymentContext = PaymentContext.AngelPayPayment(
  2943	                    venueId = venueId,
  2944	                    staffId = staffId,
  2945	                    shiftId = cachedShiftId,
  2946	                    amount = pendingAmount,
  2947	                    tip = pendingTip,
  2948	                    rating = pendingRating,
  2949	                    merchantAccountId = null, // Cash = no processor
  2950	                    deviceSerialNumber = TerminalConfig.serialNumber,
  2951	                    idempotencyKey = attemptId, // Misma llave capturada antes de cualquier suspensión.
  2952	                    terminalPaymentRequestId = _socketRequestId, // 📡 POS→TPV arbitration link (null unless socket-sourced)
  2953	                    cardDetails = CardDetails.CASH,
  2954	                    authorizationCode = "EFECTIVO",
  2955	                    referenceNumber = referencia,
  2956	                    orderId = pendingOrderId,
  2957	                    orderNumber = pendingOrderNumber,
  2958	                    // 📸 Serialized inventory (SIM) proof-of-sale — empty for a normal payment
  2959	                    isPortabilidad = pendingIsPortabilidad,
  2960	                    serialNumbers = pendingSerialNumbers,
  2961	                )
  2962	
  2963	                Timber.d("🔶 [AngelPay] Recording cash payment | amount=$pendingAmount, tip=$pendingTip")
  2964	
  2965	                // La cola se guarda ANTES del primer POST en RecordPaymentUseCase.
  2966	                // NonCancellable protege de abandonar la pantalla; si Android mata el
  2967	                // proceso, el worker recupera la fila durable al vencer su claim.
  2968	                val folioDelIntento = pendingFolio  // 🧾 P4: congelado antes del POST
  2969	                val recordResult = withContext(NonCancellable + Dispatchers.IO) {
  2970	                    recordPaymentUseCase(
  2971	                        context = paymentContext,
  2972	                        cardDetails = CardDetails.CASH,
  2973	                        authorizationNumber = "EFECTIVO",
  2974	                        referenceNumber = referencia,
  2975	                    )
  2976	                }
  2977	
  2978	                val successState = AngelPayPaymentState.Success(
  2979	                    authCode = "EFECTIVO",
  2980	                    amount = pendingAmount.toPlainString(),
  2981	                    tipAmount = if (pendingTip > BigDecimal.ZERO) pendingTip.toPlainString() else null,
  2982	                    referenceNumber = referencia,
  2983	                    orderId = pendingOrderId,
  2984	                    orderNumber = pendingOrderNumber,
  2985	                    isCash = true,
  2986	                )
  2987	
  2988	                recordResult.fold(
  2989	                    onSuccess = { receipt ->
  2990	                        Timber.i("🔶 [AngelPay] Cash payment recorded | receipt=${receipt.receiptUrl}")
  2991	                        // 🧾 P4: registrado ⇒ aceptar el folio y la comanda de apoyo, ANTES del recibo de siempre. No espera.
  2992	                        folioDelIntento?.let { despuesDeCobrarFolio?.alConfirmarse(it) }
  2993	                        _state.value = successState.copy(receipt = receipt)
  2994	                        // 📡 POS→TPV: report success to the caller (no-op unless socket-sourced).
  2995	                        emitSocketResultIfSocketSourced(
  2996	                            status = "success",
  2997	                            paymentId = receipt.paymentId,
  2998	                            transactionId = receipt.paymentId,
  2999	                            receiptUrl = receipt.receiptUrl,
  3000	                            receiptAccessKey = receipt.accessKey,
  3001	                        )
  3002	                    },
  3003	                    onFailure = { error ->
  3004	                        Timber.e(error, "🔶 [AngelPay] Cash payment failed to record to backend — enqueueing for sync")
  3005	                        // Cash rows are first-class queue citizens: QueuedPayment detects the
  3006	                        // CASH-*/EFECTIVO markers and replays with merchant=null + CardDetails.CASH.
  3007	                        _state.value = handleRecordFailure("El pago en efectivo", paymentContext, error)
  3008	                    },
  3009	                )
  3010	            } catch (e: CancellationException) {
  3011	                throw e
  3012	            } catch (e: Exception) {
  3013	                if (ventaAlCobrar == null) throw e
  3014	                if (ventaLocal === ventaAlCobrar) {
  3015	                    _state.value = AngelPayPaymentState.Error(
  3016	                        "No se pudo guardar la venta: ${e.message}. Regresa a Cobrar y pide apoyo al gerente.", canRetry = false,
  3017	                    )
  3018	                }
  3019	            } finally {
  3020	                // Un solo punto de salida: cubre también el `return@launch` de
  3021	                // «venue/staff no recuperable».
  3022	                cobroEnEfectivoEnVuelo.set(false)
  3023	            }
  3024	        }
  3025	    }

  5179	        )

  5196	            cardDetails = cardDetails,

  5244	
  5915	     * reliable maskedPan/brand. Mirrors the Blumon PaymentViewModel.
  5916	     *
  5917	     * ⚠️ De-duped via [_socketResultEmitted] — NOT by nulling [_socketRequestId]. The request id
  5918	     * must SURVIVE the emit because it is also the arbitration link threaded into the recorded
  5919	     * payment (terminalPaymentRequestId). Nulling it here silently broke retry-after-decline:
  5920	     * decline emits "failed" → cashier retries ON THE TERMINAL → card APPROVED → the recorded
  5921	     * payment carried a null link → the server could never reconcile the FAILED row to COMPLETED
  5922	     * and the 🚨 "money moved despite close" alert never fired. One emit per request, but the link
  5923	     * lives until [resetPayment].
  5924	     */
  5925	    private fun emitSocketResultIfSocketSourced(
  5926	        status: String,
  5927	        paymentId: String? = null,
  5928	        transactionId: String? = null,
  5929	        errorMessage: String? = null,
  5930	        receiptUrl: String? = null,
  5931	        receiptAccessKey: String? = null,
  5932	        outcomeEvidence: String? = null,
  5933	    ) {
  5934	        if (_paymentSource != "SOCKET") return
  5935	        val requestId = _socketRequestId ?: return
  5936	        if (_socketResultEmitted) {
  5937	            Timber.d("📡 [AngelPay Socket] Result already emitted for $requestId — skipping (status=$status)")
  5938	            return
  5939	        }
  5940	        // 🛑 El servidor sólo acredita `PROCESSOR_DECLINED` junto a `failed`: con `cancelled` lo degrada a
  5941	        // timeout, la fila queda UNKNOWN y la terminal y la tablet se quedan bloqueadas (P1, 11-sep).
  5942	        //
  5943	        // ⚠️ REDUNDANTE a propósito, y conviene saberlo antes de "limpiarla": los emisores reales de un
  5944	        // desenlace retenido ([emitirDeclinacionRetenida], el final pre-autorización) ya mandan `failed`,
  5945	        // así que hoy NINGÚN camino llega aquí con `cancelled` + `PROCESSOR_DECLINED`. Es la segunda capa
  5946	        // para el emisor que se escriba mañana. Medido el 11-sep rompiendo cada capa por separado: con una
  5947	        // sola rota las 98 pruebas siguen en VERDE (la otra tapa el hueco); rotas LAS DOS caen 6, entre
  5948	        // ellas «P1 el reloj de abandono de A nunca emite con el id de B». O sea: el invariante está
  5949	        // protegido, pero ninguna prueba distingue las capas — no la borres creyendo que alguna la cubre.
  5950	        val statusFinal = if (outcomeEvidence == "PROCESSOR_DECLINED" && status == "cancelled") "failed" else status
  5951	        socketManager.emitTerminalPaymentResult(
  5952	            requestId = requestId,
  5953	            status = statusFinal,
  5954	            paymentId = paymentId,
  5955	            transactionId = transactionId,
  5956	            cardDetails = null,
  5957	            errorMessage = errorMessage,
  5958	            receiptUrl = receiptUrl,
  5959	            receiptAccessKey = receiptAccessKey,
  5960	            outcomeEvidence = outcomeEvidence,
  5961	        )
  5962	        _socketResultEmitted = true
  5963	        Timber.i("📡 [AngelPay Socket] Emitted terminal:payment_result | status=$statusFinal | requestId=$requestId")
  5964	    }

  8399	    private suspend fun loadOrderData(orderId: String?): OrderData? {
  8400	        if (orderId == null) {
  8401	            Timber.d("📦 [Order Data] Skipping load - Fast payment (no order)")
  8402	            return null
  8403	        }
  8404

   902	     * Returns true on success (or no-op), false if the backend rejected
   903	     * persistence. The caller can decide whether to block payment on a
   904	     * failed capture; the recommendation is NOT to block (record locally
   905	     * and let the user finish the charge), since the discount was already
   906	     * applied to the cart.

   908	    suspend fun captureReferralOnPayment(orderId: String? = null): Boolean {
   909	        if (!connectionStateManager.hasInternet()) return false
   910	        val state = _referralValidation.value
   911	        if (state !is ReferralCaptureUiState.Valid) return true
   912	        val venueId = secureStorage.getVenueId() ?: return false
   913	        val customer = _selectedCustomer.value ?: return false
   914	        val staffVenueId = secureStorage.getStaffId() ?: return false
   915	        val code = _referralCode.value.trim()
   916	        if (code.isEmpty()) return false
   917	
   918	        val result = captureReferralUseCase(
   919	            venueId = venueId,
   920	            referralCode = code,
   921	            newCustomerId = customer.id,
   922	            capturedByStaffVenueId = staffVenueId,
   923	            intendedOrderId = orderId,
   924	        )
   925	        result.onFailure { e ->
   926	            // If the validation reason changed between validate + capture,
   927	            // surface it in the UI so the next attempt sees the new reason.
   928	            (e as? ReferralValidationException)?.let { ex ->

   535	     * tpv/venues/{venueId}/orders/{orderId}`, es territorio de "Cobrar" —
   536	     * intocable por regla dura del founder). En vez de eso, `PAY_CASH` viaja
   537	     * SIEMPRE como intent — online u offline, el mecanismo es el MISMO:
   538	     *
   539	     * 1. [SyncOutbox.enqueue] escribe el intent (write-ahead, mismo patrón que
   540	     *    [addItems]) — su `id` ES el `idempotencyKey` que el server usa tal
   541	     *    cual (`applyPayCash` → `payCashOrder({ idempotencyKey: intent.id })`
   542	     *    en `sync.mobile.service.ts`). Sin este id, un reintento de red
   543	     *    cobraría dos veces — regla dura de
   544	     *    `avoqado-server/.claude/rules/offline-first-y-hub-lan.md` §2.5.
   545	     * 2. [SyncOutbox.replayNow] intenta reproducir el outbox COMPLETO del
   546	     *    venue de inmediato (respeta el FIFO — si hay intents más viejos
   547	     *    pendientes, este PAY_CASH espera su turno, igual que le tocaría
   548	     *    esperar offline). Si hay red, el ack de ESTE intent llega en la misma
   549	     *    llamada. Si no hay red (o el server cortó el batch en un RETRY
   550	     *    anterior), `replayNow` regresa sin lanzar y sin ack — el intent queda
   551	     *    PENDING, se drena solo después.
   552	     *
   553	     * Nunca se llama [classifyTablesSyncFailure] aquí — `replayNow` ya absorbe
   554	     * la distinción red-caída-vs-servidor-respondió (nunca lanza salvo
   555	     * `CancellationException`) y el ack mismo (ACKED/REJECTED/RETRY) es la
   556	     * única fuente de verdad de negocio, aplicada por [SyncOutbox.applyAck].
   557	     *
   558	     * @param amount PESOS (major units) — la conversión a centavos ocurre
   559	     *   SOLO en el payload de este intent, porque el contrato del reducer
   560	     *   (`applyPayCash`) exige `amountCents`/`tipCents` enteros — ver
   561	     *   `avoqado-server/.claude/rules/critical-warnings.md` ("* 100 SOLO en un
   562	     *   boundary externo, luego inmediatamente de vuelta a pesos"). El
   563	     *   dominio (`OrderDetail`, `TableSession`, este ViewModel) sigue en
   564	     *   `BigDecimal` pesos en todo lo demás.
   565	     */
   566	    suspend fun payCash(
   567	        venueId: String,
   568	        orderId: String,
   569	        isProvisional: Boolean,
   570	        amount: BigDecimal,
   571	        tip: BigDecimal = BigDecimal.ZERO,
   572	        /**
   573	         * D86: lo que la terminal creía que debía la cuenta ANTES de este cobro (pesos). Sólo para una cuenta del SERVIDOR
   574	         * (como ANDROID: la provisional no lo manda). El SERVIDOR lo usa sólo como rastro (`COBRO_SIN_RED_CON_DIFERENCIA`
   575	         * si su saldo es otro); nunca cambia ni rechaza el cobro, y uno viejo lo ignora.
   576	         */
   577	        saldoEsperado: BigDecimal? = null,
   578	    ): Result<PayCashOutcome> {
   579	        val intentId = UUID.randomUUID().toString()
   580	        val payload = JsonObject().apply {
   581	            if (isProvisional) addProperty("localOrderId", orderId) else addProperty("orderId", orderId)
   582	            addProperty("amountCents", amount.toCents())
   583	            addProperty("tipCents", tip.toCents())
   584	            addProperty("method", "CASH")
   585	            if (!isProvisional && saldoEsperado != null && saldoEsperado >= BigDecimal.ZERO) {
   586	                addProperty("expectedBalanceBeforeCents", saldoEsperado.toCents())
   587	            }
   588	        }
   589	        // D19: si ni siquiera se pudo escribir (la caja cerrada, el permiso, el disco), nada se cobró: se dice con su texto en
   590	        // vez de dejar salir la excepción (que cerraba la pantalla). Una cancelación sigue saliendo: la fila pudo quedar escrita y
   591	        // el próximo replay la manda sola.
   592	        try {
   593	            syncOutbox.enqueue(venueId, SyncIntentTypes.PAY_CASH, payload, id = intentId)
   594	        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
   595	            throw e
   596	        } catch (e: Exception) {
   597	            Timber.e(e, "❌ [Tables] No se pudo guardar el cobro en efectivo (venue=%s, order=%s)", venueId, orderId)
   598	            return Result.failure(PayCashNoGuardadoException(e.message?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: COBRO_NO_GUARDADO))
   599	        }
   600	
   601	        // D19 (`na78d478`): el desenlace de ESTE cobro lo decide la cola bajo su candado: el acuse de la pasada propia o, si otro
   602	        // reproductor (el plano, la mesa, el trabajador) ya lo mandó, lo que dejó en su fila. Antes, sin acuse propio, salía
   603	        // «guardado» aunque el SERVIDOR lo hubiera rechazado (PAX, 18-nov: «Cobro guardado · Cobrado $203», 0 pagos).
   604	        return when (val desenlace = syncOutbox.reproducirYResolver(venueId, intentId)) {
   605	            is SyncOutbox.DesenlaceDelIntent.Aceptado -> {
   606	                val result = desenlace.result
   607	                Result.success(
   608	                    PayCashOutcome(
   609	                        paymentId = result?.stringOrNull("paymentId"),
   610	                        orderNumber = result?.stringOrNull("orderNumber"),
   611	                        queued = false,
   612	                        // 🧾 `applyPayCash` (sync.mobile.service.ts) YA regresa
   613	                        // `digitalReceipt: { accessKey, receiptUrl, autofacturaAvailable }`
   614	                        // en el ack — el mismo shape que usan avoqado-android/ios. Antes de
   615	                        // este cambio el cliente TPV lo descartaba por completo (solo leía
   616	                        // paymentId/orderNumber), así que un cobro en EFECTIVO de Mesas
   617	                        // nunca tenía forma de mostrar/compartir su recibo digital ni el
   618	                        // acceso a autofactura — el hueco que este cambio cierra.
   619	                        receiptUrl = result?.jsonObjectOrNull("digitalReceipt")?.stringOrNull("receiptUrl"),
   620	                        autofacturaAvailable = result?.jsonObjectOrNull("digitalReceipt")?.booleanOrNull("autofacturaAvailable") ?: false,
   621	                    ),
   622	                )
   623	            }
   624	            // El acuse repetido del SERVIDOR (`ackFromExisting`) no trae `message`: se dice con el código (Codex r1 #7).
   625	            is SyncOutbox.DesenlaceDelIntent.Rechazado -> Result.failure(
   626	                PayCashRejectedException(
   627	                    desenlace.message?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: rechazoSinMotivo(desenlace.errorCode),
   628	                    desenlace.errorCode,
   629	                ),
   630	            )
   631	            // Sin red, o un intent anterior cortó en RETRY: sigue PENDING, se verá como "guardado" — NUNCA como error (regla de oro
   632	            // offline). Un RETRY propio tampoco es la última palabra.
   633	            SyncOutbox.DesenlaceDelIntent.EnEspera -> Result.success(PayCashOutcome(paymentId = null, orderNumber = null, queued = true))
   634	            is SyncOutbox.DesenlaceDelIntent.NoSeSabe -> {
   635	                Timber.e("❌ [Tables] Cobro en efectivo %s sin desenlace conocido: %s", intentId, desenlace.motivo)
   636	                Result.failure(PayCashSinConfirmarException())
   637	            }
   638	        }
   639	    }

   641	    /**
   642	     * "Liberar mesa" — Plan C, Task 8. Mismo patrón idempotente-online-u-offline
   643	     * que [openTable]: `tableController.clearTable` es idempotente (liberar una
   644	     * mesa ya libre es un no-op), así que no necesita write-ahead — un
   645	     * DROP_RESPONSE que reintente offline sobre la MISMA mesa nunca duplica

   933	    /**
   934	     * "Cobro en efectivo" — ver KDoc de [TablesRepository.payCash] para el
   935	     * mecanismo write-ahead + replay inmediato. Funciona offline a propósito
   936	     * (regla dura de la Task 8: "cash *can* go offline; card cannot").
   937	     */
   938	    fun payCash(amount: BigDecimal, restanteDeLaPantalla: BigDecimal? = null) {
   939	        val session = tableSession.current()
   940	        if (session == null) {
   941	            _state.update { it.copy(errorMessage = "No hay mesa activa") }
   942	            return
   943	        }
   944	        val vId = venueId
   945	        if (vId == null) {
   946	            _state.update { it.copy(errorMessage = "No hay venue configurado en este terminal") }
   947	            return
   948	        }
   949	        if (amount <= BigDecimal.ZERO) {
   950	            _state.update { it.copy(errorMessage = "Ingresa un monto válido") }
   951	            return
   952	        }
   953	        // D86: sin cuenta leída (o sin saldo sin red seguro) no se cobra con un número que no es el de la cuenta.
   954	        _state.value.efectivoBloqueadoPor?.let { motivo ->
   955	            _state.update { it.copy(errorMessage = motivo) }
   956	            return
   957	        }
   958	        // D9: una sola reserva de cobro — con una tarjeta abriéndose, el efectivo espera.
   959	        if (_state.value.isProcessingCash || _state.value.isHandingOffToCard) return
   960	        // D9 (encargo 49276c): tras entregar una tarjeta, el saldo de aquí puede ser el de antes: no se cobra hasta releerlo.
   961	        if (_state.value.saldoPorConfirmar) return
   962	        // D49 (Codex r2 #2): el botón se dibujó con el saldo de SU pantalla; si una lectura ya lo cambió, no se cobra con el importe
   963	        // viejo, sea mayor o menor (un segundo toque antes de repintar cobraba $25 sobre una cuenta que ya debía $75).
   964	        if (botonViejo(restanteDeLaPantalla)) return
   965	
   966	        // 🧾 Capturado ANTES de mutar el estado: [onPaymentCompleted] (más abajo)
   967	        // recalcula `remaining`, así que "¿este cobro salda la cuenta?" solo se
   968	        // puede leer AQUÍ contra el restante todavía vigente — es la MISMA
   969	        // condición que decide si la mesa se libera sola, ver KDoc de
   970	        // [CashReceiptSummary.isSettlingPayment].
   971	        val remainingBeforeThisPayment = _state.value.remaining
   972	        val tipAtChargeTime = _state.value.tipAmount
   973	        val willSettle = amount >= remainingBeforeThisPayment
   974	        // D49 (Codex r1 #2): la sesión que se cobra; si el cajero sale mientras se relee la cuenta, no se cobra.
   975	        val epocaAlTocar = tableSession.epoch
   976	
   977	        _state.update { it.copy(isProcessingCash = true, errorMessage = null, avisoDelCobro = null) }
   978	        cobrosIniciados++
   979	        viewModelScope.launch {
   980	            // D86 (Codex r5): leer la cuenta, calcular el saldo y cobrar van EN FILA, nunca a la vez.
   981	            fila.withLock {
   982	            // D86 (Codex r2): el saldo se valida AL COBRAR, no sólo cuando se pintó: la cola pudo cambiar después (una apertura
   983	            // rechazada o confirmada, un cobro propio que no ha subido). Si ya no cuadra, no se cobra y se dice por qué.
   984	            val motivo = motivoParaNoCobrar(vId, session, remainingBeforeThisPayment)
   985	            if (motivo != null) {
   986	                _state.update { it.copy(isProcessingCash = false, errorMessage = motivo) }
   987	                alTerminarElCobro()
   988	                return@launch
   989	            }
   990	            // D49: otro aparato pudo cambiar la cuenta después de la última lectura. El aviso va en `avisoDelCobro` (Codex r1 #4): una
   991	            // relectura que viajaba borra `errorMessage` antes de que se vea.
   992	            val cambio = cuentaCambioAntesDeCobrar(vId, session, amount, remainingBeforeThisPayment, epocaAlTocar)
   993	            if (cambio != null) {
   994	                _state.update { it.copy(isProcessingCash = false, avisoDelCobro = cambio) }
   995	                // Codex r2 #3: si el cajero salió de la cuenta, lo pospuesto no corre sobre la sesión que quedó activa.
   996	                if (cambio == SALISTE_DE_LA_CUENTA) releerAlTerminarElCobro = false else alTerminarElCobro()
   997	                return@launch
   998	            }
   999	            repository.payCash(
  1000	                venueId = vId,
  1001	                orderId = session.orderId,
  1002	                isProvisional = session.isProvisional,
  1003	                amount = amount,
  1004	                tip = tipAtChargeTime,
  1005	                // D86: lo que esta pantalla creía que debía la cuenta antes del cobro (rastro en el SERVIDOR si no cuadra).
  1006	                saldoEsperado = if (session.isProvisional) null else remainingBeforeThisPayment,
  1007	            ).fold(
  1008	                onSuccess = { outcome ->
  1009	                    _state.update {
  1010	                        it.copy(
  1011	                            isProcessingCash = false,
  1012	                            // D9: la propina ya se cobró con ESTE pago; la siguiente parte de la cuenta no la repite.
  1013	                            tipAmount = BigDecimal.ZERO,
  1014	                            notice = if (outcome.queued) {
  1015	                                "Sin conexión — cobro guardado; se sincronizará solo"
  1016	                            } else {
  1017	                                "Cobro registrado"
  1018	                            },
  1019	                            // 🧾 Receipt: on EVERY cash payment (queued or not) — closes
  1020	                            // the asymmetry with TARJETA (which already ends on
  1021	                            // "Cobrar"'s success screen with a receipt). See KDoc of
  1022	                            // [CashReceiptSummary].
  1023	                            cashReceipt = CashReceiptSummary(
  1024	                                amount = amount,
  1025	                                tipAmount = tipAtChargeTime,
  1026	                                totalAmount = amount + tipAtChargeTime,
  1027	                                orderNumber = outcome.orderNumber ?: session.orderNumber,
  1028	                                receiptUrl = outcome.receiptUrl,
  1029	                                autofacturaAvailable = outcome.autofacturaAvailable,
  1030	                                queued = outcome.queued,
  1031	                                isSettlingPayment = willSettle,
  1032	                            ),
  1033	                        )
  1034	                    }
  1035	                    onPaymentCompleted(amount)
  1036	                    // D86 (Codex r0 #2): reproducir la cola para este cobro pudo confirmar la apertura sin avisar a la sesión. Va
  1037	                    // DESPUÉS de descontar el cobro: así la liberación (si saldó) ya tomó su cuenta local y el restante de un
  1038	                    // cobro parcial lo vuelve a leer el SERVIDOR.
  1039	                    if (session.isProvisional) {
  1040	                        viewModelScope.launch {
  1041	                            try {
  1042	                                promoverSiYaSeConfirmo(vId, session)
  1043	                            } catch (e: CancellationException) {
  1044	                                throw e
  1045	                            } catch (e: Exception) {
  1046	                                Timber.w(e, "⚠️ [TableCheckout] No se pudo revisar la apertura tras el cobro")
  1047	                            }
  1048	                        }
  1049	                    }
  1050	                    alTerminarElCobro()
  1051	                },
  1052	                onFailure = { e ->
  1053	                    if (e is CancellationException) throw e
  1054	                    val rechazado = e is TablesRepository.PayCashRejectedException
  1055	                    val duda = e is TablesRepository.PayCashSinConfirmarException
  1056	                    val aviso = (e as? TablesRepository.PayCashRejectedException)?.let { rechazo ->
  1057	                        // D6: la cola ya reintenta sola con un número nuevo cuando es seguro; si aun así llega
  1058	                        // aquí, el texto del servidor («La secuencia del dispositivo…») no le dice nada a la cajera.
  1059	                        if (rechazo.errorCode == "STALE_DEVICE_SEQUENCE") RECHAZO_POR_NUMERO else rechazo.message
  1060	                    } ?: e.message?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: "No se pudo registrar el cobro"
  1061	                    // D19: tras un rechazo o una duda, el saldo de esta pantalla ya no es de fiar (otro aparato pudo cambiar la
  1062	                    // cuenta, o el cobro pudo entrar). En una cuenta del SERVIDOR, Efectivo y Tarjeta esperan una lectura que salga
  1063	                    // DESPUÉS (la espera de D92; las que ya viajaban se tiran). En una abierta sin red aquí, el saldo se recalcula
  1064	                    // de la cola. Si ni siquiera se pudo guardar, nada cambió: sólo el aviso.
  1065	                    val releer = rechazado || duda
  1066	                    val esperaLaCuenta = releer && !session.isProvisional
  1067	                    if (esperaLaCuenta) {
  1068	                        lecturasIniciadas++
  1069	                        relecturasSeguidas = 0
  1070	                    }
  1071	                    _state.update {
  1072	                        it.copy(
  1073	                            isProcessingCash = false,
  1074	                            avisoDelCobro = aviso,
  1075	                            // D19 (Codex r1 #5): en la duda el cobro pudo entrar con su propina; no se ofrece otra vez.
  1076	                            tipAmount = if (duda) BigDecimal.ZERO else it.tipAmount,
  1077	                            pendienteSinSubir = if (esperaLaCuenta) it.pendienteSinSubir ?: CUENTA_POR_CONFIRMAR else it.pendienteSinSubir,
  1078	                            saldoSinRed = if (releer && it.isProvisional) null else it.saldoSinRed,
  1079	                        )
  1080	                    }
  1081	                    alTerminarElCobro()
  1082	                    if (releer) loadCheck()
  1083	                },
  1084	            )
  1085	        }
  1086	        }
  1087	    }

   114	                Timber.i("      $jsonBody")
   115	                Timber.i("   Contains 'blumonOperationNumber'? ${jsonBody.contains("blumonOperationNumber")}")
   116	                Timber.i("═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════")
   117	            } catch (e: Exception) {
   118	                Timber.e(e, "Failed to serialize request for debug logging")
   119	            }
   120	
   121	            val resolvedShift = if (context.shiftMembership == "MANAGED")
   122	                (shiftReplayGate ?: throw com.jaac.avoqado_tpv.features.shift.data.repository.ShiftMappingPendingException()).reference(context, referenceNumber)
   123	            else context.shiftId
   124	            val wireRequest = if (context.shiftMembership == "MANAGED") request.copy(shiftIdDeLaVenta = resolvedShift, isOfflineReplay = true) else request
   125	
   126	            // 3. Llamar al backend
   127	            val response = apiService.recordFastPayment(
   128	                venueId = context.venueId,
   129	                request = wireRequest
   130	            )
   131	
   132	            if (context.shiftMembership == "MANAGED") shiftReplayGate!!.reference(context, referenceNumber)
   133	
   134	            // 4. Procesar response
   135	            when {
   136	                response.isSuccessful && response.body() != null -> {
   137	                    val body = response.body()!!
   179	                    )
   180	                }
   181	
   182	                response.code() == 403 -> {
   183	                    Timber.w("⚠️ Forbidden (403) - Missing payments:create permission")
   184	                    Result.failure(
   185	                        BackendHttpException(
   186	                            statusCode = response.code(),
   187	                            message = "No tienes permisos para registrar pagos. " +
   188	                                    "Contacta al administrador del venue."
   189	                        )
   190	                    )
   191	                }
   192	
   193	                response.code() == 404 -> {
   194	                    Timber.w("⚠️ Not Found (404) - Venue ${context.venueId} not found")
   195	                    Result.failure(
   196	                        BackendHttpException(
   197	                            statusCode = response.code(),
   198	                            message = "Venue no encontrado. Verifica tu configuración."
   199	                        )
   200	                    )
   201	                }
   202	
   203	                response.code() == 429 -> {
   204	                    Timber.w("⚠️ Rate Limit (429) - Too many requests")
   205	                    Result.failure(
   206	                        BackendHttpException(
   207	                            statusCode = response.code(),
   208	                            message = "Demasiadas solicitudes. Por favor, espera un momento e intenta nuevamente."
   209	                        )
   210	                    )
   211	                }
   212	
   213	                response.code() in 500..599 -> {
   214	                    Timber.e("❌ Server Error (${response.code()}) - ${response.message()}")
   215	                    Result.failure(
   216	                        BackendHttpException(
   217	                            statusCode = response.code(),
   218	                            message = "Error del servidor (${response.code()}). " +
   219	                                    "Por favor, intenta nuevamente en unos minutos."
   220	                        )
   221	                    )
   222	                }
   223	
   224	                else -> {
   225	                    val errorMessage = parseBackendErrorMessage(
   226	                        rawBody = response.errorBody()?.string(),
   227	                        fallback = response.message()
   228	                    )
   229	                    Timber.e("❌ Unknown error (${response.code()}) - $errorMessage")
   230	                    Result.failure(
   231	                        BackendHttpException(
   232	                            statusCode = response.code(),
   233	                            message = "Error desconocido (${response.code()}): $errorMessage"
   234	                        )
   235	                    )
   236	                }
   237	            }
   238	        } catch (e: IOException) {
   239	            // NO envolver: classifySyncFailure() (SyncOutcome.kt) necesita la IOException
   240	            // intacta para clasificarla como Retryable. Ver BackendHttpException.kt.
   241	            Timber.w(e, "⚠️ Network error recording fast payment (will be retried)")
   242	            Result.failure(e)
   243	        } catch (e: Exception) {
   244	            Timber.e(e, "❌ Failed to record fast payment")
   245	            Result.failure(
   246	                Exception(
   247	                    "Error registrando el pago: ${e.message ?: "Error desconocido"}. " +
   248	                            "Verifica tu conexión a internet."
   249	                )
   250	            )
   251	        }
   252	    }
   253

   255	     * Construye el FastPaymentRequest DTO a partir de domain models.
   198	                    // caída», y el worker reproducía el segundo como VENTA RÁPIDA sobre una orden
   199	                    // viva: otra terminal la volvía a cobrar. El `code` del servidor viaja tal cual

   234	                }

   245	                }

   249	                        rawBody = response.errorBody()?.string(),

   729	        venueId: String,
   730	        session: TableSession.Active,
   731	        amount: BigDecimal,
   732	        restanteMostrado: BigDecimal,
   733	        epocaAlTocar: Long,
   734	    ): String? {
   735	        if (session.isProvisional) return null
   736	        if (amount.enCentavos() > restanteMostrado.enCentavos()) return cuentaCambio(restanteMostrado)
   737	        val s = _state.value
   738	        if (!s.isOnline || !s.letreroConInternet) return null
   739	        val leida = withTimeoutOrNull(RELECTURA_ANTES_DE_COBRAR_MS) { repository.getOrder(venueId, session.orderId) }
   740	        if (tableSession.epoch != epocaAlTocar || tableSession.current()?.orderId != session.orderId) return SALISTE_DE_LA_CUENTA
   741	        val detalle = leida?.getOrNull() ?: return null
   742	        val anulada = detalle.anulada()
   743	        val cobrada = !anulada && detalle.cobrada()
   744	        // Sin `amountLeft` el total no dice cuánto queda tras un abono (Codex r1): se cobra como siempre.
   745	        if (!anulada && !cobrada && detalle.amountLeft == null) return null
   746	        val saldo = saldoDeLaCuenta(detalle)
   747	        if (!anulada && !cobrada && saldo.enCentavos() == restanteMostrado.enCentavos() && amount.enCentavos() <= saldo.enCentavos()) {
   748	            return null
   749	        }
   750	        // Es la lectura más nueva: una que viajaba antes ya no manda (D86).
   751	        lecturasIniciadas++
   752	        tableSession.updateVersion(detalle.version)
   753	        tableSession.updateTotal(saldo)
   754	        _state.update { it.copy(check = detalle, remaining = saldo) }
   755	        return when {
   756	            anulada -> CUENTA_ANULADA_EN_OTRO_APARATO
   757	            cobrada -> CUENTA_COBRADA_EN_OTRO_APARATO
   758	            // Codex r4 #2: el SERVIDOR aparta lo que otro cobro todavía en curso está cobrando (un QR de cripto, D4): «falta $0.00»
   759	            // confundía, y si ese cobro se cancela la pantalla no se entera sola.
   760	            saldo.signum() == 0 -> OTRO_COBRO_EN_CURSO
   761	            else -> cuentaCambio(saldo)
   762	        }
   763	    }

  1634	    private fun observeSocketPaymentResult() {
  1635	        viewModelScope.launch {
  1636	            _state.collect { state ->
  1637	                val requestId = _socketRequestId ?: return@collect
  1638	                if (_paymentSource != "SOCKET") return@collect
  1639	
  1640	                if (state !is PaymentState.Error) cancelarCierreDelCobroRemoto()
  1641	                when (state) {
  1642	                    is PaymentState.Success -> {
  1643	                        val receipt = state.receipt
  1644	                        if (receipt?.paymentId.isNullOrBlank()) {
  1645	                            Timber.i("📡 [Socket-Payment] Success reached, waiting for backend receipt/paymentId before emitting result")
  1646	                            return@collect
  1647	                        }
  1648	
  1649	                        Timber.i("📡 [Socket-Payment] Emitting SUCCESS result for requestId=$requestId")
  1650	                        socketManager.emitTerminalPaymentResult(
  1651	                            requestId = requestId,
  1652	                            status = "success",
  1653	                            paymentId = receipt.paymentId,
  1654	                            transactionId = receipt.paymentId,
  1655	                            cardDetails = state.cardDetails?.let { card ->
  1656	                                mapOf(
  1657	                                    "lastFour" to card.maskedPan.takeLast(4),
  1658	                                    "brand" to card.cardBrand.name
  1659	                                )
  1660	                            },
  1661	                            errorMessage = null,
  1662	                            receiptUrl = receipt.receiptUrl,
  1663	                            receiptAccessKey = receipt.accessKey
  1664	                        )
  1665	                        // Clear to prevent duplicate emissions
  1666	                        _socketRequestId = null
  1667	                    }
  1668	                    is PaymentState.Error -> {
  1669	                        if (authorizationUnresolved) { cancelarCierreDelCobroRemoto(); return@collect }
  1670	                        if (state.canRetry) {
  1671	                            // 🛑 H.3: mientras la pantalla ofrezca «Reintentar» esta MISMA solicitud, su
  1672	                            // desenlace NO sale (emitirlo la daría por «no se cobró» mientras la terminal
  1673	                            // todavía puede cobrarla). Pero tampoco se queda colgada: si nadie retoma, el
  1674	                            // reloj de abandono la cierra con UN solo desenlace.
  1675	                            Timber.i("📡 [Socket-Payment] Retryable error reached, keeping Android request pending for requestId=$requestId")
  1676	                            programarCierreDelCobroRemoto(requestId)
  1677	                            return@collect
  1678	                        }
  4597	            else -> Unit // ALREADY_RESOLVED: el desenlace durable ya se reprodujo
  4598	        }
  4599	    }

  4605	    private suspend fun puedeArrancarEfectivoOCripto(): Boolean {

  4636	    @VisibleForTesting
  4637	    internal suspend fun performOnlineAuthorization(
  4638	        amount: String,
  4639	        track2: String,
  4640	        cardHolderName: String,
  4641	        emvTagList: String,
  4642	        isContactless: Boolean = false
  4643	    ): AuthorizationResult {
  4644	        // 🔒 OPEN the money window: from here on an authorization can reach the host, so a
  4645	        // POS socket-cancel must be refused (it would destroy the coroutine that receives the
  4646	        // bank response → charge approved with nobody recording it). Closed by the init-block
  4647	        // collector on the next RESOLVED state (which is only reached AFTER recording), and
  4648	        // by onCleared() as a backstop.
  4649	        authorizationReturned = false
  4650	        paymentStateHolder.setCharging(true)
  4651	        // 📒 [Libreta] Barrier: AUTORIZANDO is committed BEFORE the SDK may fire (spec §4.4).
   434	     * Used ONLY by the monitoring loop. Sends heartbeat (full OkHttp timeout),
   435	     * updates UI state, AND processes pending commands from the response.
   436	     *
   437	     * @param version Anti-stale guard — UI side effects skipped if superseded.
   438	     *                Commands ALWAYS execute regardless of staleness.
   439	     */
   440	    private suspend fun performFullHeartbeat(version: Int) {
   441	        if (!deviceInfoManager.isDeviceActivated()) {
   442	            if (isLatest(version)) _state.value = ConnectionState.Connected
   443	            return
   444	        }
   445	
   446	        val networkInfo = networkMonitor.getCurrentNetworkInfo()
   447	        if (!networkInfo.isConnected) {
   448	            if (isLatest(version)) {
   449	                scheduleOfflineTransition("heartbeat")
   450	            }
   451	            return
   452	        }
   453	
   454	        try {
   455	            Timber.d("🌐 [Connection] Heartbeat: checking backend connectivity...")
   456	
   457	            if (isLatest(version)) {
   458	                if (_state.value is ConnectionState.DisconnectedNoInternet || _state.value is ConnectionState.DisconnectedServerDown) {
   459	                    _state.value = ConnectionState.Reconnecting
   460	                }
   461	            }
   462	
   463	            val heartbeat = buildLightweightHeartbeat()
   464	            val heartbeatStartMs = System.currentTimeMillis()
   465	            val result = heartbeatRepository.sendHeartbeat(heartbeat)
   466	            val latencyMs = System.currentTimeMillis() - heartbeatStartMs
   467	
   468	            when (result) {
   469	                is Result.Success -> {
   470	                    Timber.i("✅ [Connection] Heartbeat OK (latency=${latencyMs}ms)")
   471	
   472	                    // UI state — gated by version
   473	                    if (isLatest(version)) {
   474	                        cancelOfflineTransition()
   475	                        markServerProbeSucceeded()
   476	                        connectionStateManager.updateState(hasInternet = true, hasServer = true, latencyMs = latencyMs)
   477	                        handleReconnectionSuccess(version)
   478	                    }
   479	
   480	                    // Commands — ALWAYS processed, never skipped by stale guard
   481	                    val pendingCommands = result.data.pendingCommands
   482	                    if (!pendingCommands.isNullOrEmpty()) {
   483	                        Timber.i("📥 [Connection] Received ${pendingCommands.size} pending command(s)")
   484	                        processPendingCommands(pendingCommands)
   485	                    }
   486	                }
   487	                is Result.Error -> {
   488	                    Timber.w("⚠️ [Connection] Heartbeat: backend unreachable: ${result.exception?.message}")
   489	                    if (isLatest(version)) {
   490	                        markServerProbeFailed(source = "heartbeat", cause = result.exception)
   491	                    }
   492	                }
   493	            }
   494	        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
   495	            throw e
   496	        } catch (e: Exception) {
   497	            Timber.e(e, "❌ [Connection] Heartbeat failed")
   498	            if (isLatest(version)) {
   499	                markServerProbeFailed(source = "heartbeat_exception", cause = e)
   500	            }
   501	        }
   502	    }

   506	     *
   629	    private suspend fun handleReconnectionSuccess(version: Int) {
   630	        if (!isLatest(version)) return
   631	
   632	        if (reconnectionAttempts > 0) {
   633	            viewModelScope.launch { commandExecutor.recoverPending() }
   634	            // Refresh paid access after recovery without delaying the connection banner or checkout.
   635	            viewModelScope.launch {
   636	                tpvSettingsRepository.refreshFromTerminalConfig(deviceInfoManager.getSerialNumber())
   637	            }
   638	            Timber.i("🔄 [Connection] Connection restored after $reconnectionAttempts attempts")
   639	
   640	            connectionEventManager.emitConnectionRestored(
   641	                attemptsBeforeReconnection = reconnectionAttempts
   642	            )
   643	
   644	            // Re-check after suspension point (emitConnectionRestored may suspend)
   645	            if (!isLatest(version)) return
   646	
   647	            _state.value = ConnectionState.Reconnected
   648	
   649	            // Cancel previous banner timer to avoid duplicate transitions
   650	            reconnectedBannerJob?.cancel()
   651	            reconnectedBannerJob = viewModelScope.launch {
   652	                delay(2000)
   653	                if (_state.value is ConnectionState.Reconnected) {
   654	                    _state.value = ConnectionState.Connected
   655	                }
   656	            }

  5933	    ) {
  5934	        if (_paymentSource != "SOCKET") return
  5935	        val requestId = _socketRequestId ?: return
  5936	        if (_socketResultEmitted) {
  5937	            Timber.d("📡 [AngelPay Socket] Result already emitted for $requestId — skipping (status=$status)")
  5938	            return
  5939	        }
  5940	        // 🛑 El servidor sólo acredita `PROCESSOR_DECLINED` junto a `failed`: con `cancelled` lo degrada a
  5941	        // timeout, la fila queda UNKNOWN y la terminal y la tablet se quedan bloqueadas (P1, 11-sep).
  5942	        //
  5943	        // ⚠️ REDUNDANTE a propósito, y conviene saberlo antes de "limpiarla": los emisores reales de un
  5944	        // desenlace retenido ([emitirDeclinacionRetenida], el final pre-autorización) ya mandan `failed`,
  5945	        // así que hoy NINGÚN camino llega aquí con `cancelled` + `PROCESSOR_DECLINED`. Es la segunda capa
  5946	        // para el emisor que se escriba mañana. Medido el 11-sep rompiendo cada capa por separado: con una
  5947	        // sola rota las 98 pruebas siguen en VERDE (la otra tapa el hueco); rotas LAS DOS caen 6, entre
  5948	        // ellas «P1 el reloj de abandono de A nunca emite con el id de B». O sea: el invariante está
  5949	        // protegido, pero ninguna prueba distingue las capas — no la borres creyendo que alguna la cubre.
  5950	        val statusFinal = if (outcomeEvidence == "PROCESSOR_DECLINED" && status == "cancelled") "failed" else status
  5951	        socketManager.emitTerminalPaymentResult(
  5952	            requestId = requestId,
  5953	            status = statusFinal,
  5954	            paymentId = paymentId,
  5955	            transactionId = transactionId,
  5956	            cardDetails = null,
  5957	            errorMessage = errorMessage,
  5958	            receiptUrl = receiptUrl,
  5959	            receiptAccessKey = receiptAccessKey,
  5960	            outcomeEvidence = outcomeEvidence,
  5961	        )
  5962	        _socketResultEmitted = true
  5963	        Timber.i("📡 [AngelPay Socket] Emitted terminal:payment_result | status=$statusFinal | requestId=$requestId")
  5964	    }
   796	     * Validates the typed code against the backend. Requires a selected
   797	     * customer; without one, the UI shows a hint and never calls this.
   798	     *
   799	     * When the validation succeeds, the discount is applied to the cart
   800	     * immediately so the totals update in real time before the operator
   801	     * confirms the charge.
   802	     */
   803	    fun validateReferralCode() {
   804	        if (necesitaInternet()) return
   805	        val venueId = secureStorage.getVenueId() ?: run {
   806	            Timber.w("🎁 validateReferralCode skipped: no venueId")
   807	            return
   808	        }
   809	        val customer = _selectedCustomer.value ?: run {
   810	            Timber.w("🎁 validateReferralCode skipped: no customer selected")
   811	            return
   812	        }
   813	        val code = _referralCode.value.trim()
   814	        if (code.isEmpty()) return
   815	        if (candado.rechaza("validar el referido")) return
   816	
   817	        viewModelScope.launch {
   818	            _referralValidation.value = ReferralCaptureUiState.Validating
   819	            val validacion = validateReferralUseCase(
   820	                venueId = venueId,
   821	                referralCode = code,
   822	                newCustomerId = customer.id,
   823	            )
   824	            if (candado.rechaza("aplicar el referido") || necesitaInternet()) {
   825	                // La respuesta llegó con la venta ya preparándose: su descuento no viajó. Se descarta y el cajero vuelve a validar.
   826	                _referralValidation.value = ReferralCaptureUiState.Idle
   827	                return@launch
   828	            }
   829	            validacion.fold(
   830	                onSuccess = { result ->
   831	                    when (result) {
   832	                        is ReferralValidationResult.Valid -> {
   833	                            _referralValidation.value = ReferralCaptureUiState.Valid(
   834	                                referrerName = result.referrerName,
   835	                                discountPercent = result.discountPercent,
   836	                            )
   837	                            applyReferralDiscount(result.discountPercent, result.referrerName)
   838	                        }
   839	                        is ReferralValidationResult.Invalid -> {
   840	                            _referralValidation.value = ReferralCaptureUiState.Invalid(result.reason)
   841	                            clearReferralDiscountOnly()
   842	                        }
   843	                    }
   844	                },
   845	                onFailure = { e ->
   846	                    Timber.e(e, "🎁 Referral validate request failed")
   847	                    _referralValidation.value = ReferralCaptureUiState.Invalid(
   848	                        ReferralValidationResult.Reason.UNKNOWN,
   849	                    )
   850	                    clearReferralDiscountOnly()
   851	                },
   852	            )
   853	        }
   854	    }

   879	    /**
   880	     * Applies the referral percent discount as a manual order-level discount
   881	     * tagged with [REFERRAL_DISCOUNT_SOURCE]. Computed against the current
   882	     * gross subtotal (matching what `ManualDiscountSubView` does for PERCENT
   883	     * mode).
   884	     */
   885	    private fun applyReferralDiscount(discountPercent: Int, referrerName: String) {
   886	        val pct = discountPercent.coerceIn(0, 100)
   887	        if (pct == 0) return
   888	        val subtotal = _cartState.value.subtotalCents
   889	        if (subtotal <= 0) return
   890	        val amount = (subtotal * pct / 100).coerceAtMost(subtotal)
   891	        applyManualOrderDiscountInternal(
   892	            amountCents = amount,
   893	            reason = "Referido por $referrerName ($pct%)",
   894	            source = REFERRAL_DISCOUNT_SOURCE,
   895	        )
